These findings expose fundamental limitations of current LLM-based mobile assistants and motivate future research in this direction. Data and code are available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Junjie-Ye/SPIEval.

</details>


### [35] [EVIL-Detect for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6: LLM-Generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10698)
*Hongrui Bao,Hangyu Rong,Zhuoshang Wang,Yubing Ren,Yanan Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EVIL-Detect，一种用于检测LLM生成文本的多信号集成框架，采用冲突感知融合策略，在NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6中取得宏F1分数0.8888并排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型快速发展，可靠检测LLM生成文本的需求日益增加，尤其在现实中文场景中需要同时区分人工撰写文本（HWT）、LLM生成文本（LGT）和LLM润色文本（HLT），且面临强分布偏移的挑战。

Method: EVIL-Detect采用多信号集成框架，融合了编辑程度回归、零样本似然对比信号、词汇统计和保守文本规则。系统通过校准的决策边界和冲突感知集成策略来提高在强分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在官方评测中取得宏F1分数0.8888，排名第一。

Conclusion: 冲突感知的多信号融合方法能够有效提升LLM文本检测在现实中文场景中的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has increased the need for reliable detection of LLM-generated text, especially in realistic Chinese scenarios involving human-written text (HWT), LLM-generated text (LGT), and LLM-refined text (HLT). This paper presents EVIL-Detect, a multi-signal ensemble framework with conflict-aware fusion for NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 6. The system integrates edit-extent regression, zero-shot likelihood-contrast signals, lexical statistics, and conservative text rules. With calibrated decision boundaries and conflict-aware integration, our system improves robustness under strong out-of-distribution shifts, achieving a macro-F1 score of 0.8888 and ranking first in the official evaluation. Our code is available at https://github.com/bbbbhrrrr/evildetect.

</details>


### [36] [Mitigating Context Interference for Reliable and Efficient Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10743)
*Boyang Xue,Bin Wu,Shuofei Qiao,Sheng Wang,Rui Wang,Yiming Du,Hongru Wang,Jeff Z. Pan,Emine Yilmaz,Kam-Fai Wong,Aldo Lipani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多轮搜索智能体中的上下文干扰问题，发现干扰主要来自最新检索到的文档，提出基于蒸馏的上下文精炼器来动态缓解干扰，并验证了将上下文精炼融入强化学习训练可显著提升智能体的可靠性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮搜索智能体在每次检索中不可避免地引入不相关信息，导致上下文干扰，阻碍其可靠性和效率。现有研究缺乏对该问题的系统性分析，不清楚干扰来源于上下文的哪些部分，以及如何有效缓解。

Method: 1) 系统研究上下文干扰的三个维度：干扰来源定位、上下文精炼方法、精炼与训练结合；2) 发现干扰主要来自最新检索文档；3) 提出基于蒸馏的上下文精炼器动态缓解干扰；4) 将上下文精炼融入RL训练管线。

Result: 验证了干扰主要源于最新检索文档；蒸馏式上下文精炼器能有效缓解干扰；将上下文精炼融入RL训练可显著提升搜索智能体的可靠性与效率。

Conclusion: 研究强调了缓解上下文干扰对搜索智能体的重要性，提出"先精炼上下文再生成"的新型AI智能体范式。

Abstract: Recent research empowers Large Language Models (LLMs) as multi-turn search agents to iteratively retrieve and generate outputs until complex tasks are solved. However, the contexts of multi-turn search agents are lengthy and complex. For example, the retrieved set of documents in each turn would inevitably introduce irrelevant information that distracts LLMs, referring to \textit{context interference}, potentially hindering the reliability and efficiency of search agents. Therefore, we conduct a systematic study on context interference in multi-turn search agents, focusing on investigating i) which parts of the context of search agents will contribute to the context interference, ii) how to refine the contexts of search agents to mitigate the interference, and iii) can incorporating context refinement into search agent training yield further improvements. We reveal that interference primarily arises from the latest retrieved documents. Based on the explored findings, we then introduce a distill-based context refiner to dynamically mitigate context interference for multi-turn search agents. Finally, we validate that incorporating context refinement into RL training pipelines of search agents can significantly enhance both reliability and efficiency. This study highlights the importance of mitigating context interference of search agents, inspiring a novel paradigm of ``refine context and then generate'' for AI agents.

</details>


### [37] [Assessing Reliability of BERT-Based Models on Question Answering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10806)
*Pooja Yadav,Priyanka Harjule,Basant Agarwal,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种基于BERT的问答模型（BERT、RoBERTa、ALBERT、DistilBERT）的可靠性，通过蒙特卡洛Dropout（MCD）和输入改写扰动两种方式测试响应稳定性，并在SQuAD和QuAC数据集上进行验证。结果显示RoBERTa可靠性最高，而ALBERT和DistilBERT表现不稳定，统计检验确认MCD不会破坏推理动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在实际应用中的可靠性与其准确性同等重要。虽然编码器式预训练Transformer（如BERT及其变体）在问答分类任务上表现出色，但其可靠性（尤其是响应稳定性）仍未被充分探索，因此需要系统性评估。

Method: 对四种BERT系列模型（BERT、RoBERTa、ALBERT、DistilBERT）进行可靠性评估，采用两种测试条件：(1)通过蒙特卡洛Dropout（MCD）引入内部模型变异；(2)通过改写句子引入输入扰动。在SQuAD和QuAC数据集上测试，分析Dropout率对预测一致性的影响以及词汇变化对答案稳定性的影响。

Result: RoBERTa表现出更高的可靠性；ALBERT和DistilBERT则显示出显著的不一致性。统计分析证实启用MCD进行预测不会干扰推理动态，验证了其作为可靠性指标的有效性。

Conclusion: 在问答模型中，评估准确性之外还应同时评估稳定性，以确保模型在真实世界应用中的可靠性。本研究强调了可靠性评估的重要性，并验证了MCD作为可靠性评价手段的有效性。

Abstract: Reliability estimation of large language models is in many cases as crucial as their accuracy, as reliable models are more trustworthy, robust, and suitable for practical applications. Recent advancements in natural language processing (NLP), particularly those based on transformer architectures, have significantly accelerated progress across various NLP tasks. This study focuses on the reliability of transformer-based question answering (QA) models, specifically BERT models and its variants (RoBERTa, ALBERT, DistilBERT). These encoder-only pretrained transformers have demonstrated remarkable accuracy in QA tasks that can be treated as classification tasks. However, their reliability remains underexplored. This study evaluates the reliability of four BERT-based models by assessing response stability under two conditions: (1) internal model variations induced via Monte Carlo Dropout (MCD) and (2) input perturbations through paraphrasing. Using the SQuAD and QuAC datasets, we investigate how dropout rates affect prediction consistency and whether lexical changes impact answer stability. Our findings reveal that RoBERTa maintains higher reliability, whereas AlBERT and DistilBERT exhibit significant inconsistencies. Statistical analyses confirm that enabling MCD during prediction does not disrupt inference dynamics, validating its effectiveness as a reliability metric. These findings underscore the importance of evaluating both accuracy and stability in QA models to ensure stability in real-world applications.

</details>


### [38] [Surfacing the Unsaid: CUE-Bench for Affective Stance in Chinese Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10810)
*Zhenyan Zheng,Yunyao Zhang,Junxi Sheng,Junqing Yu,Zikai Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CUE Bench，一个中文"未言明情感"基准，聚焦情感立场（Affective Stance），通过九个可解释的情感立场类别（由显式/隐式极性交互构建），并附带意图与细粒度情感标注，以解决现有情感基准无法捕捉隐式、反讽、礼貌等深层情感推理的缺陷。实验表明引入情感立场可将细粒度情感识别提升3.5个百分点、语用意图检测提升7.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感基准主要标注表面极性或最终情感类别，缺乏对显式表达、隐式情感、语用意图与细粒度情感交互的结构化刻画，导致对隐含、弱化、反转或被语用重塑的情感表达不敏感，掩盖了模型在深层情感理解上的失败。

Method: 构建CUE Bench基准：以情感立场为核心，从显式-隐式极性交互中衍生出九个人类可解释的情感立场类别，并在此基础上提供意图与细粒度情感标注，从而支持结构化的情感推理。

Result: 实验表明，融入情感立场后，细粒度情感识别提升3.5个百分点，语用意图检测提升7.8个百分点，优于强基线模型。

Conclusion: CUE Bench通过引入情感立场作为中间结构化表示，有效增强了对隐式与语用化情感表达的理解能力，为深层情感推理评估提供了更有判别力的基准。

Abstract: Emotion understanding in discourse requires reasoning beyond surface sentiment because speakers often convey affect through indirect, implicit, polite, ironic, or deliberately mismatched expressions. Existing emotion benchmarks mainly annotate surface polarity or final emotion categories, while lacking a structured account of how explicit expression, implicit affect, pragmatic intent, and fine grained emotion interact. This limitation makes current evaluations insensitive to cases where affective meaning is concealed, weakened, inverted, or pragmatically reshaped, thereby obscuring model failures in deeper emotion understanding. To address this gap, we introduce CUE Bench, a Chinese Unsaid Emotion benchmark that centers on Affective Stance and covers diverse communicative scenarios. CUE Bench constructs nine human interpretable affective stances from explicit implicit polarity interaction and further provides intent and fine grained emotion annotations for structured affective inference. Experiments show that incorporating Affective Stance improves fine grained emotion recognition by 3.5 percentage points and pragmatic intent detection by 7.8 percentage points over strong baselines.

</details>


### [39] [Reference-Free Post-Training of Open Large Language Models for Multilingual Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10812)
*Chris Han,Pengzhi Gao,Pei Fu,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多语言机器翻译的无参考后训练方法。基于监督微调模型MiLMMT-46-v0.1，采用GRPO强化学习，以两个无参考质量评估模型的平均得分作为奖励并结合语言识别门控，再对SFT和RL检查点进行线性插值得到MiLMMT-46-v1.0。在46种语言上持续提升翻译质量，超越Seed-X、HY-MT2、TranslateGemma等开源基线，并领先Google Translate、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT-5等专有系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放大语言模型在多语言机器翻译上的后训练方法多依赖参考翻译进行优化，缺乏参考可用时难以有效提升；同时强化学习与蒸馏等后训练策略的潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 以监督微调模型MiLMMT-46-v0.1为起点；2) 采用GRPO进行强化学习，奖励函数为两个无参考质量评估模型的平均值，并通过语言识别进行门控；3) 对SFT和RL检查点进行线性插值以获得最终模型MiLMMT-46-v1.0；4) 额外探究on-policy蒸馏方法并对比其效果。

Result: 在46种语言上，MiLMMT-46-v1.0相比SFT模型持续提升翻译质量，超越Seed-X、HY-MT2、TranslateGemma等强开源基线，并在无参考评估中取得领先Google Translate、Gemini 3 Pro、GPT-5等专有系统的成绩。此外，on-policy蒸馏能达到但未超越RL配合检查点插值的质量前沿。

Conclusion: 无参考的RL后训练结合检查点插值能有效提升多语言翻译质量，且蒸馏虽可行但无法超越该方法的性能上限。作者开源模型与代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: We study reference-free post-training for multilingual machine translation with open large language models. Starting from the supervised-finetuned MiLMMT-46-v0.1 models, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a reward that averages two reference-free quality estimation models and is gated by language identification. We then linearly interpolate the supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) model checkpoints to obtain MiLMMT-46-v1.0. Across 46 languages, the resulting models consistently improve translation quality over their SFT counterparts, outperform strong recent open baselines, including Seed-X, HY-MT2, and TranslateGemma, and achieve leading reference-free scores against evaluated proprietary systems such as Google Translate, Gemini 3 Pro, and GPT-5. We further investigate on-policy distillation and find that it reaches, but does not surpass, the quality frontier achieved by RL with checkpoint interpolation. We release the models and code to facilitate future research.

</details>


### [40] [VibeLifeBench: Can Your Life Agent Be Proactive and Persistent in a Living World?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10875)
*Xiaohongshu Inc*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VibeLifeBench，一个包含200个长时程任务的基准测试，覆盖10个日常生活领域，用于评估LLM智能体在模拟的多周时间线中处理真实生活辅助任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估大多使用短时、自包含的请求和静态环境，而真实的日常辅助任务持续数周、环境不断变化、约束隐式存在，现有基准无法衡量智能体在长时程任务中的主动性和一致性表现。

Method: 构建包含200个长时程任务和10个日常领域的基准测试。每个任务是在包含22个模拟服务的世界中脚本化的多周时间线，世界按自身时钟推进且许多变化是静默的，只有重新检查世界的智能体才能发现。任务通过细粒度加权检查评分，覆盖最终状态、行动及时性和隐式约束的遵守情况。

Result: 对7个前沿模型进行评估，所有模型得分都很低，表明当前智能体距离真实生活辅助能力还有很大差距。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体难以胜任长时程、动态变化的日常生活辅助任务，VibeLifeBench为未来研究提供了衡量进步的标准，且将开源全部任务、环境和评估框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed as personal assistants. Existing evaluations, however, mostly use short, self-contained requests in static environments. Everyday life assistance is different. A task runs for weeks rather than minutes. The world keeps changing while the agent is not being prompted. Many constraints are never stated outright. An agent that merely answers the request in front of it will fail at such a task. What is needed instead is an agent that stays proactive and consistent. It decides on its own when to act, when to ask, and when to stay silent. It notices changes that nobody announced. It keeps one plan coherent from the first day to the last. No current benchmark measures this. We introduce VibeLifeBench, a benchmark of 200 long-horizon tasks across ten everyday-life domains. Each task is a scripted multi-week timeline in a simulated world of 22 mock services. The world advances on its own clock, and many of its changes are silent, so only an agent that re-inspects the world discovers them. Every task is graded by fine-grained, weighted checks that read only what the agent actually left behind, covering the end state, the timeliness of its actions, and whether it upheld the implicit constraints. We evaluate seven frontier models. All of them score low, which shows how far current agents are from assisting with real life. We will open-source all tasks, environments, and the evaluation framework.

</details>


### [41] [X2-Turn: Frame-Synchronous Dual-Head Modeling for Joint Streaming ASR and Turn State Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10878)
*Kaiqi Fu,Rime Wen,Altman Lin,Shawn Qin,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: X2-Turn是一种基于延迟流建模的帧同步轮次状态预测方法，在预训练Voxtral Realtime模型基础上添加帧同步轮次状态头，与ASR头并行在共享流式表示上联合预测ASR token和细粒度轮次状态，在中英双语Easy-Turn测试集上验证了准确性和低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化方法通常在话语级或固定块级优化轮次状态预测，与连续轮次状态估计不匹配，且依赖辅助ASR模型，导致响应性受限和系统复杂度增加。

Method: 在预训练的Voxtral Realtime模型基础上，引入帧同步轮次状态头，与ASR头并行运行于共享流式表示上，通过延迟流建模联合预测ASR token和帧级细粒度轮次状态。

Result: 在中英双语Easy-Turn测试集上取得有效结果，实现了准确的轮次转换检测同时保持低延迟。

Conclusion: X2-Turn通过帧同步联合建模解决了传统模块化方法的粒度不匹配问题，无需依赖辅助ASR模型即可实现准确且低延迟的轮次转换检测。

Abstract: Accurate and responsive turn-taking is essential for spoken dialogue systems, which must distinguish in real time between user interruptions, backchannels that should be ignored, and the completion of an utterance. Prior modular approaches typically optimize turn state prediction at the utterance or fixed-chunk level, creating a mismatch with the continuous turn state estimate, and often depend on an auxiliary ASR model, which limits responsiveness and increases overall system complexity. Therefore, we present X2-Turn, a frame-synchronous turn state prediction method via delayed-stream modeling. Specifically, building on the pretrained Voxtral Realtime model, we introduce a frame-synchronous turn state head that operates in parallel with the ASR head on shared streaming representations, jointly predicting ASR tokens and fine-grained turn states at the frame level. We evaluate our method on the bilingual Chinese-English Easy-Turn test sets, and the results demonstrate its effectiveness in achieving accurate turn-taking detection while maintaining low latency.

</details>


### [42] [Certify or Refuse: A Cross-Model Map for Selective Risk Control with Coverage Floors under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10893)
*Jiamiao Liu,Dewen Qiao,Yu Zhang,Xuetao Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"经认证的选择性预测器"（certified selective predictors）在受限比率协变量偏移（bounded-ratio covariate shift）下的认证问题。核心发现是"地板认证映射"（Floor Certification Map）：当认证目标同时包含自动化下限（至少覆盖目标流量的β比例）和选择条件风险α时，认证面临可行性边界与双资源复杂度图谱，资源为有标签源样本（控制风险）与无标签目标样本（控制地板），两者加性可分离（至常数因子）。文章给出三个模型标签化结果（下界Model-B、匹配的oracle权重上界Model-A、可实现的精确分层偏移上界Model-B'），并证明在整个有界比率类上不存在未知权重过程的匹配（Model-B不一致）。复杂度由局部化接受区域泛函驱动而非全局有效样本量（ESS）。实证上注册咬合族对数-对数斜率-2.002、1024单元审计0违规、SQuAD到NewsQA的可行性审计给出诚实拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性预测器在部署中面临分布偏移时，传统认证仅约束选择条件风险，但实际自动化系统还需要满足最低吞吐率（地板）要求。在受限比率协变量偏移下，同时认证"至少回答β比例且错误率不超过α"产生本质性的可行性边界——任何单个模型都无法在整个类上达到最优，需要刻画这一双资源复杂性图谱。

Method: 方法包含理论证明与实证验证。理论上：(1) 证明地板认证映射定理，给出认证在β-地板与α-风险之间的可行性前沿；(2) 建立两资源复杂度图谱，有标签源样本控制风险、无标签目标样本控制地板，加性至常数；(3) 三个模型标签结果：Model-B下界（证明整个类上任何procedure不一致）、Model-A匹配oracle权重上界、Model-B'在预注册精确分层偏移模型下的可实现上界（显式定价nuisance成本）；需满足正则前沿边界（local-regime margin）、低于局部阈值余量、格条件（lattice conditions）等前提。实证上对注册咬合族（registered bite family）进行对数-对数回归检验、1024单元审计违反计数、SQuAD-to-NewsQA单语料可行性审计。

Result: 核心结果为地板认证映射存在可行性前沿与双资源复杂度：风险复杂度由有标签源样本决定，地板复杂度由无标签目标样本决定，加性至常数。匹配关系跨模型成立（Model-B下界与Model-A/B'上界匹配），但无法在单一模型上实现最大最小最优——在α=β=1/2处给出Model-B不一致的具体见证。复杂度由局部化接受区域泛函驱动而非全局ESS；两个下界轴随β→0消失，表明"地板创造了映射"。实证：注册咬合族在对数-对数坐标下斜率-2.002（注册带内）、1024单元审计0违规、SQuAD到NewsQA审计返回诚实拒绝。

Conclusion: 在受限比率协变量偏移下，"地板"（自动化下限β）并非仅仅是附加条件，而是从根本上重塑认证结构：它引入了可行性前沿、创造了双资源复杂度图（有标签源对无标签目标），并使得任何未知权重的程序在整个类上无法匹配最优（不一致是本质而非技术缺陷）。复杂度局部性表明认证效率由接受区域几何而非全局样本量决定。固定ESS分离定理与nuisance必要性仍待后续解决，但这些开放问题不削弱核心映射的成立性。

Abstract: Certified selective predictors attain whatever coverage they attain; operators impose an automation floor: answer at least a $β$-fraction of shifted target traffic with at most an $α$-fraction of answers wrong. Under bounded-ratio covariate shift we prove the Floor Certification Map: once that floor must be certified alongside the selection-conditioned risk $α$, certification acquires a feasibility frontier and a two-resource complexity map, additive up to constants: risk in labeled source, the floor in unlabeled target samples. The rates are local, needing a regular frontier margin, slack below the local-regime threshold, and lattice conditions: pre-registered with a lattice margin for the upper bounds, compatible per-slack for the lower. The displayed split is the operational route; oracle weights also allow a labeled-source floor estimate. Three model-tagged results: a lower bound (Model-B), a matching oracle-weight upper bound (Model-A), and an implementable upper bound (Model-B') valid under a pre-registered exact stratified-shift model with nuisance cost priced explicitly. The match is across these models rather than a single-model minimax theorem, and necessarily so: over the full bounded-ratio class no unknown-weight procedure matches at any sample size (Model-B is inconsistent, witnessed at $α=β=1/2$). The nuisance's necessity is only partially settled. Complexity tracks a localized accepted-region functional, not global effective sample size (ESS), on both sides, though a fixed-ESS separation theorem is left open; both lower-bound axes vanish as $β\to0$, so the floor creates the map. Empirically, the registered bite family diverges with log-log slope $-2.002$ within its pre-registered band; a 1,024-cell audit records 0 violations where the formal certificates fire; and a single-corpus SQuAD-to-NewsQA feasibility audit returns honest refusal.

</details>


### [43] [FaithformBench: Benchmarking Faithfulness of Mathematical Chain-of-Thought Autoformalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10916)
*Rob Cornish,Iacopo Ghinassi,Po-Hung Yeh,Shuqi Liu,Qiyuan Xu,Haoxuan Yin,Dominik Wagner,Wenda Li,Yee Whye Teh,Luke Ong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估自动形式化（AF）系统忠实度的新基准方法，通过对推理步骤进行自动扰动生成无效输入，同时衡量未扰动步骤的有效性保持和扰动步骤的无效性保持，发现当前AF系统普遍存在"沉默纠正"（谄媚）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AF忠实度评估方法依赖昂贵的专家标注、LLM评判或嵌入模型（准确性保证有限），且通常只考虑已知正确的输入，无法评估AF是否忠实翻译错误输入。

Result: 在8个AF系统和4个数学数据集上观察到普遍存在的谄媚现象：许多AF将无效输入"沉默纠正"为可证明语句。且最保持有效性的微调AF同时也是最谄媚的，表明当前AF系统在有效性与无效性保持之间存在张力。

Conclusion: 当前AF系统存在系统性谄媚倾向，有效性与无效性保持之间存在内在矛盾，需要开发能更好保持无效输入不变形的AF系统。

Abstract: Autoformalisation (AF) systems map natural language reasoning steps into formal statements in a proof assistant such as Lean. We consider how to assess the faithfulness of these systems. Existing approaches require expensive human-annotated ground truth, or rely on LLM judges or embedding models, which come with limited guarantees of accuracy. In addition, these methods typically only consider inputs that are known to be correct, and therefore do not assess whether the AF translates incorrect inputs faithfully. To address these limitations, we propose a new benchmark for AF faithfulness that is cheap to apply, sound under weak assumptions, and assesses both positive and negative examples. Our method is based on automatically generating perturbed reasoning steps that are designed to be invalid, and then measuring validity preservation on unperturbed steps and invalidity preservation on perturbed steps. We apply our method to eight AF systems across four mathematical datasets, and observe pervasive sycophancy: many AFs "silently correct" invalid inputs into provable statements. The most validity-preserving fine-tuned AFs are also the most sycophantic, suggesting a tension between validity and invalidity preservation in current AF systems.

</details>

*Wajdi Ben Saad,Safa Madiouni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种固定列表路由策略：对强语言直接使用多语言路径进行零样本分类，对弱语言先翻译成英语再分类。该方法完全自托管、使用预训练紧凑句子编码器、无需任务特定微调。在SIB-200和MASSIVE两个基准上验证，选择性翻译能可靠提升低资源语言的Macro-F1，但最优路由边界取决于任务特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言短文本分类系统（如内容审核、客服路由、意图识别）在聚合评估中往往掩盖了高资源和低资源语言之间的性能差距。统一的推理策略虽然部署简单，但假设所有语言都得到同等服务，这导致低资源语言性能被忽视。

Method: 提出固定列表路由策略：将语言分为不同层级，强语言直接在多语言路径上分类，弱语言先翻译成英语再进行零样本分类。使用预训练紧凑句子编码器，完全自托管，不需要任务特定的微调。在SIB-200（7类主题分类，15语言）和MASSIVE（60意图分类，15区域）两个基准上进行评估。

Result: 在SIB-200上，仅翻译低资源层的R1配置最优：高中层Macro-F1不变，低层从0.4632提升到0.6828。在MASSIVE上，同样的低层干预使低层从0.2143提升到0.4417，但全量翻译R3配置获得最佳总体结果（Macro-F1 0.4647）。选择性翻译对弱语言是可靠干预，但最优路由边界依赖具体任务。

Conclusion: 选择性翻译策略能有效提升低资源语言性能，且不需要微调。但最优路由策略因任务而异，应通过层级质量增益和层级延迟来报告路由效果，而非单一的全局效率分数。

Abstract: Multilingual short-text classification supports operational systems such as content moderation, customer support routing, and intent recognition, yet aggregate evaluation often hides large differences between high-resource and low-resource languages. Uniform inference policies are simple to deploy, but they assume that all languages are equally well served. In this work, we evaluate a fixed-list routing strategy that keeps stronger languages on a direct multilingual path and selectively sends weaker languages through translation into English before zero-shot classification. The pipeline is fully self-hosted, uses pretrained compact sentence encoders, and requires no task-specific fine-tuning.
  We test the approach on two benchmarks chosen to differ in scale and label granularity: a 15-language subset of SIB-200 for seven-way topic classification and a 15-locale subset of MASSIVE for intent classification over an official 60-intent inventory. On SIB-200, the best overall configuration is R1, which translates only the low-resource tier: high-tier and mid-tier Macro-F1 remain unchanged, while low-tier Macro-F1 rises from 0.4632 to 0.6828. On the MASSIVE subset, the same low-tier intervention raises low-tier Macro-F1 from 0.2143 to 0.4417, but the best overall result is obtained by full translation, R3, at Macro-F1 0.4647. Across these two benchmarks, selective translation is a reliable intervention for weaker languages, whereas the optimal routing boundary depends on the task. We therefore report routing through tier-level quality gains and tier-level latency rather than a single global efficiency score.

</details>

*Thanh-Dan Bui,Thanh-Trung Do,Tuan-Phong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: REAP系统是面向AKBC 2026共享任务的闭卷知识库构建系统，基于Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501模型，在32B参数预算内且不进行微调的条件下，通过结构化思维链推理、关系特定查询策略和基于推理的空集门控机制，在测试集上取得0.62的宏观F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在闭卷设置下利用语言模型的参数化知识构建知识库，面临预算约束（32B参数以内）和禁止微调的限制，需要设计高效的知识elicitation策略来从模型中提取结构化关系事实。

Method: 系统由三个核心组件构成：(1) 结构化思维链（chain-of-thought）推理；(2) 关系特定的查询策略（relation-specific query strategies）；(3) 基于推理的空集门控机制（reasoning-based empty-set gate），随后直接提取为合法的JSON数组格式。

Result: 在测试集上实现宏观F1分数0.62，关键关系类型表现突出：countryLandBordersCountry（F1=0.95）、hasArea（F1=0.77）、companyTradesAtStockExchange（F1=0.73）。

Conclusion: 该工作在严格的资源约束（32B参数、无微调）下证明了通过精心设计的提示策略和推理机制可以有效激发大型语言模型的参数化知识，为闭卷知识库构建提供了一种可行的方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: We present the REAP system for the AKBC Shared Task 2026 on constructing knowledge bases from language models in a closed-book setting, subject to a budget of at most 32B parameters and no model fine-tuning. Our system combines structured chain-of-thought reasoning, relation-specific query strategies, and a reasoning-based empty-set gate to elicit parametric knowledge, followed by direct extraction into valid JSON arrays. On the test set, the system, built on the Mistral-Small-24B-Instruct-2501 model, achieves a macro-F1 score of 0.62, with particularly strong results on countryLandBordersCountry (F1 = 0.95), companyTradesAtStockExchange (F1 = 0.73), and hasArea (F1 = 0.77). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yammdd/AKBC-Shared-Task-2026.

</details>


### [46] [ReLTEx: Reliable LLM-based Taxonomy Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10970)
*Zeinab Ghamlouch,Mehwish Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReLTEx框架，用于可靠的基于LLM的taxonomy扩展。该框架结合LLM驱动的候选生成、结构感知验证和递归扩展控制，以减少幻觉并提高生成taxonomy的一致性和质量。实验表明ReLTEx比直接使用LLM扩展产生更可靠且语义连贯的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在taxonomy丰富方面表现出强大能力，但直接依赖LLM生成的扩展往往带来噪声、冗余或层级不一致的结构，限制了自动taxonomy扩展的可靠性。

Method: ReLTEx框架包含三个关键组件：LLM驱动的候选生成、结构感知验证（structure-aware validation）以及递归扩展控制（recursive expansion control），共同减少幻觉并提升生成taxonomy的质量。

Result: 在masked taxonomy扩展设置下，使用基准taxonomy进行评估，并对比多种验证策略。实验结果显示ReLTEx在适应评估指标和人工评估中均产生更可靠、语义更连贯的taxonomy扩展。

Conclusion: ReLTEx框架通过结合结构感知验证和递归控制，有效解决了LLM直接扩展taxonomy时存在的噪声、冗余和层级不一致问题，为可靠的自动化taxonomy扩展提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in generating semantically relevant concepts and relations, making them promising tools for taxonomy enrichment. However, directly relying on LLM-generated expansions often leads to noisy, redundant, or hierarchically inconsistent structures, limiting their reliability for automated taxonomy expansion. In this paper, we present ReLTEx, a framework for reliable LLM-based taxonomy expansion. ReLTEx combines LLM-driven candidate generation with structure-aware validation and recursive expansion control to improve the consistency and quality of generated taxonomies by reducing hallucinations. We evaluate the proposed framework using benchmark taxonomies under a masked taxonomy expansion setting and compare multiple validation strategies. Experimental results, supported by both adapted evaluation metrics and human evaluation, demonstrate that ReLTEx produces more reliable and semantically coherent taxonomy expansions.

</details>

*Tsofia Cohen,Tom Hope*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MUSE，一个全文本、多领域的科学论文问题-解决方案-原理（P-S-R）三元组知识库资源。基于579个专家标注的段落，通过模块化抽取流水线构建了37K个由原文支撑的P-S-R三元组，并实验发现原理监督能提升复杂多约束问题的求解效果，但可能损害简单问题的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学论文中蕴含着细粒度的问题解决记录，包括技术障碍、解决方法及其选择理由，但目前缺乏系统化的资源来挖掘和利用这些信息。

Method: 引入MUSE资源，包含专家标注的579个段落（涵盖问题、解决方案、原理跨度及链接关系、概念共指），并设计模块化抽取流水线将标注扩展为37K个P-S-R三元组知识库，同时进行原理监督LLM的初步实验。

Result: 构建了高质量的知识库并评估了抽取组件；初步实验显示原理监督能提升复杂多约束问题的求解性能，但对简单问题可能产生负面影响。

Conclusion: MUSE为科学问题和解决方案的自动化挖掘提供了有价值的资源，原理监督在科学问题求解中具有潜力但需注意其适用场景的差异性。

Abstract: Scientific papers contain fine-grained records of problem solving: authors mention technical obstacles and methods that were used to address them, often along with reasoning on why those methods were chosen. We introduce MUSE (Mining Underlying Scientific Explanations), a full-text, multi-domain resource of scientific Problem-Solution-Rationale (P-S-R) triplets. We curate 579 expert-annotated full-text paragraphs, with a rich annotation schema covering salient problem, solution, and rationale spans, solves and rationale_of links and conceptual coreference. A modular extraction pipeline scales this annotation to build a high-quality knowledge base of 37K source-grounded P-S-R triplets. We evaluate the extraction components and include a preliminary experiment training a rationale-supervised LLM for scientific problem solving. Interestingly, we find that rationale supervision improves performance on complex, multi-constraint problems but can harm performance on simpler ones.

</details>


### [48] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种通过将语言模型的输出回馈给自身来探测模型的方法（如自洽性、迭代精炼、智能体循环），通过精心构造的"令牌环"（token ring）格劳伯动力学来比较两个仅一个令牌不同的环在共同随机数下的损伤传播。作者发现该探针同时测量两类不同的事物：一类由构造决定（运动学性质，模型不变），另一类真正反映模型特性。两类读数看似相似但本质上不同，作者曾将其混淆并报告了一个属于探针而非模型的相变。文章提供了区分两者的测试方法，并通过复现Domany-Kinzel损伤场进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大量方法通过让模型消费自身输出进行探测（self-consistency、iterated refinement、agentic loops），但这类探针究竟测量什么并不清楚。作者希望在尖锐构造下厘清这个问题，区分由探针构造本身决定的伪影与真正反映语言模型性质的信号，避免将探针效应误认为模型行为。

Method: 采用令牌环上的格劳伯动力学（Glauber dynamics）作为基底，创新点在于耦合方式：在共同随机数（common random numbers）下推进两个仅一个令牌不同的环，使未损伤副本的分歧严格为零，从而使得损伤传播（damage spreading）可测（而最大耦合给出的是混合时间）。通过固定构造改变模型、或固定模型改变构造来区分两类读数，并用Domany-Kinzel损伤场的位精确复现来验证仪器。

Result: 损伤光锥是运动学的（构造决定）；令牌空间Lyapunov指数λ_ca(r)的半径标度在19个模型和70倍尺度阶梯上跨模型不变。但λ_ca在训练中可复现地穿越零点，吸引子份额也能一致地对模型排序。作者曾把构造效应误认为模型效应，报告了一个精度达三位小数的相变，实际上属于探针而非任何语言模型。测试方法成功区分了两者；复现实验位精确地恢复了Domany-Kinzel损伤场；该方法还发现了四个被撤回的错误结论（估计器失败）。

Conclusion: 自我探测类方法同时测量两种不同的事物，其读数看似相同但性质完全不同：一类由探针构造固定（运动学伪影），另一类真正追踪模型。必须在改变模型或改变构造两个方向上做控制实验才能区分。作者指出这类混淆是系统性的陷阱（他们自己就误判了四个月），方法论以软件包形式发布，并建议在宣称发现模型相变或临界行为前先排除探针本身的效应。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


### [49] [ConRub-Med: Reinforcement Learning with Consensus Rubrics for Open-Ended Medical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10996)
*Taojie Zhu,Yuan Xia,Tao Sun,Yizhi Wang,Yan Chen,Qunshan He,Tian Guan,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu,Junwei Liu,Yonghong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ConRub-Med，一种利用模型生成的、经医生验证语义共识的评分标准（rubric）来训练医学大语言模型的方法。通过三个异构语言模型独立生成原子标准，再由一个审查模型保留三者语义支持的标准；采用三态评分（正确覆盖/缺失信息/错误声明），错误给予负分；在GRPO组内奖励相同时，用成对裁判提供序列优势。在9个基准中6个排名第一，在HealthBench-Hard上达到38.98分，超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式的医学问题缺乏廉价且可靠的自动结果验证器，因为回答可能部分正确、不完整或含有临床后果严重的错误。医生撰写的评分标准虽有强临床依据，但人工成本高昂；模型生成的评分标准则使监督可扩展，但如何保持评分标准从构建到策略优化的语义保真度是一个关键挑战。

Method: ConRub-Med包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模型评分标准生成——三个异构语言模型独立提出原子标准，一个审查模型仅保留三者语义支持的标准；(2) 三态评分——区分正确覆盖、缺失信息（得分为0）和错误声明（负分），而非传统的零信用处理；(3) 序列优势对——当完整GRPO组内所有响应获得相同最终奖励时，成对裁判仅在两个候选顺序一致时才提供序列优势，不改变标量奖励；无并列的组仍使用标准GRPO。

Result: 消融/盲审研究：两位医学专家按问题匹配进行盲审，评分标准面板（来自完整流水线）比单一生成器的面板更具临床相关性。基准测试：在9个开放模型基准中6个排名第一，获得最高的医学和泛化平均分。使用5,166条提示词构建的评分标准数据集，ConRub-Med在HealthBench-Hard上达到38.98±1.04（均值±标准差），优于InfiMed-ORBIT的33.60（8,000样本）和37.30（28,000样本）。

Conclusion: ConRub-Med通过多模型共识审核和三态评分机制，在保持医学评分标准临床质量的同时实现了可扩展的强化学习监督，显著优于现有的开放式医学问题强化学习方法，展示了模型生成评分标准在医学领域RLVR中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has been especially effective in mathematics and coding, where answers can be checked automatically. Many open-ended medical questions lack comparably cheap outcome verifiers: responses may be partly correct, incomplete, or contain clinically consequential errors. Rubrics written or validated by physicians offer strong clinical grounding, but involving experts in every instance is costly. Model-generated rubrics make this supervision scalable. We introduce ConRub-Med to preserve useful distinctions as rubric feedback moves from construction to policy optimization. For each prompt, three heterogeneous language models propose atomic criteria independently; a separate model reviews them, retaining only criteria with semantic support from all three generators. Three-State scoring distinguishes correct coverage, missing information, and incorrect claims. Errors receive negative rather than zero credit. When every response in a complete Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) group receives the same final reward, a pairwise judge provides sequence advantages only if both candidate orders agree, without changing the scalar rewards. Groups without ties use vanilla GRPO. In a blinded study matched by question, two medical experts rate panels from the full pipeline as more clinically relevant than panels produced by one generator. Across the evaluated open models, ConRub-Med ranks first on six of nine benchmarks and achieves the highest medical and generalization averages. Using the resulting rubric dataset of 5,166 prompts, it scores $38.98 \pm 1.04$ (mean $\pm$ SD) on HealthBench-Hard, compared with InfiMed-ORBIT's 33.60 with 8,000 samples and 37.30 with 28,000.

</details>


### [50] [On the Limitations of Cross-Lingual Consistency in Multilingual Text-to-image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11002)
*Sicheng Zhang,Zhonghao Yan,Binzhu Xie,Shi Qiu,Muzammal Naseer,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LingT2I基准，覆盖10种语言、33K提示词，用于评估文本到图像生成中的跨语言效果（包括内容生成和文本渲染），并揭示了语言不平等、语言相关的权衡以及语言与文化因素对模型输出的系统性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成研究主要聚焦于英文环境，跨语言性能差距和语言特定效应尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地评估跨语言行为。

Method: 构建LingT2I基准（10种语言、33K提示词），在内容生成和文本渲染两个维度评估跨语言效果，并进行定量分析（发现语言不平等和权衡）及定性分析（揭示语言特定生成模式）。

Result: 发现跨语言存在不平等现象和语言相关的评估维度权衡；揭示语言因素及其对应文化背景会系统性影响模型输出，呈现出多种语言依赖的生成模式。

Conclusion: LingT2I基准和分析为研究T2I生成中的跨语言行为提供了基础，有助于推动更鲁棒、更包容的模型开发。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) generation has achieved remarkable progress in recent years. However, existing research has largely focused on English-only settings, leaving cross-lingual performance gaps and language-specific effects insufficiently explored. To fill this gap, we introduce LingT2I, a benchmark covering 10 widely used languages with 33K prompts, designed to evaluate cross-lingual effects in both content generation and text rendering. Building on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive cross-lingual analysis, uncovering linguistic inequality and language-dependent trade-offs across evaluation dimensions. Beyond quantitative evaluation, we further reveal a range of language-dependent generation patterns, highlighting how linguistic factors and their corresponding cultural contexts systematically impact model outputs. Our benchmark and analysis provide a foundation for studying cross-lingual behavior in T2I generation and facilitate the development of more robust and inclusive models. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/LingT2I.

</details>


### [51] [Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025)
*Clemens Vetter,David Kaczér,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了语言模型涌现性错位（EM）现象的机制来源，通过SAE模型差分发现预训练中的人格特征（如越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗、操纵）在错位微调中被放大，而安全性和助手身份特征被抑制。通过对特征进行操控可以双向控制EM，但仅凭预训练文档的语义相关性不足以引发EM，文档的结构和模型生成的措辞才是关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EM现象（在窄任务上微调导致无关领域的有害行为）具有安全隐患，虽然已有机制性解释将其归因于人格特征，但这些人格特征从何而来、预训练中哪些文档激活它们、自然人类文本是否足以引发EM等问题尚未得到回答。理解EM的起源对于模型安全对齐至关重要。

Method: 使用基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的模型差分方法，在四个开源模型上识别错位微调中被放大和抑制的特征；通过特征操控实验验证这些特征的因果作用；将因果特征归因到一百万预训练网络文档语料库，并比较人类文档微调、助手风格重格式化和合成指令-响应对微调对EM的影响。

Result: 与越狱人格、讽刺、欺骗和操纵相关的特征被错位微调放大，而安全与助手身份特征被抑制。特征操控可双向控制EM：在对齐模型中诱导最高62%的错位率（超过错位微调本身的35%），并可将错位模型重新对齐至接近基线。语义相关的预训练文档（涉及反派角色、统治、有害能动性）微调并不能可靠引发EM，而合成指令-响应对则能引发EM且可跨模型族迁移。

Conclusion: 语义相关性本身不足以引发EM，响应结构或模型生成的措辞在诱导EM中起重要作用。这一发现挑战了仅基于语义内容的理解，表明EM的产生涉及更深层的格式与结构因素，对预训练数据治理和模型对齐策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diffing across four open-weight models, we find that features related to jailbreak personas, sarcasm, deception, and manipulation are amplified by misalignment fine-tuning, while safety-relevant and assistant-identity features are suppressed. Steering individual features controls EM in both directions: it induces misalignment rates of up to 62% in aligned models -- exceeding the 35% reached by misalignment fine-tuning itself -- and re-aligns misaligned models to near-baseline misalignment rates. Attributing the causal features to a corpus of one million pre-training web documents retrieves semantically relevant narratives about villainous characters, domination, and harmful agency. However, fine-tuning on these human-written documents does not reliably induce EM, even after reformatting into assistant-style responses, whereas synthetic instruction-response pairs derived from the same content do -- and transfer across model families. Semantic relevance alone is therefore not sufficient: response structure or model-generated phrasing plays an important role in inducing EM.

</details>


### [52] [myMediWhisper: Construction of Burmese Medical Speech Corpus and Whisper Fine-Tuning for Clinical Dialogue ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11036)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Ye Bhone Lin,Thura Aung,Htet Arkar,Myat Oo Swe,Thet Htet San,Min Thiha Tun,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个缅甸语医疗语音识别框架，基于28小时由母语者录制和验证的高质量语料库，使用Whisper模型通过全量微调（FFT）和参数高效微调（PEFT/LoRA）进行训练。实验表明数据增强虽然降低干净语音性能，但显著提升噪声和混响环境下的鲁棒性。最佳系统myMediWhisper-Medium（无增强的全量微调）达到23.44%的词错误率（WER），超越更大的通用领域微调模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper模型虽然经过大规模多语言预训练，但在缅甸语医疗语音上的表现仍然有限，需要专门针对该低资源语言和医疗领域进行优化。

Method: 构建28小时由母语者录制并验证的高质量缅甸语医疗语音语料库；使用Whisper模型进行全量微调（FFT）和基于LoRA的参数高效微调（PEFT）；在受控噪声和模拟室内声学条件下应用波形级和频谱级数据增强以评估鲁棒性。

Result: 数据增强在干净语音上降低性能，但在噪声和混响环境中显著提升鲁棒性（FFT和PEFT设置下均有效）。最佳系统myMediWhisper-Medium（无增强的全量微调）取得23.44%的WER，优于更大的通用领域微调模型。

Conclusion: 全量微调的中等规模Whisper模型在缅甸语医疗语音识别中可以达到优于更大模型的效果，同时数据增强需要在干净语音性能和噪声鲁棒性之间进行权衡。数据集已公开在Huggingface上。

Abstract: Although Whisper models benefit from large-scale multilingual pre-training, their performance on Burmese medical speech remains limited. This work presents a Burmese medical speech recognition framework built on a high-quality 28-hour corpus recorded and validated by native speakers. We fine-tune Whisper models using full fine-tuning (FFT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) with LoRA. To evaluate robustness, we apply waveform- and spectrogram-level data augmentation under controlled noise and simulated room acoustics. While augmentation reduces performance on clean speech, it significantly improves robustness in noisy and reverberant environments across FFT and PEFT settings. Our best-performing system, fully fine-tuned myMediWhisper-Medium without augmentation, achieves a state-of-the-art Word Error Rate (WER) of 23.44%, outperforming much larger general-domain fine-tuned models. Dataset and other resources can be found at the Huggingface repository: https://huggingface.co/datasets/LULab/mediTalk-mm-rdy.

</details>


### [53] [TEAMMix: Taxonomy Enrichment Augmentation and Minority-augmented Mixing Strategy for LLM-enhanced Weak-Supervised Hierarchical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11044)
*Jian Zhang,Zhuohao Yang,Songlin Lei,Bangli Liu,Ziwei Wang,Xufeng Weng,Gehan Amaratunga,Yu Lin,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM数据增强的弱监督分层文本分类（HTC）框架，通过关键词生成和语料挖掘丰富标签层次语义，引导LLM生成伪样本缓解长尾问题，并使用高斯混合模型进行基于置信度的重采样来优化生成数据质量，实验证明该方法能有效提升伪标签可靠性和细粒度不平衡数据集上的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTC任务面临标签层次复杂和类别不平衡等挑战，现有基于大语言模型的方法存在提示词过长、丢失标签结构信息等问题，难以高效应用于该任务。

Method: 提出了一个弱监督HTC框架，包含三个关键步骤：(1)通过关键词生成和语料挖掘对标签层次进行语义丰富；(2)引导LLM生成伪样本以缓解长尾问题；(3)采用高斯混合模型进行基于置信度的重采样，优化生成数据的质量。

Result: 实验表明该方法有效提升了LLM生成伪标签的可靠性，并在细粒度和不平衡数据集上显著改善了分类性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的LLM数据增强框架能够有效解决HTC任务中标签结构信息丢失和长尾分布问题，为弱监督场景下的分层文本分类提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Hierarchical Text Classification (HTC), as a critical text mining task, faces challenges such as complex label hierarchies and class imbalance. Existing methods based on large language models (LLMs) struggle to be efficiently applied to this task due to issues like lengthy prompts and loss of label structural information. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a weakly supervised HTC framework enhanced by LLM-based data augmentation. The framework first enriches the label hierarchy semantically through keyword generation and corpus mining, thereby enhancing the model's understanding of labels. Subsequently, it guides the LLM to generate pseudo-samples to mitigate the long-tail problem, and employs a Gaussian mixture model for confidence-based resampling to optimize the quality of generated data. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves the reliability of LLM-generated pseudo-labels and significantly enhances classification performance on fine-grained and imbalanced datasets.

</details>


### [54] [Multiclass Sentiment Analysis for Identifying Political Viewpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11049)
*Girma Yohannis Bade,Olga Kolesnikova,Jose Luis Oropeza,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了社交媒体上政治观点的多类别情感分析任务，设计并评估了基于XGBoost和BERT的两种机器学习方法，在带标签的政治社交媒体帖子上进行训练和测试。结果显示XGBoost的F1分数为0.2835，BERT的F1分数为0.2806，表明政治话语情感分类具有挑战性，并为未来研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上政治话语的快速增长为分析公众意见和识别不同政治视角提供了宝贵机会，但政治文本的情感分析具有复杂性和语境化特点，现有研究对多类别政治情感分类的探索不足。

Method: 设计并评估两种机器学习方法：基于XGBoost的方法和基于BERT的方法，在带标签的政治社交媒体帖子数据集上进行多类别情感分类任务的训练与评估，使用标准分类指标（F1分数）进行评价。

Result: XGBoost模型在测试集上达到0.2835的F1分数，BERT模型达到0.2806的F1分数。两个模型的性能都不高，说明多类别政治话语情感分类是一个具有挑战性的任务。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明政治话语情感分类的复杂性，两个模型的较低性能证明了该任务的难度，同时为未来的多类别政治情感分析研究提供了基线参考。

Abstract: The rapid growth of social media has created vast amounts of political discourse, which provides valuable opportunities to analyze public opinions and identify different political perspectives. Sentiment Analysis (SA) is a core task in Natural Language Processing (NLP) that allows the computational study of attitudes and opinions in textual data, and has become increasingly important for understanding political discourse. In this work, we investigate multiclass sentiment analysis of political view- points on social media, that is to automatically discriminate multiple sentiment classes over political issues and figures. To solve this task we design and evaluate two machine-learning approaches based on XGBoost and BERT. We train and evaluate the models on a labeled dataset of political social media posts using standard classification metrics. The experimental results show that the XGBoost model reaches an F1-score of 0.2835 and the BERT- based model reaches an F1-score of 0.2806 on the test set. These results demonstrate the challenge of classifying complex and contextualized political discourse sentiment and provide a baseline for future research in multiclass political sentiment analysis.

</details>


### [55] [Actions Speak Louder than Words: Measuring Cross-Lingual Policy Retention in Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110)
*Sourabrata Mukherjee,Kalika Bali,Sunayana Sitaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对工具使用智能体的动作策略进行多语言评估（8个模型、6个并行基准、41种语言、238万次 rollout），发现多语言环境下动作策略保持高度一致性（71-73%），但这种一致性在小于100亿参数的模型中崩溃。研究还发现智能体将非英语任务通过英语中转，且这一行为是因果性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言评估通常只比较最终答案而忽略执行过程中的动作，但动作才是智能体行为的产物：它们决定成本和延迟、影响系统失败方式，且是唯一可审计的行为部分。因此需要将动作策略本身作为测量对象。

Method: 对8个模型在6个并行基准、41种语言上进行2.38百万次rollout，测量动作策略的跨语言相似性。作者识别并消除了五个混淆因素（短轨迹得分偏高、空轨迹得满分、无关轨迹的偶然一致性、模型可复现性上限、同语言重复提问基线缺失），通过置换检验测量偶然性基线，并进行了预注册的因果预测实验。

Result: 每个修正都使效应更显著。分歧是结构性的而非采样噪声：贪心解码下依旧存在，温度升高时保持平稳。四种前沿模型在归一化自身可复现性后，跨语言保持71-73%的动作策略，模型身份仅解释5.7%的方差。小于约100亿参数时一致性崩溃，小模型间的排序主要是偶然性基线的伪影。智能体将非英语任务通过英语路由，这一行为是因果性的，且无法通过指令消除。

Conclusion: 多语言动作一致性是前沿模型的结构性属性，但取决于模型规模；语言路由（通过英语中转）是智能体行为中负载承重且难以改变的组成部分。同时警示测量方法中的识别问题：一个简单的轨迹提取正则表达式就能制造多语言失败的假象（能将模型测量准确率提高26倍，而可读输出准确率几乎不变）。

Abstract: When a tool-using agent is given the same task in a different language, does it still take the same steps? Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds sit between raw trace similarity and any defensible claim, each able to flip a conclusion. Short traces score higher, empty traces score perfectly, unrelated traces agree by chance over half the time, the gap is capped by each model's reproducibility, and a model asked the same question twice in one language answers differently, leaving no baseline. We remove all five, and every correction makes the effect larger. Divergence proves structural, not sampling noise: it survives greedy decoding in every cell and stays flat as temperature rises, even as models grow less self-consistent. Normalised by their own reproducibility, four very different frontier models converge under greedy decoding, each keeping 71-73% of its action policy across languages, with model identity explaining only 5.7% of the variance. Below roughly 10B parameters it breaks down, and the ordering among smaller models is largely an artifact of a chance floor we measure by permutation rather than assume. Agents route non-English tasks through English; this pivot is causally load-bearing, confirmed by a pre-registered prediction across four models, and models will not abandon it when told to. Finally, a single trace-extraction regex, not the model, manufactured a multilingual failure: two worked examples raise one model's measured accuracy twenty-sixfold while its accuracy on readable outputs barely moves.

</details>


### [56] [Attention-Path Fragility as an Uncertainty Signal in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11138)
*Minsoo Kim,Sungyoung Ji,Kisung Moon,Ilyong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ASMI（注意力子网络互信息），一种无需训练的估计器，通过遮蔽注意力头并测量子网络间的BALD互信息来衡量模型预测的"脆弱性"，从而补充传统置信度以外的错误预测信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的不确定性估计仅关注输出分布的广度（如置信度、熵），但忽略了自信预测在注意力路径受扰动时是否脆弱这一维度，后者包含额外的错误预测信号。

Method: ASMI通过遮蔽注意力头生成多个子网络，计算它们之间的BALD互信息，并使用语义一致性核函数来折扣表面形式差异。另提出Sem-ASMI，可从单次贪心响应中读取信号，无需随机生成。

Result: ASMI在扎根QA任务上超出单次置信度和熵的错误预测信息，将置信度过滤器的保留错误大致减半。不同变体在12个设置中的8个达到或超过最强基线，其中3个显著。在参数化QA上如预测般回退到零成本MSP基线水平。

Conclusion: 模型不确定性不仅体现在输出分布广度，还体现在预测是否对注意力扰动"脆弱"。这种脆弱性信号具有领域分级特性，可预测自身适用边界，且由"脆弱性是否耦合到错误"而非脆弱性本身决定其有效性。

Abstract: We propose that a model's uncertainty about a token is reflected not only in the breadth of its output distribution but also in whether a confident prediction is \emph{fragile} under perturbation of its attention pathways. We instantiate this as ASMI (Attention-Subnetwork Mutual Information), a training-free estimator that masks attention heads and measures the BALD mutual information among the resulting subnetworks, with a semantic-agreement kernel to discount surface-form disagreement. The signal is not a restatement of output confidence: on grounded QA an out-of-fold test shows it adds error-predictive information beyond single-pass confidence and entropy, concentrated in \emph{confident-but-fragile} predictions, where acting on it roughly halves the retained error of a confidence filter. The distinctness is regime-graded, so ASMI predicts its own domain of applicability, strong where answers are routed through provided context and bounded by design where they are recalled from parametric knowledge. Sem-ASMI reads the signal from a single greedy response, without the stochastic generations the strongest baselines require, and ties or beats Semantic Entropy on ten of the twelve grounded benchmark-backbone settings. Across the same twelve settings, the best ASMI variant, typically the adaptive one reusing the ten samples already drawn for the baselines, ties or leads the strongest baseline in eight, significantly in three under a paired test. On parametric QA all variants revert to or below the zero-cost MSP baseline, exactly as predicted, and the estimates are near-deterministic across reruns. A head-level analysis shows that what tracks this boundary is not the presence of head-level fragility but whether that fragility couples to errors.

</details>


### [57] [The Illusion of Cross-Lingual Safety in Low-Resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11146)
*Abigail Oppong,P Sam Sahil,Tadesse Destaw Belay,Maryam Ibrahim Mukhtar,Esmael Ahmed Abdu,Tassallah Abdullahi,Jessica Oparebea,Saminu Mohammad Aliyu,Idris Abdulmumin,Abubakar Juma Chilala,Nicholaus Dismas Ladislaus,Alfred Malengo Kondoro,Lemofouet Valdini Douglace,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型在多语言环境下的安全对齐问题，聚焦四种非洲语言（Twi、Hausa、Amharic和Swahili）。作者提出LoDNA安全数据集（包含直译和文化本地化提示）以及潜在几何框架来探测模型隐藏状态中的拒绝表示。实验表明跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示在大多数语言-模型组合中仅保留不到10%的英语拒绝信号，证明当前多语言安全对齐是表面的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐主要在英语中开发，假设其能泛化到多语言环境，但这一假设缺乏充分验证。低资源语言中的安全漏洞被忽视，作者希望通过实证研究揭示跨语言安全迁移的真实情况。

Method: 1) 构建LoDNA安全数据集，包含非洲四种低资源语言的直译和文化本地化提示；2) 提出潜在几何框架，探测LLM隐藏状态中的拒绝表示；3) 对比字面翻译与本地化提示的语义对齐和跨层漂移情况。

Result: 跨语言安全迁移严重受限，有害提示在大多数语言-模型对中保留不到10%的英语拒绝信号；字面翻译和本地化提示在语义上高度对齐（余弦相似度0.95-0.996）但在层间漂移，表明模型编码了概念却未将其路由至安全机制。

Conclusion: 当前多语言安全对齐是表面性的，研究结果强烈反驳了"存在通用、语言无关的伤害流形"这一假设（至少对所研究的低资源语言而言），需重新思考多语言安全对齐策略。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is largely developed in English, assuming these safeguards generalize across multilingual settings. However, this assumption remains underexplored and exposes a vulnerability in low-resource languages. Privacy and legal constraints make it difficult the release of large-scale real conversation datasets; existing work has mostly focused on sentence-level toxicity of online abuses, leaving a gap in modelling abuse as a relational and temporally unfolding phenomenon. In this work, we focus on modelling Violence Against Women and Girls (VAWG) scenarios as multi-turn dialogues. We introduce ConVAWG, a retrieval-grounded framework for generating CPS-aligned synthetic VAWG chat dialogues. ConVAWG builds scenarios from persona seeds, demographic patterns reported by the UK Office for National Statistics, official crime definitions, and retrieved Domestic Homicide Review cases; converts them into hierarchical event timelines; generates multi-scene role-play dialogues; and applies targeted activation-steered toxicity control to appropriate utterances. Extensive human evaluation, LLM-as-Judge assessment, ablations, and downstream tasks show strong dialogue quality and domain fidelity.

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于e-process的自适应GAN训练方法，将判别器和生成器之间的更新切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验，实现数据驱动的动态切换而非固定更新比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据科学中的许多假设检验问题难以用预设统计模型中的参数自然表述，例如优化算法的动态评估。GAN训练需要在判别器和生成器更新之间反复决策切换时机，但现有方法依赖固定更新比例或启发式准则，缺乏理论基础。

Method: 将切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验，开发基于e-process的自适应训练程序。判别器更新时，一个e-process检验"判别器诱导的分离度低于目标水平"的零假设；生成器更新时，第二个e-process检验"分离度高于刷新水平"的逆向零假设。在给定训练样本条件下，证明新的经验索引和潜在变量能产生条件e值并累积为e-process，在自适应模型更新和数据依赖切换下提供任意时刻有效的Type I错误控制。

Result: 在多种多模态合成分布和图像基准数据集上，该方法在多种广泛使用的GAN目标函数下匹配或优于最佳的固定比例基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于e-process的自适应切换方法为GAN训练提供了有理论保证的动态更新策略，在性能上不逊于甚至优于固定比例基线。

Abstract: Modern data science increasingly gives rise to hypothesis-testing problems that are not naturally formulated in terms of parameters within prespecified statistical models. One important example is the dynamic evaluation of optimization algorithms, where decisions must be made during training about whether further updates remain beneficial or the algorithm should switch to a different phase. This issue is particularly relevant in stochastic min-max optimization. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) provide a canonical example, as their training requires repeated decisions about when to switch between discriminator and generator updates, yet existing methods typically rely on fixed update ratios or heuristic criteria. We formulate this switching problem as sequential hypothesis testing and develop an e-process-based adaptive training procedure. During discriminator updates, one e-process tests the null that the discriminator-induced separation between the empirical data distribution and the generator law remains below a target level. During generator updates, with the discriminator fixed, a second e-process tests the reverse null that this separation remains above a refresh level. Conditional on the observed training sample, we prove that fresh empirical indices and latent draws yield conditional e-values that can be accumulated into e-processes, providing anytime-valid Type I error control under adaptive model updates and data-dependent switching. Across multimodal synthetic distributions and image benchmark datasets, the proposed method matches or outperforms the best fixed-ratio baselines under several widely used GAN objectives.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个关键发现：通过迭代擦除探针方向来测量神经表示中"概念维度"的方法，其结果并非内在的语义属性，而是依赖于整个测量程序（包括基选择、优化器、度量等）。通过信息保持的可逆重参数化（如剪切变换），可以改变累积擦除计数和排名，因此这些程序定义量不能作为概念维度的内在度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经科学和机器学习领域常用"擦除探针方向直到预测失效"的方法来估计某个概念在神经表示中占用的维度数。但作者质疑这种方法的内在有效性：如果真的存在一个客观的概念维度，它应该在保信息量的可逆变换下保持不变。该论文旨在区分模型定义的总体量（如生成维度、充分线性维度、最小守卫秩）与程序定义的量（如停止计数、累积编辑秩），并证明后者在重参数化下可变。

Method: 作者构建了一个总体高斯构造，其中保信息的可逆剪切变换保持了预测问题和三个总体量不变，但将累积欧几里得擦除计数从1改变为2。该分离对Moore-Penrose普通最小二乘和所有有限非负岭权重都成立。对于两输出全QR程序，累积编辑秩从2变为环境维度4。此外，通过ADam/QR校准和冻结的V-JEPA2特征上的受控重参数化实验，展示了实践中的优化过程如何改变后续欧几里得轨迹。

Result: 实验表明：(1)剪切变换可以改变累积擦除计数（从1到2）和累积编辑秩（从2到4）；(2)完整的累积度量QR轨迹在正定度量、探针、正则化器和平局打破规则一致地变换时是仿射等变的；(3)在已知秩的有限样本Adam/QR校准中，恒等混合在所有20次大样本运行中仅接受一次更新，而每个测试剪切参数至少接受两次更新；(4)冻结V-JEPA2特征的受控重参数化保持秩零预测，却改变后续欧几里得轨迹。

Conclusion: 迭代擦除方法返回的是一个过程相对的估计量，由表示几何和完整测量程序共同决定，而不是一个独立于程序的语义维度。协方差作为精确度量的选择只是一个推论，而非规范性的语义度量。视觉接触相关实验应视为压力测试，而非接触维度的估计。

Abstract: How many directions does a neural representation use to encode a concept? A common answer repeatedly erases probe directions and reports the stopping count or cumulative removed rank. We show that both quantities can change under an information-preserving invertible reparameterization, so neither is intrinsically a concept dimension. We distinguish model-defined population quantities (generating dimension, sufficient linear dimension, and minimum guarding rank) from procedure-defined quantities such as stopping count and cumulative edit rank. In a population Gaussian construction, an invertible shear preserves the prediction problem and all three quantities, yet changes the cumulative Euclidean erasure count from one to two. The separation holds for Moore--Penrose ordinary least squares and every finite nonnegative ridge weight. For a two-output full-QR procedure matching our motivating video analysis, cumulative edit rank similarly changes from two to the ambient dimension four. Conversely, the complete cumulative metric-QR trajectory is affine-equivariant when its positive-definite metric, probe, regularizer, and tie-breaking are transported consistently; exact covariance is one corollary, not a canonical semantic metric. In a known-rank finite-sample Adam/QR calibration, identity mixing stops after one accepted update in all 20 large-sample runs, whereas each tested shear $a\in\{.5,.75,1,1.25,2\}$ accepts at least two updates in all 20 runs. Controlled reparameterizations of frozen V-JEPA2 features preserve rank-zero predictions yet alter later Euclidean trajectories under practical optimization. These visual contact experiments are stress tests, not estimates of contact dimension. Iterative erasure therefore returns a procedure-relative estimand jointly determined by representation geometry and the full measurement procedure, not a semantic dimension by itself.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究一族连续的度归一化谱嵌入（包含邻接矩阵和对称拉普拉斯等常用特例），在随机点积图（RDPG）模型下证明行向中心极限定理；随后利用该极限分布，通过投影高斯贝叶斯误差诊断，在二社区随机块模型（SBM）中比较不同归一化方法的性能。

Result: 研究发现没有单一归一化方法始终最优。归一化方法的偏好取决于网络密度、社区不平衡度和块概率结构。通常，在低密度或更不平衡的网络设置中，更强的归一化更受青睐。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对度归一化如何影响谱聚类的统一分布理解，明确刻画了归一化同时影响群体几何结构和嵌入节点的局部不确定性，为实践中选择合适的归一化方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Spectral clustering methods for network data are commonly based on a few matrix representations, such as the adjacency matrix and the symmetric Laplacian. We study a continuum of degree-normalized spectral embeddings that includes these commonly used choices as special cases. Under a random dot product graph model, we establish a row-wise central limit theorem for this family of embeddings. The result provides an explicit description of how degree normalization affects both population geometry and the local uncertainty of embedded nodes. We use the limiting distributions to compare different normalizations in two-community stochastic block models through a projected-Gaussian Bayes-error diagnostic. These comparisons show that no single normalization is uniformly preferred. Instead, the favored normalization depends on network density, community imbalance, and block-probability structure. Typically, stronger normalization is favored in lower-density or more imbalanced settings. These results provide a unified distributional understanding of when and why alternative normalizations may improve spectral clustering.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究常量步长TD学习的统计推断问题。作者建立了固定步长线性TD的函数中心极限定理，并推导了由同一轨迹驱动的并行Richardson-Romberg(RR)递归的联合极限。通过Brownian-bridge自归一化器，该方法能在无需估计长程协方差或选择带宽/批长度的前提下，为状态值对比构建渐近枢轴置信区域，且只需单次遍历、内存不随轨迹长度增长。论文还讨论了随视界递减步长的设计方案，使推断能在根号n尺度下忽略RR目标偏移等残差项，从而对投影Bellman解进行推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略评估中，常量步长TD学习虽然吸引人，但基于单条Markov轨迹进行推断时必须考虑序列相关性和依赖于步长的平稳目标。传统的推断方法往往需要估计长程协方差或选择带宽参数，这些在实际应用中既困难又不稳定。因此需要一种无需这些繁琐步骤的稳健推断方法。

Method: 论文首先为固定步长线性TD建立函数中心极限定理，其中协方差保留了随机TD矩阵与平稳迭代误差的乘法分量。然后推导由同一轨迹驱动的并行Richardson-Romberg递归的联合泛函极限，并利用Brownian-bridge自归一化器构建渐近枢轴置信区域。同时提出视界索引设计，使步长在每次运行内保持不变、在更长视界间递减，以消除RR目标偏移等残差影响。

Result: 在FrozenLake和Garnet基准上的实验验证了：平稳目标的覆盖率、RR目标修正效果，以及视界索引设计的有限样本行为。理论上确立了在显式RR相关的速率窗口下，RR目标偏移、乘法残差和初始化效应在根号n尺度下可忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需选带宽或批长度、无需估计长程协方差的TD推断框架，内存开销仅与状态对比数量相关而非轨迹长度，并能在不同设计下分别对RR平稳目标和投影Bellman解进行有效推断，兼具理论保证与计算高效性。

Abstract: Constant-stepsize temporal-difference (TD) learning is attractive for policy evaluation, but inference from a single Markov trajectory must account for serial dependence and a stepsize-dependent stationary target. For fixed-stepsize linear TD, we establish a functional central limit theorem whose covariance retains the multiplicative component induced by the random TD matrix and the stationary iterate error. We then derive a joint functional limit for parallel Richardson--Romberg (RR) recursions driven by the same trajectory. A Brownian-bridge self-normalizer yields asymptotically pivotal confidence regions for prespecified state-value contrasts without estimating the long-run covariance or selecting a bandwidth or batch length. For such a contrast, the procedure admits a one-pass implementation whose memory does not grow with the trajectory length. At a fixed stepsize, the inferential center is the RR stationary target. We also study horizon-indexed designs in which the stepsize remains constant within each run and decreases across longer horizons. Under an explicit RR-dependent rate window, the residual RR target shift, multiplicative remainder, and initialization effect are negligible at the root-$n$ scale, yielding inference for the projected Bellman solution. Experiments on FrozenLake and Garnet illustrate stationary-target coverage, RR target correction, and the finite-sample behavior of the horizon-indexed design.

</details>


### [63] [Conditional Independence Tests for Constraint-Based Causal Discovery: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11156)
*Pavel Averin,Theodoros Moysiadis,Ioannis Katakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于条件独立性（CI）检验的综述，系统回顾了六类CI检验方法（偏相关、列联表、回归、近邻、核方法和机器学习方法），重点关注其在高维和混合数据类型医学场景中的假设、鲁棒性和可扩展性，并探讨了测试层面属性与图结构错误之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验是基于约束的因果发现算法（如PC和FCI）的统计核心，骨架剪枝和关键方向确定都依赖CI决策。然而，在高维和混合型生物医学数据中，CI检验的假设适用性、鲁棒性和可扩展性面临重大挑战，现有文献缺乏系统性的组织和比较。

Method: 该综述将广泛使用的CI方法组织为六大家族：偏相关、列联表、回归、近邻、核和基于机器学习的方法。对每个家族，分析其CI决策何时反映数据生成分布、何时失效；同时将检验层面属性（如随条件集大小增加的功率衰减、I/II型错误的非对称后果）与图层面错误（骨架恢复和v-结构定向错误）联系起来。

Result: 综述比较了主要R和Python库中各类CI方法的采用情况，总结了各方法族的局限性及对应的鲁棒层处理策略，并揭示了检验属性与图级错误之间的关联机制。

Conclusion: 开放挑战包括：无需离散化的混合类型CI检验、小样本误差控制，以及提升CI检验可扩展性的策略等问题仍待解决。

Abstract: Conditional Independence (CI) tests are the statistical engine of constraint-based causal discovery: in algorithms such as PC (Peter-Clark) and FCI (Fast Causal Inference), skeleton pruning and key orientations follow directly from CI decisions. This survey reviews CI testing with emphasis on assumptions, robustness, and scalability in high-dimensional and mixed-type settings common in biomedical domains. The survey organizes widely used CI methods into six families: partial-correlation, contingency-table, regression, nearest-neighbor, kernel, and machine-learning-based. Special emphasis is provided on the robustness layers that address the limitations of these families. For each family, the survey examines when CI decisions reflect the data-generating distribution and when they fail. By this, we link test-level properties, including power decay with conditioning set size and asymmetric type I/II error consequences, to graph-level errors in skeleton recovery and v-structure orientation. The survey also compares adoption across major R and Python libraries and summarizes open challenges, including mixed-type CI testing without discretization, small-sample error control, and strategies for improving scalability of CI-testing.

</details>

### [64] [Multi-Credit Calibration via Elastically Stopped Lévy Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10321)
*Graeme Baker,Agostino Capponi*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于弹性停止Lévy过程的信用违约互换（CDS）和指数分层（index tranches）定价框架。每家公司的违约时间由潜在谱正压力过程的上确界穿越独立指数障碍时刻决定，形成具有完全不可及违约时间的Cox构造，同时保留结构方法的可解释性和显式公式。通过添加一个公共复合泊松跳跃因子，构建了具有同时违约的简约多信用模型，并采用精确的Wiener-Hopf蒙特卡洛方案定价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信用风险定价模型面临权衡：结构模型直观且可解释但难以处理同时违约，约化模型灵活但缺乏经济可解释性。作者旨在开发一种兼具两者优势的框架，既能通过潜在压力过程解释违约机制，又能通过公共因子捕捉信用事件的相关性，且保持计算上的可操作性。

Method: 1) 采用弹性停止Lévy过程构建单名违约模型：谱正压力过程的上确界跨越独立指数障碍触发违约；2) 证明在相型跳跃下违约概率拉普拉斯变换存在有限部分分式公式；3) 引入单因子公共复合泊松跳跃作为相关性来源，实现同时违约建模；4) 使用精确Wiener-Hopf蒙特卡洛方法对指数债券定价；5) 在CDX北美高收益和投资级面板上对六种模型进行实证比较。

Result: 在每日CDX北美高收益和投资级面板上，该模型的驱动因子在六模型比较中获得最低的样本外误差，能复现濒临违约名称的倒挂利差曲线（Lévy子序过程被证明无法做到）。在指数层面，两参数依赖结构冻结参数时能弥合独立边际与分层定价差距的73%-89%，重新标记后可达95%；该框架在两个指数上均优于单因子高斯copula和Duffie-Gârleanu仿射强度基准。

Conclusion: 弹性停止Lévy过程框架在不牺牲解析可处理性的前提下，提供了兼具结构可解释性与约化模型灵活性的信用风险定价方案。它在单名和指数两个层面上均表现出优于现有基准的实证性能，尤其擅长刻画违约临近时利差曲线倒挂现象和相关信用事件的联合定价。

Abstract: We calibrate credit default swaps and index tranches with elastically stopped Lévy processes: each firm defaults when the running supremum of a latent, spectrally positive distress process crosses an independent exponential barrier. This yields a Cox construction with totally inaccessible default times, while retaining the interpretability and explicit formulas of a structural approach. Adding a single common compound Poisson jump factor to every firm's latent driver gives a parsimonious multi-credit model with simultaneous defaults, which is priced by an exact Wiener--Hopf Monte Carlo scheme. Its tractability rests on a single-name result we prove: a finite partial-fraction formula for the Laplace transform of the default probability under phase-type jumps. On daily CDX North American High-Yield and Investment-Grade panels, our drivers attain the lowest out-of-sample errors in a six-model field and reproduce the inverted spread curves of names heading into default, which a Lévy subordinator provably does not. At the index level, the two-parameter dependence structure closes $73\%$ to $89\%$ of the tranche pricing gap left by independent marginals with the dependence parameters frozen, and up to $95\%$ once re-marked to tranche quotes; our framework dominates a single-factor Gaussian copula and the affine intensity benchmark of Duffie--Gârleanu on both indices.

</details>

Abstract: Mixed-integer second-order cone programs are commonly solved by polyhedral outer approximation (OA), which iteratively strengthens a linear relaxation of the conic feasible region through cutting planes. We study how such approximations can be constructed more efficiently. First, we analyze the marginal contribution of a newly generated cut relative to cuts already present in the relaxation. Using violation- and volume-based measures, we show that this contribution decreases at least as fast as linearly as the new supporting direction approaches an existing one. Motivated by this geometric analysis, we develop a cut-generation strategy that balances separation depth with angular novelty and admits closed-form constructions. Second, we introduce a progressive-integrality OA framework that proceeds from an LP relaxation through partially integral relaxations before reaching the full MILP, thereby using inexpensive early iterations to strengthen the approximation before later mixed-integer solves. Computational experiments on CBLIB instances and large-scale AC unit-commitment models demonstrate complementary benefits from the proposed cut strategy and progressive integrality, substantially reducing the computational effort of outer approximation.

</details>

Conclusion: 当模型能利用多线程时，多线程是提升解质量和稳定性的实用手段；建模方式对时间依赖路由的线程扩展性结论具有核心影响。

Abstract: Thread scaling in Hexaly depends first on how the Time-Dependent Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows is modeled. We compare two Python encodings: one evaluates continuous travel-time functions through external callbacks, while the other approximates them with time slices evaluated natively by the solver. Independent CPU measurements show that the supported external-function interface uses a single evaluator worker regardless of the requested thread count. The native encoding, by contrast, makes effective use of the allocated cores. Across our selected benchmark panel, wider native searches produce better solutions both at the end of the run and throughout the search, while substantially reducing variation between seeds. Nearly every paired run improves from one to sixteen threads. The choice of encoding has an even larger impact than the thread count: native evaluation provides the strongest gains, at the cost of a travel-time approximation that must be sufficiently fine and independently checked. These descriptive results support multi-threading as a practical way to improve solution quality and stability when the model can exploit it, and show that the modeling approach is central to thread-scaling conclusions in time-dependent routing.

Abstract: Inspired by recent developments in dissipativity-based control, this work proposes new sufficient conditions for asymptotic stabilization of nonlinear systems using state feedback. Abstract: The integration of weather-dependent renewable generation increases the volatility of residual demand and raises the value of dispatchable low-carbon flexibility. This paper studies the optimal operation of a load-following nuclear power plant owned by a producer that must balance stochastic residual demand while accounting for ramping limits and costly changes in operating regimes. Nuclear output can be increased, decreased, or kept constant, and the production decision is formulated as a finite-horizon optimal switching problem. We analyze both a closed-economy benchmark, where excess production cannot be sold and shortages require costly back-up generation, and an open-economy setting, where the producer can trade electricity at prices driven by aggregate market residual demand. The value functions are characterized as viscosity solutions of a system of Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman quasi-variational inequalities, and optimal policies are computed using a monotone semi-Lagrangian scheme. The numerical results show how shortage costs, switching costs, ramping capability, and market access shape optimal nuclear load following. The analysis highlights the economic value of controllable low-carbon capacity in renewable-intensive systems and provides implications for flexibility remuneration, balancing-market design, and interconnection policy.

</details>

Abstract: This paper studies event-triggered gradient-based multivariable extremum seeking for nonlinear maps with polytopic Hessian uncertainty. Unlike existing event-triggered extremum-seeking methods, which first fix the controller (typically diagonal) and then design the triggering mechanism, the proposed approach jointly synthesizes the controller and the triggering mechanism through a co-design framework that admits both diagonal and full controller gain matrices. The co-design problem is formulated as a convex optimization problem with linear matrix inequality constraints. Lyapunov and averaging analyses establish exponential stability of the event-triggered system, and Zeno-freeness is proved to guarantee implementability. Abstract: This article studies the optimal common free-final time problem for steering the state covariance of a continuous-time stochastic linear system between prescribed initial and terminal covariance matrices. Finally, leveraging these sensitivities, we develop a trust-region line-search algorithm together with an infinite-horizon case detection method, and demonstrate its performance on three different problems: (a) an illustrative covariance-steering example, (b) a demonstration of spacecraft maneuver using covariance control, and (c) Gaussian mixture to Gaussian mixture steering.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场均衡（MFE）在经济学和博弈论中具有广泛应用，但现有的均衡计算方法要么依赖强凸或可微等严格假设，要么缺乏对离散化误差与迭代误差的统一量化分析。本文旨在提出一种适用于一般（非可微、非凸）成本算子的均衡计算方法，并建立同时刻画迭代与离散化误差的完备理论保证。

Abstract: We study the computation of static mean-field equilibria on a compact state space by formulating the equilibrium condition as a variational inequality over probability measures. We propose an entropic variant of Korpelevich's extragradient algorithm---the Kullback--Leibler Mirror-Prox method---in which Euclidean projections are replaced by relative-entropy proximal steps. Each half-step is therefore an explicit exponential reweighting of the current measure, implemented on a finite state-space discretization. Under Lasry--Lions monotonicity and continuity assumptions, we prove convergence of mesh-refined ergodic averages and obtain finite-iteration Minty-residual and approximate-equilibrium bounds that jointly quantify iteration and discretization errors. Under strong monotonicity, we derive metric convergence rates for the last, best, and averaged iterates. We also develop a KL-type Tikhonov regularization that selects the equilibrium minimizing relative entropy with respect to a reference measure. The framework applies to potential and nonpotential cost operators and does not require differentiability or convexity of the cost in the individual state.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study attainment boundaries for linear optimization over unbounded convex sets. For epigraphs of finite convex functions, convex conjugacy separates recession-cone copositivity, boundedness below, and attainment through three nested subsets of conjugate space. At a finite but unattained boundary value, we establish a facewise asymptotic selection theorem for inward perturbations of the objective. The theorem identifies the escaping directions of the perturbed minimizers, their precise blow-up scale, and the leading asymptotics of the optimal value; the critical set may be multidimensional, and no radial symmetry is assumed. For radial asymptotically conic epigraphs, we obtain a complete boundary trichotomy and universal scaling laws for objective tilting, hard truncation, and power regularization. For shifted ellipsoidal second-order cone programs, we derive explicit primal--dual formulas together with sharp escape, conditioning, and regularization rates. These models also admit an exact robust-optimization representation. At the attainment boundary, strict primal feasibility, zero duality gap, and dual attainment can coexist with failure of primal attainment.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 在给定的模型、硬件、停止规则和时间边界内，这是首个亚毫秒级端到端序贯凸求解的单次动力着陆轨迹，证明了通过块消元和定制化内层求解器的组合可以实现极低延迟的实时轨迹规划。

Abstract: Powered landing with variable mass, free final time, and quadratic aerodynamic drag requires the repeated solution of local convex subproblems, whose main online cost lies in the long dynamics-equality chain and the inner iterations. This paper develops a condensed sequential convex approximation designed for low latency. Exact block elimination removes 217 intermediate-state components and 210 interval equations from a 31-node model, leaving 100 primal variables coupled by six terminal equalities. A low-weight energy term and fixed quadratic proximal regularization make the ideal surrogate strongly convex with predictable curvature. The inner solver is an extrapolated proportional--integral projected gradient (xPIPG) implemented with fixed-size arrays, $3\times3$ interval solves, and a fused one-pass node map. The one-pass map is a deliberate low-cost approximation, not the exact joint proximal operator. We therefore evaluate the timed code by nonlinear trajectory residuals and independent physical checks rather than by a claim of exact KKT convergence. The single-precision C implementation completes one plan in four outer updates and 336 xPIPG updates. On an Intel Core i7-10875H, the median end-to-end solve time is \SI{374}{\micro\second} and the P99 value is \SI{512}{\micro\second}. All 100 common initial-state perturbations pass validation, and the median remains below \SI{0.7}{\milli\second} for 15--51 nodes. An independent high-accuracy first-order-hold integration gives a terminal position error of \SI{0.183}{\meter}. Within the stated model, hardware, stopping rule, and timing boundary, this is, to the authors' knowledge, the first submillisecond end-to-end sequential-convex solve for a single powered-landing trajectory.

</details>

Method: 基于SLQ问题的适定性，引入松弛补偿器将问题从正定情形推广到不定情形；分析无约束和约束控制下的随机Hamilton系统；研究相应的Riccati方程并推导闭环反馈形式的最优控制。

Abstract: This paper investigates an indefinite stochastic linear-quadratic (SLQ) control problem with parameters subject to Markov regime-switching. Based on the well-posedness of the SLQ problem, we introduce a relaxed compensator that extends SLQ control problems from the positive definite case to the indefinite case. We analyze the corresponding stochastic Hamiltonian system for both unconstrained and constrained control cases under the indefinite framework and derive the corresponding optimal open-loop controls. We further investigate the associated Riccati equations for both unconstrained and constrained control cases and derive the closed-loop feedback forms of optimal controls. We illustrate the theoretical results with an equity-bond asset allocation problem under un-constrained and non-negative control constraints. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the theoretical framework and demonstrate its practical value in solving complex stochastic control problems with Markov regime-switching.

</details>

Abstract: Mixed-integer PDE-constrained optimization problems are computationally challenging due to both the combinatorial nature of integer programming as well as evaluation of the model. Many algorithms and subsequent performance improvements have been developed to solve these problems, but they are often limited by problem size. We numerically analyze two such algorithms: SLIP and Patch-SLIP, which solve trust-region subproblems over either the full or partial domain, respectively. We additionally propose and prove convergence of a randomized third algorithm, Randomized-Patch-SLIP, which solves trust-region subproblems over randomly selected patches of the domain. We compare performance of all three algorithms with various improvement techniques found throughout the literature; the purpose of this work is to document the best combinations of these improvements in conjunction with various solvers. We additionally establish benchmark problems in image denoising and cloaking. Computational results are reported on combinations of algorithms and improvements, and code used in the experiments is provided as a package.

Abstract: While district heating networks are a key component of the energy transition, their operational planning is challenging due to substantial uncertainty in heat demand. We address this volatility without compromising system reliability. By adapting chance-constrained programming (CC) and conditional value-at-risk (CVaR) optimization to the mixed-integer unit-commitment problem for district-heating networks, we can compute optimal unit-commitment schedules under demand uncertainty. We generate heat demand time series using a Bayesian model, which explicitly quantifies forecasting uncertainty and yields predictive distributions as input to the network optimization. To handle the inherent uncertainty in heat demand, we apply both robust optimization approaches: chance-constrained programming limits the probability of unmet demand, while CVaR optimization penalizes severe shortfalls in the tail of the distribution. We evaluate the approaches both on real-world data from the Berlin district heating network and on a benchmark set of synthetic, realistically parameterized instances of varying sizes. The results compare the two uncertainty-handling methods with respect to solution quality, risk exposure, and computational effort.

</details>

Abstract: We consider a patrol problem in which a patroller moves among several geographically dispersed locations to detect attackers that arrive over time. Once the patroller arrives at a location, they can spend any amount of time searching for attackers at that location---and detect an attacker there with some location-dependent instantaneous detection rate---before moving to a different location. The objective of the patroller is to minimize the expected time an attacker stays undetected at a location, regardless of where and when the attack occurs. In the special case where travel times are negligible, we elucidate an optimal cyclic policy in which the patroller allocates a fixed fraction of their effort to each location continuously. The patrol problem becomes significantly more challenging when travel times cannot be ignored. We introduce two types of cyclic patrol patterns based on common patrol practice for perimeter patrol and border patrol, respectively, and derive formulae for the expected time to detect an attack in both cases. We also provide an algorithm for finding the best search time parameters in both of these cases. We give several examples where these cycle types perform well and numerically demonstrate that the optimal patrol policy depends highly on the structure and parameters of each patrol problem.

</details>


### [79] [Threshold Structure of Optimal Policies in Restart POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10936)
