  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重启POMDP在许多实际应用（如服务器重启、故障恢复、缓存管理等）中具有重要价值，但以往研究多局限于有限或可数状态空间。本文将研究推广到一般Borel状态空间，并系统性地刻画最优策略的结构性质（阈值结构）。

Method: 利用由最近观测状态和重启后流逝时间构成的充分统计量表示，将POMDP转化为完全可观测的MDP。在一阶成本劣化条件下证明折现与未折现准则下最优策略的阈值结构，在状态空间偏序和核随机单调条件下进一步证明阈值关于状态非增。对平均成本准则，采用消失折现方法，先证明最优阈值和相对值函数的一致有界性，再建立阈值结果。

Abstract: We study a Restart POMDP (Partially Observable Markov Decision Process) on a general Borel state space, where the controller either lets the hidden state evolve unobserved or restarts the system and observes the new state. Exploiting a sufficient-statistic representation consisting of the last observed state and the elapsed time since restart, we reduce the problem to a fully observed MDP. Under a natural one-step cost deterioration condition, we prove that optimal policies have a threshold structure in the elapsed time for both the discounted and total undiscounted cost criteria. When the state space is partially ordered and the kernel is stochastically monotone, we further show that the optimal threshold is nonincreasing in the state. For the average cost criterion, under additional assumptions of geometric ergodicity and domination of the transient gain, we establish analogous threshold results via the vanishing discount approach, after showing the uniform boundedness of the optimal thresholds and relative value functions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究线性无穷维无源系统的漏斗控制问题，针对受控输出为无源输出的积分（即机械应用中的基于速度测量实现位置控制）的场景，提出了一种带有动态增益监管器的非线性递归漏斗控制器，证明了闭环轨迹的全局存在唯一性、预设漏斗性能以及相关信号的全局有界性。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在系统-节点（system-node）框架下，提出一种非线性递归漏斗控制器，配备动态增益监管器。监管器在归一化递归误差接近预设监控水平时自动增加阻尼增益。控制器利用跟踪误差及其导数（导数直接由无源输出获得而非数值微分），通过递归结构处理受控输出为积分型输出的约束。

Result: 证明了闭环轨迹的全局存在唯一性、预设漏斗性能的满足、跟踪误差导数与无源输出的全局有界性。在额外的位置兼容严格耗散条件下，监管增益仅有限次增加并最终趋于常数，从而增益、内部状态和控制输入全局有界，递归误差漏斗裕度关于时间一致。最后以具有一致正分布阻尼的边界驱动欧拉-伯努利梁为例进行验证。

Conclusion: 本文为受控输出为无源输出积分的线性无穷维无源系统提供了严格的漏斗控制理论保证，扩展了漏斗控制在位置控制（基于速度测量）场景中的适用性，并通过Euler-Bernoulli梁的例子展示了该方法的实际可行性。

Abstract: We study funnel control for linear infinite-dimensional passive systems whose controlled output is obtained by integrating the passive output. In mechanical applications, this corresponds to position control based on a measured passive velocity output. Within the system-node framework, we propose a nonlinear recursive funnel controller with a dynamic gain supervisor for systems with distributed or boundary control and observation. The supervisor automatically increases a damping gain whenever the normalized recursive error approaches a prescribed monitoring level. The controller uses the tracking error and its derivative, with the latter obtained directly from the passive output rather than by numerical differentiation. We prove global existence and uniqueness of the closed-loop trajectory, prescribed funnel performance, and global boundedness of the derivative tracking error and the passive output. Under an additional position-compatible strict dissipativity condition, the supervised gain is increased only finitely many times and eventually becomes constant. Consequently, the gain, the internal state, and the control input are globally bounded, and the recursive-error funnel margin is uniform in time. The results are illustrated by a boundary-actuated Euler-Bernoulli beam with uniformly positive distributed damping.

</details>


### [81] [DD-suite: A cross-platform package to build Decision Diagrams for optimization purposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10957)

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DD-suite，一个跨平台、开源的决策图（DD）软件包，支持Python和C++两种语言，通过共享建模接口让用户能为任意以递归形式表达的离散优化问题构建精确、受限和松弛的DD。该包实现了DD归约、最短路径求边界、可视化工具和自动化测试，并设计了可扩展架构，允许用户添加新构造机制或运行自定义算法。实验表明C++实现比Python快5-6倍，且与专门的Rust框架性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策图已成为离散优化的强大工具，支持割生成、分解方法和分支定界搜索等多种算法。然而，现有的DD代码大多针对特定算法或应用定制，难以复用，限制了DD技术的广泛采用。作者希望通过提供一个通用的、可扩展的软件包来解决这一可复用性问题。

Method: 作者设计了DD-suite软件包，包含：(1) Python和C++双语言实现，通过共享建模接口保持一致性；(2) 支持构建精确、受限和松弛三种DD；(3) 实现DD归约过程、最短路径求原始和对偶边界、可视化工具和自动化测试套件；(4) 提供详细的文档、支持网页和四个组合问题的示例；(5) 开放的可扩展架构，允许用户添加新的构造机制或运行自定义算法，例如在混合整数规划求解器中嵌入基于DD的割平面过程。

Result: 数值实验表明，C++实现比Python版本快5-6倍，同时生成完全相同的DD；与专门的Rust框架ddo相比，性能仅在小常数因子范围内，证明DD-suite在保持代码可访问性和可扩展性的同时具有竞争力。文中还展示了将DD-based割平面过程嵌入到先进MIP求解器的成功案例。

Conclusion: DD-suite通过提供统一、可扩展且性能有竞争力的开源软件包，克服了现有DD代码缺乏可复用性的障碍，为决策图在离散优化领域的更广泛应用奠定了基础。它不仅是一个工具，更是一个可扩展的构建模块，连接了Python的易用性和C++的性能优势，同时验证了与专用框架的性能可比性。

Abstract: Decision diagrams (DDs) have become a powerful tool for discrete optimization, supporting a wide range of algorithms that span cut-generation procedures, decomposition methods, and specialized branch-and-bound searches. Despite this growth, their adoption remains limited, partly because most existing DD code is tailored to a specific algorithm or application and is therefore hard to reuse. We introduce DD-suite, a cross-platform, open-source software package for building and manipulating DDs for discrete optimization. DD-suite is available in both Python and C++ through a shared modeling interface, and lets users construct exact, restricted, and relaxed DDs for any discrete optimization problem expressed in recursive form. The package implements the DD reduction procedure, shortest-path routines for obtaining primal and dual bounds, a visualization tool, and an extensive automated test suite. Furthermore, it includes extensive documentation, a support webpage, and ready-to-use examples for four combinatorial problems. Rather than a closed solver, DD-suite is designed as an extensible building block: users can add new construction mechanisms or run custom algorithms on top of the resulting diagram, as we illustrate with a DD-based cutting plane procedure embedded in a state-of-the-art mixed-integer programming solver. Our numerical experiments show that the C++ implementation is 5-6 times faster than the Python one while producing identical diagrams, and remains within a small constant factor of ddo, a specialized Rust framework, confirming that DD-suite combines an accessible, extensible codebase with competitive performance.

</details>


### [82] [Gromov-Wasserstein Quantization and Clustering: Structure, Rates, and Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11016)
*Florian Beier,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gromov-Wasserstein（GW）量化问题，作为传统Wasserstein量化（如k-means）的推广，不仅对数据点聚类，还同时对空间的几何结构进行聚类。作者证明了GW量化问题解的存在性，给出了类似于k-means（Lloyd算法）的数值逼近算法，计算了欧几里得几何下的量化率，并通过实验展示了该方法在3D形状测地距离和神经网络结构化剪枝等场景中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚类方法（如k-means）通过Wasserstein距离进行量化，只能对固定空间内的点进行聚类，无法对空间本身的几何结构进行聚类。这限制了聚类方法在需要同时学习数据分布和空间几何的场景中的应用。

Method: 1) 提出GW量化问题，即在Gromov-Wasserstein距离下用离散测度近似一般概率测度；2) 证明GW量化问题解的存在性并给出解的刻画；3) 提出类似Lloyd算法的迭代数值逼近算法；4) 推导欧几里得几何下的量化率并与Wasserstein量化率进行对比。

Result: 证明了GW量化问题解的存在性及刻画；推导了GW量化率；数值实验表明该算法在3D形状测地距离聚类和神经网络结构化剪枝等新颖应用中能获得接近理论最优的近似质量。

Conclusion: GW量化将聚类推广到同时对空间几何进行聚类，为超出传统聚类方法的应用（如非欧几里得几何数据）提供了新的理论和算法框架，且数值性能与理论最优速率一致。

Abstract: Clustering is a fundamental class of data analysis techniques with the most important representatives being centroid-based methods like $k$-means. Such methods are strongly connected to quantization problems, which aim to approximate general probability measures with discrete ones. For example, $k$-means corresponds to quantization with respect to the Wasserstein distance. While Wasserstein quantization clusters points within a fixed space, this paper studies Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) quantization, which additionally aims at clustering the ambient geometry of the space. We show existence of solutions to the GW quantization problem and give a characterization that justifies an analogue to the $k$-means algorithm (Lloyd's algorithm) to approximate them numerically. We further calculate the quantization rate for usual Euclidean geometries that are used in the GW context, and relate it to standard Wasserstein quantization rates. Finally, numerical experiments show that GW quantization opens up many modeling possibilities beyond normal clustering methods (e.g., for geodesic distances of 3D shapes or structured pruning of neural networks) and that the introduced algorithm leads to useful numerical solutions with approximation quality often in line with theoretically optimal rates.

</details>

Motivation: 医院在周末床位容量有限的情况下，需要同时优化手术排程和病例分配，而现有研究通常分别处理这两个问题，未能有效整合。此外，在资源受限时如何在患者可及性、运营效率和员工工作量之间取得平衡是医院管理的核心挑战。

Method: 构建多准则混合整数线性规划模型，目标函数同时优化三个指标：最大化患者总优先权权重、最小化周末超额床位占用、最大化手术室利用率。开发列生成算法，将定价子问题建模为资源约束最短路径问题。框架支持块状、开放和改良块状三种排程策略。

Result: 计算实验表明，列生成算法在大规模实例上的解质量和计算时间均优于直接求解MILP。研究还分析了周末床位可用性、手术室利用率和患者总优先权之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该框架为医院在资源受限情况下平衡患者访问、运营效率和员工工作量提供了管理洞见，并证明了列生成方法在求解大规模联合排程问题上的有效性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the integrated Master Surgical Scheduling Problem and Surgical Case Assignment Problem under weekend bed capacity constraints. We propose a joint optimization framework that simultaneously determines the assignment of specialties and surgeons to Operating Room (OR) blocks, the selection and assignment of patients to these blocks, and the sequencing of surgeries, while accounting for reduced bed availability during weekends. The model is formulated as a multi-criteria Mixed-Integer Linear Program (MILP) that prioritizes the total priority-weighted amount of scheduled patients, minimizes excess weekend bed occupancy, and maximizes OR utilization. To solve large instances, we develop a Column Generation (CG) algorithm in which the pricing subproblem is formulated as a Resource-Constrained Shortest Path Problem. The framework accommodates block, open, and modified block scheduling policies. Computational experiments show that the CG approach outperforms the direct MILP solution in terms of solution quality and computational time, particularly for larger instances. We further analyze the trade-offs between weekend bed availability, OR utilization, and total patient priority. Our results provide managerial insights into how hospitals can balance patient access, operational efficiency, and staff workload when resources are limited.

</details>

*Georgios Pantazis,Michelle Chong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出将电压证书从单纯的评估工具扩展为控制器设计工具。针对下垂控制的低压馈线，推导了保持局部扰动界限、下垂斜率、逆变器限制和馈线敏感性的逐节点全时电压包络，并引入带虚拟协调和前馈补偿的下垂架构来重塑有效电压灵敏度，将联合控制器与证书设计转化为线性规划问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电压证书基于全局最坏情况构建，在异构馈线中会掩盖电压风险的来源位置，且沿下游方向因敏感性累积而日益保守。现有方法只用于评估给定控制器，无法指导控制器设计。

Method: 1) 推导确定性的全时、逐总线电压包络，保留局部扰动边界、下垂斜率、逆变器限制和馈线依赖敏感性，最坏情况证书作为特例；2) 提出带虚拟协调和仿射前馈补偿的下垂架构以重塑有效电压灵敏度，克服网络拓扑导致的界劣化；3) 通过缩放变换将联合控制器与证书设计转化为线性规划，确保前向不变性和无功功率储备约束；4) 控制器仅使用选定的通信链路。

Result: 在两个径向低压网络基准上评估：五用户住宅馈线和含四馈线的26用户农村网络。研究表明证书更紧致且空间信息更丰富，同时满足逆变器限制。

Conclusion: 电压证书可以同时作为控制器设计的工具，通过架构重塑和线性规划优化，可获得更紧致、空间信息更丰富的电压证书并保证逆变器约束。

Abstract: Voltage certificates for droop-controlled low voltage feeders are often constructed from global worst-case quantities. In heterogeneous feeders, such bounds can hide where voltage risk arises and become increasingly conservative downstream as feeder sensitivities accumulate. This paper shows that voltage certificates can be used not only to assess a given controller, but also to design it. Specifically, we derive deterministic all-time, bus-wise voltage envelopes that retain local disturbance bounds, droop slopes, inverter limits, and feeder dependent sensitivities, while recovering the worst-case certificate as a special case. To overcome the deterioration of these bounds induced by the network topology, we introduce a droop architecture with virtual coordination and affine feedforward compensation that reshapes the effective voltage sensitivity. A scaling transformation converts the joint controller and certificate design into a linear program which ensures forward invariance and the satisfaction of reactive power reserve constraints. The controller uses only selected communication links and guarantees the certified voltage and inverter bounds for all admissible disturbances. Our certificates and methodology are evaluated on two radial low voltage network benchmarks: a five-customer residential feeder and a 26-customer rural network comprising four feeders. The studies demonstrate tighter and more spatially informative certificates, while respecting inverter limits.

</details>

### [85] [Graph-Laplacian Variance Estimators for Finely Stratified Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10177)
*Yuehao Bai,Xun Huang,Joseph P. Romano,Azeem M. Shaikh,Max Tabord-Meehan*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究精细分层实验中平均处理效应的基于设计的推断，提出一类图拉普拉斯方差估计量，并分析了完整图估计量（Imai 2008）与配对分层估计量之间的偏差-最坏情况权衡，提出一种正则化图估计量来平衡局部性与最坏情况保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在精细分层实验中，不确定性仅来自随机化处理分配。不同方差估计量（如完整图与配对分层）在效率和稳健性上存在权衡，需要理论指导如何选择估计量以获得有效推断。

Method: 提出图拉普拉斯方差估计量的一般框架，以分层为顶点、边权决定层间比较的聚合方式。推导了度校准图估计量的精确偏差恒等式，并在此基础上提出正则化图估计量以控制最坏情况归一化偏差。

Result: 证明度校准图估计量存在向上偏差，偏差由相邻层真实处理效应平方差决定；配对分层估计量在协变量平滑时偏差小，但在非平滑时最坏情况偏差大；完整图估计量在弱异质性约束下是最小最大最优的；模拟展示了局部性与最坏情况保护之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 任何度校准图估计量都可用于有效推断，但配对分层估计量的有效性依赖于处理效应随协变量平滑变化的假设；在缺乏该假设时，完整图估计量更稳健；正则化图估计量提供了中间方案。

Abstract: This paper considers design-based inference on the average treatment effect in finely stratified experiments, where uncertainty arises only from the randomized treatment assignment. We focus on settings in which units are first stratified into groups of fixed size according to baseline covariates and, then within each group, exactly one unit is assigned to treatment. In this setting, we introduce a class of graph-Laplacian variance estimators in which strata form the vertices of a weighted graph and edge weights determine how between-stratum comparisons are aggregated. The canonical estimator of Imai (2008) corresponds to a complete graph with edge weights normalized so that each stratum has weighted degree one, while a paired-stratum estimator arises from a perfect matching graph. For the subclass of degree-calibrated graphs, in which each vertex has weighted degree one, we derive an exact bias identity showing that the corresponding estimators are upward-biased, with bias governed by squared differences in the true stratum-level treatment effects across adjacent strata. As a result, any such estimator may be used for valid inference. The identity further suggests that paired-stratum estimators constructed from a covariate-based perfect matching can induce small biases when treatment effects vary smoothly with the covariates. Without such smoothness, however, we show that paired-stratum estimators can exhibit large worst-case bias, and that, within the class of degree-calibrated estimators, the complete-graph estimator is minimax optimal for normalized bias under a weak bound on treatment-effect heterogeneity. Motivated by this contrast, we propose a regularized graph estimator that controls worst-case normalized bias while preserving much of the locality of the paired-stratum estimator. Simulations illustrate the resulting tradeoff between locality and worst-case protection.

</details>


### [86] [Testing Sign Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10294)
*Deborah Kim*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究有限个参数符号一致性检验问题，提出两种新颖检验方法：最不利检验（least favorable test）和条件检验（conditional test），两者均能控制渐近规模且适用于估计量间任意依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际经验研究中（如亚组/结果/时间段的处理效应符号相反检验、工具变量有效性检验）需要检验参数符号一致性，但现有符号一致性检验方法不能容纳估计量间的任意依赖结构。

Method: 提出两种检验：1) 最不利检验使用最坏情况零假设临界值；2) 条件检验先筛选具有大正值或负值估计的组成部分，然后对剩余符号未决部分在筛选事件条件下进行检验。两种检验均允许估计量间任意依赖；在独立估计量情形下，临界值仅依赖维度和检验水平。

Result: 两种检验在一大类非参数分布上均匀控制渐近规模。局部渐近功效分析显示权衡：最不利检验在符号约束起作用的边界配置附近功效更强，而条件检验在某组分与零分离较远时功效更强。模拟证据在有限样本中支持这些理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文的两种检验方法为符号一致性检验提供了灵活且稳健的工具，能应对任意依赖结构，并揭示了不同情形下功效的权衡取舍，为实践应用提供了选择依据。

Abstract: This article considers the problem of testing sign agreement among a finite number of parameters. This problem arises in empirical settings such as detecting treatment effects with opposite signs across subgroups, outcomes, or time periods, and testing instrument validity for local average treatment effects. For the null hypothesis that the parameters are either all non-negative or all non-positive, I propose two novel tests: a least favorable test and a conditional test. The least favorable test uses a worst-case null critical value, while the conditional test first screens components with large positive or negative estimates and then tests the remaining sign-unresolved components conditional on the screening event. Unlike existing sign agreement tests, both procedures accommodate arbitrary dependence among estimators; in the special case of independent estimators, the critical values depend only on the dimension and testing levels. We show that both tests control asymptotic size uniformly over a large class of nonparametric distributions. Local asymptotic power analysis reveals a tradeoff: the least favorable test is more powerful near boundary configurations where sign restrictions bind, whereas the conditional test is more powerful when some components are well separated from zero. Simulation evidence supports these theoretical predictions in finite samples.

</details>


### [87] [A Multinomial Probit Model for Asymmetric Choice Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10336)
*Cash Looi,Ruben Loaiza-Maya,Didier Nibbering*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出偏态多项概率（SMNP）模型，通过为潜在效用指定多元偏态正态分布，捕捉消费者选择中不对称的响应行为，并解决由此带来的识别和计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MNP模型假设对称的潜在效用分布，导致选择概率对等幅正负协变量变化作出对称响应，这在实证选择情境中往往不合理，会产生误导性的弹性和替代预测。

Method: 提出SMNP模型：采用多元偏态正态分布刻画潜在效用，引入备选方案特定的偏度参数；通过协方差重参数化保证识别和正定性，设计可解释先验，并提出双重数据增广方案实现Metropolis-Hastings within Gibbs采样器。

Result: 数值实验和消费者选择数据应用表明，SMNP能够恢复不对称的选择响应，提升概率预测表现，并在价格弹性与替代模式上产生具有经济意义的差异。

Conclusion: SMNP在保留MNP灵活替代结构的同时引入偏度，能更准确刻画实证选择行为中的不对称性，为选择模型的弹性和替代预测提供更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Standard multinomial probit (MNP) models specify symmetric latent utility distributions, implying that choice probabilities respond symmetrically to positive and negative covariate shifts of the same magnitude. This restriction is often implausible in empirical choice settings and can lead to misleading elasticity and substitution predictions. We propose a skewed multinomial probit (SMNP) model that captures asymmetric choice responses by specifying a multivariate skew-normal distribution for the latent utilities. The model preserves the flexible substitution patterns of the MNP framework, introduces alternative-specific skewness parameters, and nests the standard MNP model when skewness is zero. Introducing skewness creates identification and computational challenges because the skewness parameters interact with the MNP scale normalization and disrupt the conditional Gaussian updating structure used in Bayesian MNP estimation. We address these challenges through a covariance reparameterization that enforces identification and positive definiteness by construction, interpretable priors on the identified parameter space, and a double data-augmentation scheme that yields a Metropolis-Hastings within Gibbs sampler. Numerical experiments and applications to consumer choice data show that SMNP recovers asymmetric choice responses, improves probabilistic prediction, and produces economically meaningful differences in price elasticities and substitution patterns.

</details>

### [88] [Context and Symmetry in Auditing: A Case Study of Skeleton Inference in Motion Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10194)
*Emma Harvey,Emanuel Moss,Hauke Sandhaus,Abigail Z. Jacobs,Mona Sloane*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"情境化审计"（contextual audit）作为一种审计AI测量系统的方法，强调在产生测量的实践背景中进行审计，并引入STS中的对称性概念来处理真实地面真值未知或有争议的情况。通过动捕系统中骨骼推断的案例研究展示该方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类越来越频繁地与观察并推断他们的AI系统互动，但缺乏有效的方法来审计这些系统是否真的有效。传统AI审计需要识别系统行为并与"应有行为"对比，但这依赖于明确的地面真值，而许多AI系统（如人体推断系统）的地面真值往往是未知、不可知或有争议的。

Method: 提出情境化审计方法：在产生测量的实践背景中审计测量本身。该方法允许审计者审视审计过程中隐含的假设，并明确什么构成地面真值（定义为可验证的关于真实世界的测量）。同时引入STS中的对称性概念，使审计在地面真值未知、不可知或有争议时仍然可行。通过动捕系统骨骼推断的案例研究进行演示。

Result: 展示了情境化审计和对称审计如何在实践中实施，并提出了基于案例研究的未来动捕系统审计方向建议。

Conclusion: 审计AI系统不应简单依赖固定的地面真值，而应在实践的语境中审视测量过程本身。对称性概念为处理地面真值不确定的情况提供了理论工具，情境化审计为AI系统审计提供了更细致和可操作的方法论框架。

Abstract: Humans are increasingly expected to interact with AI systems that observe and make inferences about them - but do these systems actually work? A standard approach to answering this question is AI auditing. Conducting an AI audit requires identifying how a system behaves (i.e., determining what types of inputs to audit it with and then observing and documenting actual system behavior) and contrasting that with how a system should behave (i.e., determining what the nominal outputs of a system should look like). We argue that this is best done through a contextual audit, which we introduce as a method for auditing measurements within the context of the practices that produce them. We show how contextual auditing enables the interrogation of assumptions implicit in the audit process and allows auditors to be explicit about what serves as ground truth, which we define as verifiable measurements about the real world against which systems are evaluated. We outline how the concept of symmetry from science and technology studies can enable audits when ground truth is unknown, unknowable, or contested. Finally, to demonstrate how contextual and symmetric audits can be conducted in practice, we present a case study of skeleton inference in motion capture and propose areas suggested by our case study as particularly fruitful for future audits of motion capture systems.

</details>


### [89] [Who Gets Heeded? An Obligation-Level Audit of Responsiveness in EPA Rulemaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10329)
*Jianing Fan,Yue Yao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种义务级（obligation-level）响应性审计框架，用AI辅助方法度量公众评论参与是否与特定监管义务的修改相伴发生。将该框架应用于36个EPA锚定规则制定中的70,075条评论，发现：参与与修订呈适度相关；支持/反对方向不能区分结果；组织多数派参与集中于编辑性修改而非实质性修改。该发现将公平不对称定位于机构响应之前——即不同评论者群体在识别、解释和质疑具体法律义务上的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略在规则级或语料库级操作，过于粗糙，无法捕捉评论者寻求改变的具体监管义务。正式参与权利不等于实质性塑造规则文本的能力，需要更精细的分析单元来审计评论与监管变化之间的关联。

Method: 提出可审计的AI辅助框架：(1) 提取拟议和最终规则中的义务；(2) 将评论与它们所针对的义务进行匹配；(3) 对拟议到最终的结果变化进行分类。每个关键组件均通过盲人评审进行评估。应用于36个EPA规则制定中的70,075条评论，语料库包含786,197条评论、6,145个案卷（2010-2022）。

Result: 三个描述性发现：(1) 参与与修订在案卷内呈适度规模相关；(2) 支持与反对的方向不能清楚区分结果，这一无效结果与简单偏好聚合不一致；(3) 在组织的多数参与集中于编辑性修改而非实质性修改。盲人审计确认了第三个发现，并揭示文本相似性方法不足以区分编辑性与实质性监管变化。

Conclusion: 公平不对称存在于机构响应之前——不同评论者群体在识别、解释和质疑具体法律义务时的能力差异（识别的义务、解释的深度、质疑的能力），而非机构对评论的响应本身存在系统性偏见。文本相似性方法不足以衡量监管变化的类型，需要更精细的测量工具。

Abstract: Notice-and-comment rulemaking gives any affected party the same formal right to influence federal regulation, but formal access is not substantive capacity to shape rule text. Existing strategies operate at the rule or aggregate-corpus level, too coarse to capture the discrete regulatory obligations where commenters seek change. We introduce obligation-level responsiveness auditing, an auditable, AI-assisted framework for measuring whether public-comment engagement co-occurs with changes to specific regulatory duties. The framework extracts proposed and final-rule obligations, matches comments to the obligations they address, and classifies proposed-final outcomes; each load-bearing component is evaluated against blind human judgment. We apply the framework to 70,075 comments across 36 EPA anchor rulemakings, drawn from a corpus of 786,197 comments across 6,145 dockets from 2010-2022. Three descriptive findings emerge. First, engagement is associated with revision at a modest within-docket magnitude. Second, support-versus-opposition direction does not clearly differentiate outcomes, an informative null inconsistent with simple preference-aggregation. Third, under a permissive reconstruction of commenter type, organizational-majority engagement concentrates in editorial-refinement rather than substantive-modification outcomes at the cross-docket level. A blind human audit of the load-bearing outcome contrast preserves this third finding under corrected labels and reveals that text-similarity methods are insufficient for distinguishing editorial from substantive regulatory change, a measurement-validity lesson we treat as a supporting methodological contribution. Together, these findings locate the equity asymmetry upstream of agency response: in differential capacity across commenter populations to identify, interpret, and contest specific legal obligations.

</details>


### [90] [Inferential Capability Does Not Determine Legal Scope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10601)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文分析欧盟《人工智能法案》与《通用数据保护条例》（GDPR）中"推理"（inference）一词的不同法律含义及其不重合的适用范围。作者提出双层框架：推理兼具"构成性"（constitutive）与"保护性"（protective）两种法律功能，后者通过识别性、归因性和决策性三条路径运作。在智能体（agentic）架构下，这种不重合变得操作上紧迫。文章提出"构成性效应测试"作为解释规则，以确定GDPR第22条下的决策单元，并为推理链配置相应的文件义务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《人工智能法案》将推理能力作为界定监管对象的构成性特征，而GDPR则以保护性方式规制推理的后果，但二者从未明确定义推理且适用范围不重合。这一差异在传统单次系统中不可见，但在智能体架构中变得操作上尖锐。作者的核心论点是：推理能力本身不决定法律适用范围，缺乏推理能力也不等于获得法律豁免。

Method: 采用双层法律分析框架：第一层区分推理的两种法律功能（构成性与保护性），第二层将保护性功能细分为识别性、归因性和决策性三条路径。提出三个核心概念——推理阈值（inferential threshold）、推理范围（inferential reach）和推理链（inferential chain），并映射到归因链（chain of imputation）。针对新法规（EU）2026/1744，提出"构成性效应测试"（compositional-effects test）作为解释规则，确定GDPR第22条的决策单元及举证责任分配。

Result: 文章确认AI法案与GDPR的监管边界并非同心圆。分析了Regulation (EU) 2026/1744保留了构成性标准不变，虽新增了关于输出影响未来操作输入的条款，但未提供聚合规则。作者提出的构成性效应测试旨在填补这一空白，通过确定决策单元来协调两部法律的适用。

Conclusion: 在智能体架构时代，法律适用不能仅依赖推理能力的有无来划界。推理的构成性功能与保护性功能必须分离考量，并通过推理链的概念将技术架构与法律归因联系起来，以构成性效应测试作为弥补立法空白、协调AI法案与GDPR之间张力的解释工具。

Abstract: Two instruments of EU digital law place inference at their centre and mean different things by it. Article 3(1) of the AI Act uses the capability to infer constitutively: it is the central feature separating the regulated category from conventional software. The GDPR never defines inference, yet governs it protectively: the consequences follow from the processing of personal data and from what the inference says about, or does to, a person, whether or not the technology that produced it qualifies as an AI system.
  The two perimeters are not concentric. Their non-coincidence remained invisible in single-shot systems; agentic architectures make it operationally acute. The thesis: inferential capability does not determine legal scope, and its absence does not create immunity.
  The framework is two-level. Inference performs two legal functions, constitutive and protective; the protective function operates through three pathways - identificatory, attributive and decisional. Composition is not a fourth pathway but a cross-cutting architectural dimension which, with reach, persistence and reviewability, is what agentic architectures modify. Three concepts support it: the inferential threshold, the inferential reach and the inferential chain, mapped onto the chain of imputation.
  Regulation (EU) 2026/1744 left the constitutive criterion untouched and inserted a provision contemplating outputs that influence the inputs of future operations, without supplying any rule of aggregation. The article proposes an interpretive rule, a compositional-effects test identifying the decision unit under Article 22 GDPR together with the allocation of the burden of establishing it, and documentation duties calibrated to inference chains.

</details>


### [91] [Smart Enough to Go Extinct? An Evolutionary Challenge to the Value of General Intelligence and Its Ethical Implications for AGI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10730)
*David Klotz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对"通用智能具有极高价值"这一AGI研究的基本前提提出批判性质疑。通过进化视角论证，通用智能在进化时间尺度上的适应性价值远未得到证实，甚至可能是唯一会对其拥有物种产生生存威胁的生物策略（存在性风险悖论），并据此主张在创造AGI或人工意识时应遵循审慎原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战AGI研究领域公认的前提假设——通用智能是有价值的。作者指出，尽管这一假设看似不证自明，但缺乏实证支持，且现有AI风险论述（如对齐问题）忽略了通用智能本身的结构性风险。

Method: 采用跨学科论证方法：首先呈现通用智能价值的直觉论证，然后通过进化生物学证据提出挑战（比较蓝藻、鲎等长寿物种与智人的进化历程），提出"存在性风险悖论"概念，最后结合道义伦理学与预防原则进行伦理推导。

Result: 论证了通用智能在进化尺度上的价值不确定甚至可能为负；提出了通用智能可能是唯一产生自我生存威胁的生物策略这一存在性风险悖论；指出即使完美对齐的AGI也会继承此结构性缺陷；从道义伦理学与预防原则推导出创造人工智能体的谨慎义务。

Conclusion: 通用智能的长期进化价值存在根本性不确定性，这要求我们在创造AGI尤其是人工意识时承担审慎的责任，确保为新智能体创造能够繁荣的条件，否则应谨慎行事。

Abstract: The pursuit of artificial general intelligence (AGI) rests on a seemingly self-evident premise: that general intelligence, the kind of flexible, domain-general cognitive capacity exemplified by Homo sapiens, is extraordinarily valuable. This paper subjects this premise to critical scrutiny. We first present the intuitive case for the value of general intelligence before mounting an evolutionary challenge. We argue that, on evolutionary timescales, its adaptive value is far from empirically established. Numerous taxa, from cyanobacteria to horseshoe crabs, have persisted for hundreds of millions or even billions of years without anything resembling general intelligence, while Homo sapiens has existed for roughly 300,000 years and already faces self-generated existential risks. Mass extinction events do not preferentially favour cognitively sophisticated species. We argue that general intelligence may be the only biological strategy that generates existential threats to the species possessing it, an existential risk paradox with no parallel among non-intelligent survival strategies. Unlike prevailing accounts of AI risk that trace the danger to misalignment, we locate it in structural features of general intelligence itself, implying that even well-aligned AGI would inherit this liability. If the long-term evolutionary value of general intelligence is uncertain or negative, this raises ethical questions about engineering AGI and, more urgently, creating artificial consciousness. Drawing on deontological ethics and the precautionary principle, we argue that this uncertainty imposes a duty of caution: if we create a new kind of intelligent being, we bear responsibility for ensuring the conditions under which it can flourish.

</details>


### [92] [The GenAI Catch-22: Use of Generative Artificial Intelligence in Norwegian Newsrooms During the 2025 Parliamentary Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10773)
*Mari Reisjå,Anders Sundnes Løvlie*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对挪威新闻编辑室在2025年议会选举期间使用生成式AI（GenAI）的十个月案例研究，揭示了新闻业面临的内部威胁——记者自身使用AI所带来的风险，而非仅关注外部虚假信息威胁。研究发现，新闻室管理者的社会技术想象导致对AI能力和发展速度的不切实际乐观，使面向受众的GenAI服务计划崩溃，转而退化为内部工具的日常使用。同时，论文识别出一个"GenAI第22条军规"悖论：新闻室依赖人类专业知识来监控GenAI，但过度使用GenAI又会侵蚀这些专业知识，形成恶性循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在新闻业中的日益普及，研究者担忧其可能对新闻业及其民主功能产生不利影响。已有研究多聚焦于外部虚假信息威胁，而忽视了来自新闻室内部——记者自身使用AI——的潜在风险。因此需要通过对实际新闻室使用案例的深入调查，揭示这些被忽视的内部挑战。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，对2025年挪威议会选举期间挪威新闻编辑室使用GenAI的情况进行为期十个月的跟踪调查，通过对管理层和记者进行访谈收集定性数据，并分析其对AI的愿景、实践与挑战。

Result: 研究发现：1）新闻室管理者共享的社会技术想象导致对AI技术能力和发展速度过于乐观，最终面向受众的GenAI服务计划失败，退化为内部工具的日常使用；2）识别出"GenAI第22条军规"悖论——监控GenAI需要人类专业知识，但广泛使用GenAI又会侵蚀这些专业知识，导致监控能力下降。

Conclusion: 新闻业面临的GenAI风险不仅来自外部虚假信息威胁，更关键的是来自内部的、由记者自身使用AI所引发的威胁。新闻室需要在拥抱GenAI与维护人类专业知识之间找到平衡，否则将陷入无法有效监控AI系统的恶性循环，威胁新闻业的核心能力与民主功能。

Abstract: The increasing use of Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) in journalism raises concerns about possible detrimental effects both on journalism and its democratic function. We explore these risks through a case study of GenAI in Norwegian Newsrooms during the 2025 parliamentary election campaign. Based on interviews with managers and journalists over a ten-month period, we analyse how ambitious visions fared in the face of technological and practical challenges. We highlight the risk of an internal threat stemming from the journalists' own use of AI, contrasting the dominant focus on external disinformation threats. We show how newsroom managers shared sociotechnical imaginaries resulting in unrealistically optimistic beliefs about the capabilities of the technology and the pace of development, leading to plans for audience-facing GenAI services collapsing and giving way to more mundane uses of GenAI tools internally in the newsrooms. Furthermore, we identify a vulnerability in the newsroom's resilience against GenAI influence: a GenAI Catch-22. In order to monitor the GenAI tools and prevent errors and undue influence, newsrooms rely on human expertise. But by using GenAI extensively, the newsrooms risk a deterioration of human expertise, preventing them from monitoring the GenAI systems adequately.

</details>


### [93] [Technology, education and critical media literacy: potential, challenges, and opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10778)
*Mayte Santos-Albardía,Simón Peña-Fernández,Irati Agirreazkuenaga*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过深度访谈专家和调查141名传播与教育专业大学生，探讨媒体教育、媒介素养和教传播学中技术的影响。研究发现：技术虽提供教学改进机会，但课程融入有限且流于表面，教师培训不足，阻碍了学生批判性思维发展。结论呼吁建立批判性媒介素养，不仅教授技术使用，更培养学生在伦理反思框架下质疑和评估内容的能力，促进参与式批判公民意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息消费面临虚假信息、假新闻、偶然信息接触和深度伪造等新挑战，需要审视技术如何在媒体教育和媒介素养领域被理解和应用，以及学生和学术专家对这些领域的认知。

Method: 采用定性研究方法：对批判性媒介素养领域的顶尖专家进行半结构化深度访谈，并对141名传播与教育专业大学生进行问卷调查。

Result: 技术虽能为教学-学习过程提供改善机会，但在课程中的融入有限且往往表面化；教师缺乏有效管理这些工具的培训，阻碍了学生批判性思维的发展。

Conclusion: 需要建立批判性媒介素养，不仅要教育学生使用技术，还要让他们能够在伦理和反思框架下质疑和评估所消费的内容，从而促进参与式和批判性的公民意识。

Abstract: This study examines the impact of technology within media education, media literacy, and educommunication, and explores how these fields are perceived and understood by students and academic experts, focusing on the development of critical competencies and critical media literacy. Based on semi-structured in-depth interviews with leading experts in the field of critical media literacy, and a survey conducted with 141 university students in Communication and Education programs, this study explores how recent technological advances are linked to challenges in information consumption-such as disinformation, fake news, incidental exposure to information, and deepfakes-as well as the challenges and opportunities these issues present within educational contexts. The results reveal that, although such technologies provide opportunities to improve teaching-learning processes, their inclusion in the curriculum is limited and often superficial. In addition, we identify shortcomings in how teachers are trained to manage these tools effectively, hindering the development of critical thinking by students. The conclusions suggest a need for critical media literacy that not only educates students in the use of technologies but also allows them to question and evaluate the content they consume, all within an ethical and reflective framework that promotes participatory and critical citizenship.

</details>


### [94] [Policy Convergence and Divergence Across National and Within Regional AI Strategies: A Policy Design Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11006)
*Benjamin Faveri,Brie Bhasin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过对全球74个国家和3个区域AI战略的系统编码与分析，揭示各国AI战略在目标、方法和原则三个政策设计要素上的横向（国家间）与纵向（区域-国家间）趋同与分化模式，为政策制定者提供全面的证据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各国AI战略迅速扩张，但现有研究未检视其潜在的政策设计要素，也未评估这些要素在横向（国家间）或纵向（区域-国家间）维度上是否随时间趋同或分化。填补这一空白有助于识别共同实践、理解区域差异，并为政策设计者提供全面的设计要素集合。

Method: 对全球205个联合国成员国和非成员国的全面扫描中提取的74个国家和3个区域AI战略进行编码分析，采用潜在归纳式方法，围绕目标、方法和原则三个功能性政策设计要素展开，并分别检验横向与纵向两个维度上的趋同/分化趋势。

Result: 结果发现：横向维度上，各国AI战略在经济竞争力、研究支持和伦理AI使用上呈现强趋同，但在人权目标、参与式治理方法和以人为中心原则上持续分化；纵向维度上，非洲联盟垂直趋同度最高，欧盟在监管和经济优先事项上高度一致但在以人为中心价值观上存在分歧，北欧-波罗的海地区呈现混合的垂直趋同。

Conclusion: 这些发现为政策设计者提供了全面的证据基础，有助于在AI战略制定和更新过程中识别新兴的AI政策设计选择规范。

Abstract: Governments worldwide have responded to the rapid expansion of AI by publishing national and regional AI strategies. Comparing national and regional AI strategies to identify their convergences and divergences can uncover their common practices, understand regional variations, and provide policy designers a comprehensive set of policy design elements for their ongoing AI strategy developments. Yet, existing work has not examined their underlying policy design elements or assessed whether those elements are horizontally (country-to-country) or vertical (region-to-country) converging or diverging over time. This paper addresses that gap by coding and analyzing 74 national and 3 regional AI strategies drawn from a global scan of all 205 UN member and non-member states. The coding used a latent-inductive approach organized around three functional policy design elements: goals, approaches, and principles. Two research questions guided the analysis: to what degree are national AI strategies becoming horizontally convergent or divergent over time; and to what degree are national strategies becoming vertically convergent or divergent with those countries' regional AI strategy. Results indicate strong horizontal convergence around economic competitiveness, research support, and ethical AI use, alongside persistent divergence in human rights goals, participatory governance approaches, and human-centric principles. Across the three regions, the AU exhibits the highest vertical convergence, the EU demonstrated strong alignment on regulatory and economic priorities but diverges on human-centric values, and the Nordic-Baltic Region displays mixed vertical convergence. These findings offer policy designers a comprehensive evidence base for identifying emerging AI policy design choice norms as AI strategies are developed and updated.

</details>


### [95] [Who Uses Open-Weight Models? China and the Shifting Geography of AI in Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11090)
*Zackary Okun Dunivin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究科学领域中大语言模型（LLM）的选择趋势，分析了截至2026年6月的2100万篇全文论文，发现GPT系列模型在应用型和基础型AI研究中均占主导，但开放权重模型的使用比例稳步上升至2026年的44.0%。研究进一步揭示，这种开放权重的增长主要由高质量的中国开放权重模型驱动，中国机构研究者的开放权重使用几率是其他机构的2.23倍，表明开放权重模型的采用并非普遍转向开放科学，而是模型生态系统在地缘政治和平台市场背景下重新调整的一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM成为科学研究的热点，计算机科学家和STS学者一直倡导使用开放权重模型。作者希望系统性地调查：随着LLM研究日趋成熟、高质量模型家族日益丰富，科研人员是否真的采纳了开放权重模型？这是首次对科学研究中模型选择进行的系统研究。

Method: 研究使用了Semantic Scholar OpenAI Research Corpus（S2ORC）中截至2026年6月的2100万篇全文文章，采用混合NLP管道从文章全文中提取模型出现情况，并区分模型是被实际使用还是仅被提及。将语料库划分为单模型家族和多模型家族研究（分别作为应用型和基础型AI研究的代理），并使用逻辑回归和多项逻辑回归模型分析开放权重模型采纳的影响因素。

Result: 研究发现：GPT系列模型在单模型和多模型家族研究中均占主导，但两个领域正变得更加多样化。在单模型家族论文中，开放权重模型使用比例稳步上升，2026年达到44.0%。近期增长主要由高质量的中国开放权重模型驱动。逻辑回归显示，中国机构研究者的开放权重使用几率是其他机构的2.23倍，占2023年以来开放权重采纳增长的44.0%。多项逻辑回归模型显示这一关联集中在中国开放权重模型上：2026年中国机构论文的调整后使用率为37.1%，比无中国关联论文高出27.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 科学中开放权重模型的采纳并非普遍转向开放科学，而是模型生态系统更广泛重新调整的一部分，其中平台和市场以及塑造它们的社会文化和地缘政治背景决定了哪些AI系统成为科学工具。

Abstract: As LLMs have become a flashpoint for scientific research, computer scientists and STS scholars have advocated the use of open-weight models. Since LLM research has matured and more high-quality model families are available, have researchers adopted open-weight models? We present the first systematic study of model selection in scientific research, analyzing 21 million full-text articles through June 2026 from the Semantic Scholar Open Research Corpus (S2ORC). We employ a mixed NLP pipeline to extract model occurrences in article full text and determine whether they are used or merely mentioned by researchers. We divide our corpus into single- and multi-model family studies, which we take as a proxy for applied and foundational AI research. We find GPT-family models dominate both single- and multi-family research, but that both areas are becoming more diverse over time. In single-family papers, open-weight model use rises steadily, reaching 44.0% in 2026. However, we find that recent growth is driven by the availability of high-quality open-weight Chinese models. Further, a logistic regression model finds that open-weight adoption is heterogeneously distributed, estimating that researchers at Chinese institutions have 2.23 times the odds of using an open-weight model, accounting for 44.0% of the increase in open-weight adoption since 2023. A complementary multinomial model shows this association is concentrated in Chinese open-weight models: in 2026, their adjusted use is 37.1% among papers with Chinese affiliations, a 27.9 percentage-point over papers with no observed China link. These findings suggest that open-weight adoption in science is not a general turn toward open science, but part of a broader realignment of model ecosystems in which platforms and markets, and the sociocultural and geopolitical contexts which shape them, determine which AI systems become scientific instruments.

</details>


### [96] [Mediatised Participation: Citizen Journalism and the Decline in User-Generated Content in Online News Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11159)
*Simón Peña-Fernández,Ainara Larrondo-Ureta,Irati Agirreazkuenaga*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文基于系统性文献综述，对新闻媒体中的受众参与研究进行了批判性回顾。研究发现受众对信息内容创作兴趣普遍偏低，参与日益成问题；记者因维护自身专业角色而持保留态度，公司策略则优先追求参与的商业化盈利。文章认为公民新闻这一理念已近过时，可被视为一次失败的创新，并将受众参与界定为"媒介化参与"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第二代网络工具约二十年前将受众主动纳入媒体，公民新闻与用户生成内容被视为具有民主化价值的参与形式。然而困难很快显现，受众参与受到限制。在此背景下，作者希望通过对相关研究的批判性回顾，重新审视受众参与新闻媒体的现实状况及其本质。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，对受众参与新闻媒体的相关研究进行批判性梳理与分析。

Result: 研究发现：受众对创作信息性内容兴趣普遍较低，且其参与问题日益增多；记者为捍卫专业角色而普遍持抗拒态度；媒体公司战略则优先致力于将参与转化为盈利。公民新闻及其用户生成内容已显过时，具有失败创新的诸多特征。

Conclusion: 受众参与媒体可被界定为"媒介化参与"，公民新闻理想已成过去式，参与的实际效果与早期民主化愿景存在显著落差。

Abstract: The second generation of web tools shook the journalist profession approximately two decades ago with the proactive incorporation of audiences into the media. Citizen journalism and user-generated content arose as an object of interest due to the democratising value of participation attributed to them, with empowered citizens who could emulate the professional and institutional practises of journalists. However, difficulties soon came to the surface, and audience participation in news media began to be limited. Within this context, this article conducts a critical review of studies on audience participation in news media based on a systematic literature review. The results indicate that, in general, audiences showed low interest in the creation of informative content and that their participation has grown increasingly problematic. In addition, journalists are reticent as they defend their professional role above all else, while company strategies have prioritised making participation profitable. For this reason, the idea of citizen journalism that offers user-created content through the media appears to be a thing of the past, with many characteristics that could define it as a failed innovation. Therefore, the text concludes that audience participation in the media could be defined as mediatised participation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为TGO-IV（Transformer Geometry Observatory）的拓扑分析框架，利用持续同调（Persistent Homology）来系统研究Transformer表示在层间演化过程中的全局拓扑结构变化，从而揭示原始输入表示如何逐步转变为任务相关特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量Transformer可解释性研究，但现有工作主要分析孤立层或整个网络的表示，缺乏对单个表示在层间发育演化的深入研究。回答"Transformer如何学习"这一核心问题需要理解表示的点云流形如何在各层间演化。

Method: TGO-IV框架从token级表示点云构建Vietoris-Rips单纯复形，并利用多个互补的拓扑观测工具（持续图、条形图、Betti曲线、持续景观、瓶颈距离和Wasserstein距离）来追踪表示拓扑特征在Transformer各层间的演化过程。

Result: 该框架能够提供Transformer表示全局拓扑在正向传播过程中的全面演化分析，有助于隔离关键层或建立趋势，从而更接近解释原始输入表示何时以及如何演化为任务相关的特征表示。

Conclusion: TGO-IV通过引入拓扑学视角，为Transformer表示演化提供了超越局部几何属性的全局拓扑分析方法，为理解Transformer学习机制提供了新的分析工具和视角。

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [98] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于直觉模糊理论的深度随机向量函数链接网络框架（IF-dRVFL 和 IF-edRVFL），通过同时考虑隶属度和非隶属度，为训练样本赋予自适应权重，从而增强模型对噪声和异常值的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度随机神经网络（如dRVFL和edRVFL）对所有训练样本一视同仁，缺乏对噪声和异常值的处理能力。同时，隐藏层中污染特征的传播会削弱模型的决策能力，导致模型在真实噪声数据集上鲁棒性不足。

Method: 将直觉模糊理论引入dRVFL和edRVFL框架中。在核空间中利用样本邻域信息，联合考虑每个样本的隶属度（基于样本到各类质心的距离计算）和非隶属度（量化样本在局部邻域内的异质性），以此赋予训练样本自适应权重，有效区分干净样本、噪声和异常值。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上（有/无高斯噪声条件下）进行了大量实验，结果表明所提出的IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型在性能上优于现有的SOTA模糊和非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 直觉模糊理论能够有效提升深度随机神经网络的鲁棒性，通过样本加权机制使模型更好地处理真实世界中的噪声和异常数据。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [99] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurveFP，一种闭环乘积码本家族，通过在紧凑块缩放下将量化幅度分布在交错对数曲线上，实现低精度数据类型的算术协同设计。其核心创新在于使乘积运算成为精确的符号异或和整数索引更新，显著简化了乘积路径，同时保持FP8级别的数值行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数据类型可以降低语言模型成本，但大多数格式只优化标量保真度，却忽略了由其乘积引起的算术问题。这种分离导致乘积运算产生额外误差，限制了低精度训练和推理的质量。

Method: CurveFP采用闭环乘积码本家族设计：在紧凑块缩放下将量化幅度分布到交错的对数曲线上；有理基数调节动态范围与局部分辨率之间的权衡；统一条目索引确保每个非零乘积在代数上封闭；推导出有限相位计数来确定累加调度。具体实例化为CurveFP八位（E4C3/E5C2）用于训练，CurveFP七位（E3C3）用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP七位在四个7B-9B模型上以少一个元素位的优势击败张量级FP8困惑度，并与原生质量保持1.32%以内差距；CurveFP八位在全部36个配对前向和反向GEMM比较中降低操作数NMSE；在三个匹配的128.3M参数三元组中，每个模式完成3B token预训练，CurveFP八位的平均BF16推理困惑度为22.5366 vs FP8的22.5407，且三种子下格式诱发惩罚均更低。

Conclusion: CurveFP建立了算术协同设计范式，结合了FP8类数值行为、七位推理和显著更简化的乘积路径，证明通过数学上闭合的乘积编码可以同时实现精度与效率的优化。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [100] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SFRL（基于层的联邦表示学习）框架，通过可学习的层限制映射和流形约束几何对齐正则化，实现在异构联邦系统中无需共享全局潜在空间的有效表示学习。配套的Sheaf-FRL算法交替进行局部模型梯度更新和闭式Procrustes更新，并在确定性和随机设定下均收敛到一阶驻点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统面临数据分布、感知模态、模型架构、潜在维度及局部学习目标的差异，现有方法通常假设共享全局潜在空间，这在异构场景下不成立，需要更通用的表示学习框架。

Method: 提出SFRL框架，联合优化局部目标与基于可学习层限制映射的流形约束几何对齐正则化器。全局一致性通过正交变换和等距嵌入对齐相邻潜在表示实现，并由层拉普拉斯算子诱导的二次粘合正则化器强制执行。惩罚在少量共享试点样本上评估以保证可扩展性和通信效率。开发Sheaf-FRL分散式算法，交替进行局部模型梯度更新和边限制映射的闭式Procrustes更新。

Result: Sheaf-FRL在语义通信的协作分类任务中，在不同局部分布偏移水平下均优于基线方法，在局部和通信后分类准确率方面表现更佳，且对潜在空间维度压缩具有更强的鲁棒性。算法在确定性和随机设定下均收敛到一阶驻点。

Conclusion: SFRL为异构联邦系统提供了一种不依赖共享全局潜在空间的通用表示学习范式，其几何对齐机制在异构性和通信受限场景下展现出优越性与鲁棒性，且理论收敛性得到保证。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [101] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型智能体的工具文档进行了大规模实证研究，发现现有工具文档的信息字段存在显著异质性，且不同信息字段的有效性高度依赖于任务领域、LLM骨干和智能体范式。基于此发现，提出DocsChisel框架，通过分析失败执行轨迹自适应地优化工具文档（添加、移除和精炼信息字段），相比原始文档和现有基线分别提升任务成功率95.89%和75.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要聚焦于提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，而将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管有少数工作尝试通过重写或压缩来优化工具文档，但关于工具文档中包含的信息如何在不同设置下影响智能体性能，目前知之甚少。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究，揭示工具文档信息字段的异质性及其对智能体性能的影响；然后提出DocsChisel优化框架，通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹来识别文档相关的问题，并迭代地对每个工具进行信息字段的添加、移除和精炼操作。

Result: DocsChisel相比原始工具文档将LLM智能体的任务成功率提升95.89%，相比现有最强基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时优化时间和token开销有限。

Conclusion: 不存在能够跨不同智能体设置一致泛化的固定工具文档，自适应地优化工具文档能够显著提升LLM智能体的任务成功率，为工具文档优化这一重要但被忽视的研究方向提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [102] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowScout，一个执行引导的框架，用于从历史任务解决记录中自动生成集成工具调用的智能体工作流。与现有方法生成LLM-centric工作流不同，FlowScout通过挖掘工具协调骨架并利用蒙特卡洛树搜索结合执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑，在四个任务域上显著提升了工具调用正确率和执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高质量的智能体工作流目前主要依赖人工，需要大量领域专业知识。现有自动生成方法虽然能从历史记录中生成工作流，但主要产生以LLM为中心的工作流，真实工具执行被抽象和模拟，限制了生成工作流的可用性和稳定性。

Method: FlowScout将智能体工作流表示为包含LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边的有向图。首先从历史记录中挖掘通用的工具协调骨架构建初始工作流，然后通过蒙特卡洛树搜索，利用执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑结构。

Result: 在四个代表性任务域上与PM4Py、ReAct和AFlow三个基线比较，FlowScout生成的智能体工作流在工具调用正确性上提升至少92.69%，执行质量提升至少17.66%，且多次运行性能变化更低。

Conclusion: FlowScout通过将真实工具执行纳入工作流生成过程，有效解决了现有方法中工具执行被抽象化的问题，生成了更可靠、更稳定的工具集成智能体工作流。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [103] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了UserToolBench，一个用于评估工具使用大语言模型在个性化决策能力上的基准测试。该基准基于隐私清洗后的真实交互轨迹，包含10个用户画像、36个工具集、1,065轮对话、170个独特工具，覆盖信息缺失、单工具和多工具三种任务场景。实验表明当前强工具使用LLM在个性化委派任务上仍存在明显困难，特别是在多工具协调、缺失约束推断和长程行为一致性方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注画像回忆、风格模仿、泛化工具使用或响应级别的个性化，但缺少对工具使用LLM在代表用户做决策时的个性化评估。实际场景中LLM越来越多地代表用户执行操作，因此需要一种评估模型能否从交互历史中推断潜在用户偏好、识别何时需要澄清、并在信息不完整情况下产生用户对齐的工具调用轨迹的基准。

Method: 构建UserToolBench基准，包含：(1) 基于隐私清洗的真实交互轨迹构建数据；(2) 结构化人物画像与公开API风格工具生态系统结合；(3) 长程多轮轨迹；(4) 三类评估任务：信息缺失、单工具、多工具设置。数据集规模为10个用户画像、36个工具集、1,065轮、170个独特工具。

Result: 实验显示当前强工具使用LLM在个性化委派上仍存在困难。主要瓶颈包括：多工具协调能力不足、缺失约束推断困难、以及长程行为一致性差。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应从"输出是否听起来像用户"转向"LLM是否为代表用户做出正确决策"。当前模型在需要真正理解用户偏好并据此执行决策的工具使用场景中表现不足，这是未来需要重点改进的方向。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [104] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PI-VM算法，通过截断和边缘化路径积分控制公式导出值函数的时序递归形式，并利用时序差分学习和Girsanov定理实现离策略训练，在低维场景下实现与SOTA方法相当的精度但效率提升一个数量级，在高维场景下有效缓解模式崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于策略的LQ-SOC方法依赖全轨迹模拟导致计算成本高且不稳定；标准PIC方法也面临高方差瓶颈，需要寻找更高效且稳定的替代方案。

Method: 提出范式转变，从策略方法转向基于值的方法。核心创新在于对原始路径积分公式进行截断和边缘化，推导出值函数的时序递归形式；在此基础上提出PI-VM算法，使用时序差分学习近似递归值动态，并结合Girsanov定理与经验回放实现离策略训练。

Result: 在多种SOC基准和采样任务中，PI-VM在低维设置下达到与SOTA方法相当的精度，但效率提升一个数量级；在高维场景下有效缓解了模式崩溃问题。

Conclusion: PI-VM提供了一个可扩展的高效解决方案，能够以较低的计算成本求解复杂的随机最优控制问题，为SOC领域提供了新的基于值方法的研究方向。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [105] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了从成对比较中学习物品奖励的问题，重点考虑了众包工人可靠性（能力）的联合学习。作者采用Boltzmann理性模型（扩展Bradley-Terry-Luce模型），通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转化为条件高斯形式，推导出基于EM的算法，并将问题简化为矩阵感知问题以获得理论收敛保证。实验证明该算法在真实和合成数据集上优于多个基线，对垃圾邮件发送者和对抗性工人具有很强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台上的工人往往是不可靠的，可能因领域知识有限或追求收入最大化（刷量）行为而产生噪声比较。现有研究大多假设工人完全可靠，因此需要一种能够在学习物品奖励的同时学习工人能力（可靠性）的方法。

Method: 采用Boltzmann理性模型（扩展BTL模型以包含工人能力）；通过引入Polya-Gamma潜变量将逻辑似然转换为条件高斯形式，使优化可处理；基于此推导EM算法，简化E-step中的Q函数；将公式简化为矩阵感知问题并获得理论收敛保证。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验表明，该算法优于多个基线方法，并对垃圾邮件发送者和对抗性工人表现出很强的鲁棒性，展示了其在现实众包和奖励学习场景中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 工人可靠性与物品奖励可以联合学习，Polya-Gamma潜变量技术能够有效地处理逻辑似然而推导出可追踪的EM算法，该方法在实际众包环境中具有实用价值。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [106] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文填补了多类学习中最优超额风险率的空白：在每个固定oracle风险L*下，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，统一了可实cân边界(d_DS/n)与不可知边界(√(d_N/n)+d_DS/n)之间的所有区间，并将结果推广到列表学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最坏情况下的多类界限在最优分类器已经近乎正确时不会变小，缺少的是一个乐观率（optimistic rate），即波动随oracle风险本身缩放的理论保证。已有工作在两个端点（可实cân和不可知）已知最优超额风险，但中间区间是开放的，本文旨在填补这一gap。

Method: 上界通过组合cover-menu-compression架构（来自CEH+26）与可实cân率（来自Pab26），并引入一个新的面向比较器的相对压缩定理：一个size-k压缩规则在经验上支配比较器h时，其总体风险不超过L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)。下界通过pair-Assouad方案校准到L*，以及在Natarajan与DS分离所依赖的伪立方体上的纤维论证，在固定L*处用一个类别和一个分布同时强制两项。

Result: 在两个端点之间任意固定的oracle风险L*处，最优超额风险为Θ̃(√(L*d_N/n)+d_DS/n)，且对齐字母表大小一致。该界可由一个既不知道L*也不知道置信水平的learner达到。两个定理都推广到列表学习：针对最优r元组假设，同样架构得到乐观率和同形状的下界，移除了已知可实cân列表下界中的因子r。

Conclusion: 本文完成了多类学习中从可实cân到不可知学习全部oracle风险范围内的最优超额风险刻画，通过新的相对压缩定理将二元理论中的比较原理推广到多类标签（丢弃了不适用的Boolean立方体几何），并在列表学习中也建立了对应的乐观率和下界。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [107] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究首次从求职者（供给方）视角分析ML-enabled系统构建中三类角色（ML工程师、数据科学家、软件工程师）的软技能表述。通过300份精选CV语料，利用LLM管道提取显式列举和隐式叙述的软技能，并将需求方文献中的主张转化为13个可证伪假设进行检验。结果支持11个假设、1个部分支持、1个被否定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于ML协作中软技能的知识几乎完全来自需求方（招聘广告、调查、招聘经理访谈），忽略了求职者自身的表述视角。且已有的CV挖掘工作存在两个缺陷：一是基于关键词匹配，无法捕捉叙事性技能表达；二是描述性的，仅报告频率排名，未检验组间差异是否超出抽样变异。

Method: 使用300份覆盖三种角色的精选平衡CV语料，采用LLM管道提取显式列出和隐式叙述的软技能，并与人类标注的真实数据进行验证。将需求方文献的主张转化为13个关于角色特征、资历进阶和披露风格的可证伪假设，使用效应量并在族系错误率控制下进行检验。

Result: 11个假设得到支持，1个部分支持，1个被否定。求职者通过叙事而非关键词列表披露软技能的比例约为3:1，且在雇主最重视的能力（领导力、协调、指导）中叙事占比高达88-96%。资历使阐明领导力的几率几乎翻三倍。先前被假定为普遍存在的领导力能力，软件工程师的表达率仅为同行的二分之一。技术候选人的确会表达软技能，但基于关键词的筛选系统性地遗漏了这些表达。

Conclusion: 研究填补了软技能研究中供给方视角的空白，证明求职者通过叙事方式表达软技能，且候选方数据能够证实或反驳需求方的主张。关键发现是关键词式筛选会系统性地遗漏技术候选人的软技能表达，这对招聘实践和筛选工具有重要启示。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [108] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Fisher 信息矩阵的逆强化学习（IRL）双层级优化方法，利用内层策略最优处 Hessian 与 Fisher 矩阵成比例的性质，推导出结构化 Fisher 超梯度，并通过流式谱草图（streaming spectral sketch）近似逆 Fisher-向量乘积，避免了显式构建大型 Fisher 矩阵的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRL 可自然建模为双层级优化问题（内层做策略优化，外层度量策略与专家数据的差异），但外层超梯度更新需要计算涉及内层目标函数逆 Hessian-向量乘积，计算上极具挑战性，这是该方法的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 证明在内层最优处，内层目标的 Hessian 与策略的 Fisher 信息矩阵成正比，从而得到与自然超梯度下降（Natural Hypergradient Descent）密切相关的结构化 Fisher 超梯度；2) 为解决大型 Fisher 矩阵的可扩展性问题，使用流式谱草图近似所需的逆 Fisher-向量乘积，避免显式构造 Fisher 矩阵。

Result: 在离散控制和连续控制环境中与一阶随机双层级基线进行对比，所提方法在策略性能和奖励排序质量上均表现出竞争力，且 Fisher 草图降低了曲率存储复杂度，相较于显式 Fisher 求解器可提升计算效率。

Conclusion: Fisher 草图化的超梯度方法为 IRL 双层级优化提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案，兼顾了策略性能、奖励恢复质量与计算效率。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [109] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对212项研究的系统文献综述，探讨了物理信息机器学习（PIML）在预测与健康管理（PHM）中的应用，提出了包含观测偏差、归纳偏差、学习偏差和混合方法四类分类方案，并指出PIML虽能提升预测性能，但对某些局限性的改进声明缺乏充分证据支持，未来应聚焦于可迁移设计模式、基准比较和轻量级不确定性感知模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM应用中存在泛化能力差、无法推断因果关系和缺乏可解释性等固有局限，物理信息机器学习（PIML）通过将先验物理知识融入ML流程来缓解这些问题，但目前缺乏对其在PHM中应用的系统性审视。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，对212项研究进行分类分析，提出四类分类方案（观测偏差、归纳偏差、学习偏差和混合方法），并进一步按PHM任务维度对研究进行归类。

Result: 四类PIML方法在各类资产上均比传统基线展现出更优的预测性能，但文献严重偏向锂离子电池和轴承，且以特定问题解决方案为主；物理信息方法确实带来实际收益，但关于改善泛化、因果推断和可解释性等局限性的声明缺乏充分证据。

Conclusion: PIML在PHM中已展现出切实益处，但未来研究应优先关注可迁移的设计模式、比较不同集成策略的基准测试，以及开发轻量级、稳健且适用于实际在线部署的不确定性感知模型。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [110] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAR-PL方法，利用多动作会计日志为中小企业提供个性化财务指导。在85,078条公司月观察数据上，CAR-PL在毛利润上表现最佳，T-Learner在收入上最佳，上下文价值模型在速动比率上最佳，展示了不同KPI需要不同策略选择的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是自我选择的、经常共同发生的业务变更，而非随机化的推荐结果。这种观测性设置需要能够处理选择性偏差的策略学习方法。

Method: 提出Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL)，一种逐动作的R-learner，直接操作多热编码日志，并通过观测支持度进行选择正则化。与uplift T-Learner、保守上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化基线进行比较，采用公司不相交的保留样本和共享模型辅助评分规则。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利润点估计上最高(0.084)，T-Learner在收入上最高(0.085)，上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高(0.062)。在增长类KPI上CAR-PL与T-Learner无统计显著差异，但CAR-PL覆盖33-34个类别的选择面更广。结果在稳健性测试中保持一致。

Conclusion: 研究支持从多动作会计日志进行特定目标（objective-specific）的中小企业财务指导排序，不同KPI需要模型的选择取决于具体目标指标。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [111] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ChronoSSM，一种联合建模事件与时间戳的自回归状态空间模型（SSM），通过共享主干网络和组合token+时间生成目标训练，证明联合训练相比两阶段方法能产生更具时间信息量的表示且不损害内容生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型训练时主要关注"发生什么"（事件预测），而将"何时发生"（时间信息）作为次要考量。常见策略是两阶段方法——先用事件预测训练表示，再用冻结表示训练时间模型，但这隐含假设事件预测优化出的表示已包含足够的时间结构，这一假设可能不成立。

Method: 提出 ChronoSSM，一个自回归状态空间模型，使用共享骨干网络联合建模事件和时间戳，采用组合的token生成和时间生成目标进行训练。作者比较了联合训练（时间监督更新骨干网络）与两阶段训练（时间信息仅从冻结的事件表示中学习）两种范式。

Result: 在覆盖密集和部分时间戳监督的四个领域上，联合训练一致性地使冻结表示中更可恢复间隔（inter-arrival）信息，且整体上不系统性地损害内容生成质量。时间监督能产生更具时间信息量的表示，而不显著降低自回归事件建模性能。

Conclusion: 联合时间-事件训练是一种优于两阶段方法的策略：在不牺牲内容生成质量的前提下，能够产生更具时间信息量的表示，有助于时间推理、异常检测和事件时序重建等下游任务。

Abstract: Modern sequence models, from Transformers to State Space Models, have enabled powerful generative modeling across diverse domains, yet they are typically trained to predict what happens while treating when it happens as a secondary concern. In data-mining settings where events are associated with explicit timing information, this separation can limit temporal reasoning, anomaly detection, and faithful reconstruction of event chronology. A common strategy is to treat timing as an auxiliary signal, training a separate timing model using representations learned solely for event prediction. However, this two-stage approach implicitly assumes that representations optimized for event prediction already contain sufficient temporal structure.
  We introduce ChronoSSM, an autoregressive State Space Model (SSM) that jointly models events and timestamps with a shared backbone trained using combined token and temporal generation objectives. We compare the joint regime, where temporal supervision updates the backbone, with the two-stage regime, where timing is learned only using the frozen event representations. Across four domains spanning dense and partial timestamp supervision, joint training consistently makes inter-arrival information more recoverable from frozen representations without any systematic degradation in content-generation quality overall. Our results show that temporal supervision can produce more temporally informative representations without materially degrading autoregressive event modeling.

</details>


### [112] [Toward Human Rights Benchmarking for LLMs: A Pilot Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10268)
*Savannah Thais,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,Abhigyan Acherjee,Matilda Wysocki,Malcolm Langford,Caitlin Kraft Buchman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HumRightsBench——首个由专家验证的、基于场景的国际人权法推理评估基准。该基准改编IRAC法律推理框架为IRAP结构，并通过全球人权律师标注的真实场景，评估大语言模型在人权法义务结构上的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
