Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [247] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧几里得旅行商问题（TSP），提出了利用节点坐标几何信息的新型过滤算法，在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下实现了比现有方法更强的约束传播，并扩展到了欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP），实验验证了其计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束编程方法在处理欧几里得TSP时，通常计算完整的距离矩阵并将其视为一般TSP处理，忽略了节点坐标所携带的几何信息，导致约束传播不够强，求解效率受限。

Method: 提出新的过滤算法，在约束逻辑编程（CLP）中利用点的坐标几何信息（如欧几里得距离的几何特性）实现更强的约束传播；并将该方法推广到欧几里得TSP的其他变体，包括欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于几何信息的过滤算法在计算性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 利用坐标几何信息可以有效增强约束传播，提升欧几里得TSP及其变体的求解效率，对智能车辆和智能交通系统等实际应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [248] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Combodied Agents"（组合具身智能体）这一新范式，它将数字智能体与具身智能体的能力统一起来，以人的状态轨迹而非外部软件或物理状态作为建模、干预和评估的核心对象，实现从"完成任务"到"持续造福人类"的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI存在结构性缺口：数字智能体主要变换软件状态，具身智能体主要变换物理状态，但两者都无法理解人的内在状态（如遗忘、困惑、副作用或故意拒绝），也无法判断应提供何种适当支持。以老年人漏服药物这一场景为例，现有智能体只能发送提醒或送药，却无法解释行为背后的原因并提供精准支持。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents范式，构建闭环框架：(1)基于事件的多模态感知重建有意义的个人事件；(2)纵向、可修正的记忆提供时间上下文；(3)个人世界模型（Personal World Models）在不同决策和干预下预测个人未来状态与结果；(4)可接受性干预策略在同意、不确定性、安全性、可逆性和用户控制约束下选择适度支持。人员与环境反馈更新闭环，采用目的受限、不确定性感知、用户可修正的表示，而非构建完整的数字孪生。

Result: 统一了个人助手、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人机系统的碎片化能力；按人状态目标、关系上下文和智能体角色组织设计空间；提出以场景为中心的评估、代理权保持指标、基准要求、边缘端个人模型和治理方向。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉，确立了人的状态与代理权作为智能体的首要建模、干预和评估对象。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [249] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IO Factory，一个用于模拟信息与影响力战役的AI驱动框架，将其视为完整、可追踪的过程。该框架在受控模拟平台上连接角色、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化模型评估与人口配置变化，支持高达10万智能体的规模模拟，并产生可检查的曝光证据与信念变量变化测量，支持可复现研究和红队分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵威胁已从单个语言模型的文本扩展到AI蜂群——即持续协调的智能体群体，它们能适应平台反馈并将组织化战役伪装成普通社交互动。这类战役无法从孤立消息中识别，必须从规划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量到适应的连续频谱进行分析，现有方法缺乏对此完整过程进行模拟和追踪的框架。

Method: IO Factory在受控模拟平台内实现战役全过程，连接多个关键组件：角色（actors）、平台行动、曝光记录、结构化基于模型的评估，以及模拟人口的配置变化。系统记录每次运行中的参与者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则，支持战役时间线的大规模执行。

Result: 作者实现了该架构并在高达10万智能体的配置中进行了评估。结果表明IO Factory能够大规模执行战役时间线，并产生可检查的曝光证据和配置信念变量的移动测量。

Conclusion: IO Factory通过记录战役运行中的全部要素（参与者、目标、约束、曝光路径、测量规则），支持可复现的研究和针对协调影响力的红队分析，为理解和防御AI驱动的信息操纵提供了系统性工具。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [250] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，它在大型推理模型（LRMs）的每一步推理过程中动态检索并注入已解决的示例，以缓解顺序测试时扩展带来的收益递减或负收益问题。实验表明该方法在多个推理任务上持续提升准确率，在AIME 2025上相对提升高达60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型推理模型通过增加推理时计算量来提升性能，但研究表明顺序测试时扩展常常带来递减甚至负面的回报，因为更长的推理轨迹会导致不确定性增加、错误累积以及与原始问题偏离。因此需要一种更好的测试时扩展方式。

Method: ThinkRetrieve是一个测试时扩展框架，其核心思想是：给定一个包含问题及逐步解答的外部语料库，在LRM推理的每个中间步骤动态检索相关的已解决示例，并将这些示例直接注入到思考轨迹中，为模型提供"如何推理"的引导，而不仅仅是"什么事实相关"的信息。

Result: 在五个推理模型（1.5B-8B参数）上，针对GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025和SciQ四个数据集进行实验，ThinkRetrieve相较于标准测试时扩展持续提升准确率，在AIME 2025上相对提升高达60%。

Conclusion: 通过在推理过程中动态注入相关的已解决示例，ThinkRetrieve有效地克服了顺序测试时扩展的局限，证明了"如何推理"的示范引导比单纯的事实检索更能增强大型推理模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [251] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCGR，一个联邦生成式跨域推荐框架。通过将商品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列，跨域对齐由共享语义词表诱导，而非依赖于稀疏或隐私敏感的用户重叠。FedCGR通过可靠性感知语义接口注入本地协同过滤信号，并训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域特定参数本地化。实验表明其在多个Amazon跨域场景中优于联邦生成式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦跨域推荐面临核心矛盾：跨域对齐通常依赖行为锚点（如重叠用户、共享交互信号），但在联邦部署中这些锚点稀疏、不可用或隐私敏感。现有方法难以在保护隐私的同时实现有效的跨域语义对齐。

Method: 1) 将商品表示为公共元数据派生的离散语义ID序列，共享词表诱导跨域对齐；2) 设计可靠性感知语义接口注入本地CF证据，弥补语义瓶颈；3) 训练原型个性化生成器，根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，保留域特定知识。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域场景中，FedCGR一致优于联邦生成式基线，并在全排序和采样评估协议下与强序列推荐和联邦CDR方法达到竞争性性能。

Conclusion: 通过稳定的语义商品语言实现跨域对齐，同时显式自适应地处理域异质性，可以兼顾联邦隐私约束和跨域推荐性能，为联邦CDR提供了新的生成式范式。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [252] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA提出用紧凑可执行的CoT令牌（XCoT）替代传统的冗长自然语言Chain-of-Thought，用于自动驾驶的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中，通过自动构建的Reason-Action监督学习，实现高效、实时的轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自然语言CoT推理在自动驾驶实时控制中效率低下，因为其生成是开放式的、解码成本高，且难以作为面向行动的表征进行优化，不适合实时规划。

Method: 1) 用2-6个紧凑可执行的XCoT令牌替代冗长自然语言推理；2) 从记录的轨迹中自动构建Reason-Action监督；3) XCoT令牌通过共享多模态自注意力条件化固定轨迹查询；4) 确定性令牌-函数路由：Reason FFN处理XCoT令牌，Control FFN处理轨迹查询用于流匹配轨迹生成；5) 提出XCPO（XCoT策略优化）作为可选的细化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布数据集上将纵向ADE从1.645降至1.323；在换道场景中将横向FDE从1.616降至0.648；仅使用2-6个XCoT令牌即可大幅减少自回归推理开销，保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 实验证明面向驾驶的推理可以做到紧凑、可执行，并直接连接到轨迹生成，在保证实时性的同时提升轨迹预测精度。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [253] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出V-FiLLM框架，基于可执行计算树从真实表格生成金融推理基准，通过符号求值获得正确答案，无需人工标注。该框架支持四个独立可控的难度维度，实验发现模型在推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，在对抗性数值扰动下下降47%，而轻量级LoRA微调可显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在STEM领域评估LLM方面进展显著，但对结构化数据上的金融推理评估相对不足。需要一种可规模化生成、答案正确性有保证的金融推理基准，同时避免标注成本和生成器错误传播问题。

Method: V-FiLLM框架从真实表格构建可执行计算树，通过符号求值获取ground truth，再将计算树渲染为自然语言问题。难点通过计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文大小四个独立维度进行控制。

Result: 推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，对抗性数值扰动下下降47个百分点；LoRA微调使准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，并在FinQA上超越基础模型5个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化数据上的稳健金融推理仍是LLM的挑战，但轻量级、低成本的定向LoRA微调是提升组合推理能力的有效方向。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [254] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip是一种免评估的技能压缩方法，通过将技能表示为"技能契约+残差"结构，并遵循"解释一次、多处引用"的原则，将有重复的规则在适用作用域内只陈述一次、将重复动作序列分解为共享过程、仅将差异保留为显式异常，从而找到最短的可信结构解释。它支持一键模式（一次结构化抽取调用+确定性优化）和Zip-on-Write持续模式（无需重放任务或重解析历史），在压缩性能、泛化性和开销方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体（self-evolving agents）通过追加成功流程和失败修复来积累可复用技能，但随时间推移会出现同一需求在多个分支、示例和警告中重复陈述，常用动作序列被复制而非复用的问题，导致技能注入成本高、难以维护。通用提示压缩不适用，因为技能并非扁平文本——其名称和描述定义适用时机、工作流控制执行、工具和输出契约约束有效性、罕见异常可能仍至关重要。评估引导压缩虽能测试这些行为，但引入大量rollout、成本和压缩时评估集的依赖。

Method: SkillZip的核心思想是"解释一次、引用多次"：在适用作用域内仅陈述一次重复规则，将重复动作序列因子化为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式异常。该方法将这一直觉形式化为一个类型化的最小描述长度（minimum description length）目标，作用于技能契约和残差之上，并施加硬覆盖约束以覆盖每个抽取的触发器、工作流边、工具要求、义务和输出字段。该公式提供简单的共享阈值，结构性保证保留唯一罕见规则，并支持高效的局部更新。SkillZip有两种模式：一键模式（one-shot mode），仅需一次结构化抽取调用和确定性优化；以及Zip-on-Write持续模式，可整合每个自我进化补丁而无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史。

Result: 通过全面实验评估，SkillZip在压缩性能、泛化性和成本开销方面均展现了有效性和优越性，证明其相比基线方法（通用提示压缩和评估引导压缩）在保持技能全部语义约束（触发器、工作流、工具契约、输出字段和罕见异常）的前提下实现了更优的压缩率。

Conclusion: SkillZip通过将技能压缩建模为"找到最短可信结构解释"的问题，以类型化最小描述长度目标和硬覆盖约束为手段，实现了免评估、可持续更新、且能保留罕见异常语义的技能压缩框架，为自进化智能体技能库的维护与注入提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [255] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个轨道交通站点知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，该数据集显式建模了各类城市实体（如轨道站点、道路段、兴趣点）之间的空间与语义交互关系，并通过统一模式整合异构城市数据，以支持城市级轨道交通站点相关任务（如客流预测、站点周边商店推荐），实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级轨道交通站点相关任务需要大规模城市数据，但现有研究在数据组织上往往忽视了各类城市实体之间复杂的交互关系，缺乏对空间和语义信息进行统一建模的数据资源。

Method: 构建了RTSKG知识图谱数据集，设计统一模式将异构城市实体（轨道交通站点、道路段、兴趣点等）整合到一起，显式建模实体间的空间与语义交互，并以Linked Data形式发布，数据可通过w3id.org访问。评估采用站点周边商店推荐和知识增强的客流预测两个任务。

Result: 在站点周边商店推荐和知识增强客流预测两项任务上的评估均证明了RTSKG的有效性，表明其能够支持城市级轨道交通站点分析任务。

Conclusion: RTSKG作为一项开放可访问的知识图谱数据集，展现了在城市级轨道交通站点分析领域的潜力，为相关研究提供了统一的数据组织与建模方案。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [256] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现Agentic编程中的README文件（如CLAUDE.md）会无限制增长，将其归因于"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering）——由于不完全回忆，删除旧指令的代价随指令数指数增长。作者用24.7万个指令生命周期数据验证了这一现象，并证明提示词注释（prompt comments）可以显著抑制增长（-99.3%过剩指令）并提升真实Agentic指令遵循能力（最高+23.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent编程中的提示词文档（如CLAUDE.md）无限增长，影响模型性能和可维护性。现有研究聚焦于灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting），但忽视了其反面的"灾难性记忆"问题——即提示词只增不减的机制性根因。

Method: 1) 大规模实证：分析1,867个仓库中247,694条指令的生命周期，量化提示词增长规律（+226%增长、+4.9条/提交、删除对数风险-0.032/提交）；2) 注释方法：通过反转IFEval构建已知最优提示词的可验证世界，证明编码潜在推理的注释可去除99.3%过剩指令；3) 泛化验证：将相同反转方法应用于WildIFEval，验证注释在真实场景下的效果。

Result: 1) Agentic提示词无界增长：平均增长226%，每提交净增4.9条指令，旧指令被删除概率随年龄下降（log-hazard -0.032/提交）；2) 在IFEval反转世界中，注释消除了99.3%的过剩指令（从+211.3%降至+1.4%）；3) 在WildIFEval真实场景中，注释将Agentic指令遵循性能提升最高23.1%。

Conclusion: 提示词的无限增长源于删除指令的高指数代价（O(2^|D|)），这是"灾难性记忆"的具体表现。通过在提示词中加入注释（编码指令背后的潜在推理），可以有效控制提示词膨胀并提升Agent性能。作者以"如果英语是新代码，为什么还没有注释？"作结，呼吁将传统软件工程中的注释实践引入提示词工程。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [257] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出sLTN，一个扩展Logic Tensor Networks (LTN)的神经符号框架，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为一等公民引入语言中，使时序、序列和关系约束能在逻辑层面直接表达，并保持与原LTN语义的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始LTN框架主要适用于扁平的数据集合，无法显式捕获时序顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构组织信息，限制了其在结构化数据上的表现。

Method: 将结构维度（命名为与领域特定组织相关的张量轴，如时间步、序列位置、图节点）作为语言中的一等要素；支持显式量化、结构关系表达；形式化定义了sLTN的语法和模糊张量语义，并通过PyTorch实现一个包含声明式签名、公式解析和张量解释的框架。

Result: 证明在无结构维度时，sLTN会退化为原始LTN语义作为特例；在代表性时序和序列推理示例上进行了说明，并提供了配套的sltn开源库。

Conclusion: sLTN通过将结构维度引入神经符号框架，扩展了LTN对结构化数据的表达能力，同时保持了向后兼容性，为时序、序列和关系推理提供了一种统一的逻辑级约束表达方式。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [258] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个关于Grothendieck常数$K_G$上下界的案例研究，展示了AI在数学研究中的应用。研究者利用AI系统将$K_G$的已知最优界限收紧为$\frac{6\pi}{11} \le K_G \le \frac{\pi}{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$，并详细讨论了使用AI进行数学研究的经验、优势与局限，以及创造AI突破性见解理想条件的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在数学研究中越来越普遍，但如何有效使用它们仍不清楚。作者希望通过一个深入的案例研究，阐明AI在数学研究中究竟能发挥什么作用，以及如何为AI创造最佳条件以促成突破性发现。

Method: 作者采用了一个广泛的案例研究方法，使用AI研究系统来改进Grothendieck常数$K_G$的上下界。该系统能够产生被领域专家认定为新颖的见解，作者详细记录了AI辅助数学研究的过程，包括其优势、劣势以及创造理想研究条件的经验。

Result: 通过AI辅助研究，成功将Grothendieck常数$K_G$的最优已知界限收紧为$\frac{6\pi}{11} \le K_G \le \frac{\pi}{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$，且这些改进中体现的见解被领域专家认为是新颖的。

Conclusion: 该案例证明了AI系统能够在数学研究中产生真正新颖且有价值的见解，但仍然需要仔细设计研究环境和条件才能充分发挥AI的潜力。作者的经验为未来在数学及其他科学领域使用AI提供了宝贵的实践参考。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>

### [259] [Objective-oriented quantitative investment: A specification-driven framework for automated synthesis of trading strategy pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10410)
*Liangliang Zhang*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出目标导向量化投资（OOQI）框架，反对传统以标量指标打分的结果导向范式，主张将投资者意图形式化为可验证的策略规格，并通过编译器将规格翻译为受限的模块化流水线组装，逐条验证满足情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动化量化研究只回答"哪个策略在标量指标上得分最高"这一不完整问题，而专业投资者真正需要的是具有身份特征的策略——如纯净的选股alpha、抗单边下跌、受换手率和容量约束——而非单纯最高收益。

Method: 构建OOQI框架：(i)将完整策略流水线建模为带接口契约的可互换模块类型化设计空间（参考实例含8.85×10^8种组装）；(ii)将投资者意图形式化为策略规格语言——由8个需求家族的条款组成，支持硬/软语义与交互代数；(iii)编译器将规格翻译为约束组装并逐条验证。同时开发以满意度率为统计对象的验证协议，对搜索宽度、时间留出和随机组装零模型进行修正。

Result: 合成演示中，32个流水线组装下结果导向选择获得样本内最高信息比率但仅满足25%规格；规格驱动选择100%满足规格，仅付出5.5%得分代价。理论证明满意度驱动的合成在一般情况下NP难，但在无冲突场景可常数因子近似；规格构成与组装对偶的格结构；每条条款携带拉格朗日影子价格；滚动重认证通过e-processes实现随时有效。

Conclusion: 结果导向的选择会因表面高分而牺牲投资者真实意图，目标导向（规格驱动）的量化投资框架能在可接受的小幅得分代价下完整满足投资者规格，为自动化量化研究提供更本质的理论与方法论基础。

Abstract: Automated quantitative research has made striking progress, yet each system answers the same question: which strategy scores highest on a scalar metric? We argue this question is incomplete. Professional investors do not order "the highest return"; they order an identity--pure stock-selection alpha uncontaminated by style exposure, resilient in unilateral market declines, within turnover and capacity budgets. We call the incumbent paradigm result-oriented and propose Objective-Oriented Quantitative Investment (OOQI): a specification-driven framework in which (i) the full strategy pipeline is modeled as a typed design space of interchangeable modules with explicit interface contracts (8.85 x 10^8 assemblies in our reference instantiation); (ii) investor intent is formalized as a strategy profile specification--a composable language of measurable, falsifiable clauses from eight requirement families, with hard/soft semantics and an interaction algebra; and (iii) a compiler translates specifications into constrained assemblies and verifies satisfaction clause-by-clause. Because search over large assembly spaces inflates apparent satisfaction, we develop a verification protocol treating the satisfaction rate itself as a statistical object, subject to deflation for search width, temporal holdout, and random-assembly null models. A synthetic demonstration with 32 pipeline assemblies shows that result-oriented selection attains the top in-sample information ratio while satisfying only 25% of the specification, whereas specification-driven selection satisfies 100% of it at a 5.5% score cost. The accompanying theory shows satisfaction-driven synthesis is NP-hard in general yet constant-factor approximable in a conflict-free regime; specifications form a lattice dual to assemblies; each clause carries a Lagrangian shadow price; and rolling re-certification is anytime-valid via e-processes.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.ST'></div>

# q-fin.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [When the Fed Speaks: Dynamics and Forecasts of the Volatility Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10693)
*Lukasz Adamski,Robert Slepaczuk*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文旨在预测并实证检验隐含波动率（IV）曲面的演变，特别关注FOMC预定会议日期前后。研究发现公告前IV上升且该效应在短期OTM期权及高波动率环境下更显著；使用卷积二维LSTM模型结合FOMC日期特征进行预测，结果表明虽然IV曲面的噪声特性限制了ML框架的优势，但ML模型在异常日仍能有效预测IV曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FOMC会议公告会引发市场不确定性和IV的显著变化，准确预测这一效应对于风险管理和期权定价至关重要。以往研究多为定性分析，缺少对公告前效应的定量预测，且现有ML框架预测IV曲面多依赖降维技术，尚未充分探索直接在曲面上应用ML模型以及纳入外生信息（如FOMC日期）的可能性。

Method: 采用卷积二维LSTM模型，直接对IV曲面（不经过降维）进行时空信号学习；在模型中引入与FOMC预定会议日期相关的特征作为外生信息增强，以检验模型能否学习到公告前不确定性升高的效应；以随机游走模型作为基准进行比较。

Result: 实证证实IV在公告前上升，且该效应在短期OTM期权和高波动率环境下更强。ML框架（卷积二维LSTM）的预测优势因IV曲面的噪声特性而受限，但结合FOMC日期特征的ML模型在异常日（FOMC会议期间）仍能有效预测IV曲面。

Conclusion: 本文是首次将FOMC日期等外生信息直接纳入ML框架进行IV曲面定量预测的研究之一，也是最早直接应用ML模型于IV曲面而不依赖降维的工作。虽然噪声限制了模型优势，但研究强化了ML模型在异常市场条件下仍能有效预测IV曲面的观点。

Abstract: Our primary goal is to forecast and empirically examine the evolution of the implied volatility (IV) surface, with particular focus on the dates of scheduled meetings of the Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC). Firstly, we check if IV increases before the announcement and if thes effect is stronger for short-dated, out-the-money (OTM) options in high volatility regimes. In the second part, we turn the focus to verifying if the ML framework can beat the benchmark random walk in forecasting this effect. A feature related to dates of scheduled FOMC meetings augments the model, which allows us to discover if it can learn the effect of elevated pre-announcement uncertainty. Our contribution relies mainly on the quantitative prediction of the pre-announcement effect and the inclusion of exogenous information inside the ML framework used for the IV surface forecasting. It is also on of the first attempts to apply ML models directly on the IV surface without relying on dimensionality reduction. To achieve this, we employ a convolutional two-dimensional LSTM model, which is capable of learning spatio-temporal signals in the surface. Our analysis reveals that the edge of the ML framework can be limited due to the noisy characteristics of the IV surface. Nevertheless, our study reinforces the perspective that ML models can effectively forecast the IV surface also during abnormal days.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Complexity of Local Observation Consistency in Discrete-Event Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10111)
*Tomáš Masopust*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了分层和多智能体监督控制中两种局部一致性条件的验证复杂度问题：局部观测一致性（LOC）和局部重标记观测一致性（LROC）。结果表明，对于非确定性系统，两者均为PSpace完全问题；对于确定性系统，两者可在多项式时间内判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层和多智能体监督控制在部分观测下依赖LOC和LROC这两种局部一致性条件来保证抽象与原始系统之间的行为等价性。尽管这些条件已被广泛使用，但其验证的计算复杂度从未被确定，作者旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者从计算复杂度理论角度，对LOC和LROC两种一致性条件的验证问题进行了形式化分析，分别针对非确定性系统和确定性系统两种情形，给出了复杂度上界和下界的证明。

Result: 对于非确定性系统，LOC和LROC的验证均为PSpace完全（PSpace-complete）；对于确定性系统，这两者的验证可在多项式时间内完成（polynomial time decidable）。

Conclusion: LOC和LROC的验证复杂度已得到完整刻画：非确定性情形下是PSpace完全的，确定性情形下是多项式时间可判定的。这一结果对分层和多智能体监督控制的实用性评估具有重要意义。

Abstract: Hierarchical and multi-agent supervisory control under partial observation relies on local consistency conditions between a plant and its abstraction: local observation consistency (LOC) for the projection abstractions of hierarchical control, and local relabeling observation consistency (LROC) for the relabeling abstractions of multi-agent systems. Both are companions to a global condition and both have been used without their verification complexity being settled. We show that both are PSpace-complete for nondeterministic plants and decidable in polynomial time for deterministic plants.

</details>


### [262] [A Pragmatic Guide to Building Conservative Discrete Abstractions of Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10254)
*Jordan Peper,Krish Kapadia,James Gast,Ethan Howes,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个保守构造的、模块化的工作流程，用于为闭环动力系统构建离散抽象，以支持符号模型检验在信息物理系统上的验证，并通过三个案例研究展示了端到端流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号模型检验对CPS的验证依赖于将连续状态动力学离散化为有限状态抽象，但模型构建者在保持保真度的同时平衡悲观性和可处理性时面临常见陷阱（如欠逼近状态空间、欠逼近转移、不健全剪枝、规格提升不当），需要一个系统性的解决方案。

Method: 提出一个四步模块化工作流程：(i) 状态空间划分与抽象函数设计；(ii) 通过轴对齐包围盒、多面体或带PAC覆盖证书的采样构建保守转移；(iii) 使用认证擦除和反例引导抽象精化缓解伪转移和自环；(iv) 使用may-must语义实现对LTL规格的健全提升。

Result: 在三个案例研究上演示了端到端流程，并报告了不同设计选择对抽象结构、运行时间和验证结果的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作流提供了一种实用且默认保守的抽象构建方法，帮助模型构建者避免常见陷阱，使符号模型检验在CPS验证中可靠地应用。

Abstract: Symbolic model checking is an effective approach for verifying semantically rich temporal-logic properties of cyber-physical systems, but it hinges on discretizing continuous-state dynamics into a finite-state abstraction. To transfer verification guarantees from the abstract model to the concrete CPS, the abstraction must conservatively approximate the concrete state space and behaviors. Hence, model-builders must maintain this soundness while balancing pessimism with tractability. However, they face several common pitfalls such as under-approximating the state space, under-approximating transitions, unsound pruning of "degenerate" behaviors, and improper specification lifting. This tutorial presents a pragmatic, conservative-by-construction workflow for building discrete abstractions of closed-loop dynamical systems. The workflow consists of four modular steps with interchangeable subroutines: (i) state-space partition and abstraction-function design, (ii) conservative transition construction via axis-aligned bounding boxes, polytopes, or sampling with PAC coverage certificates, (iii) mitigation of spurious transitions and self-loops using certified erasure and counterexample-guided abstraction refinement, and (iv) sound lifting of LTL specifications using may-must semantics. We demonstrate the end-to-end pipeline on three case studies and report how these design choices affect abstraction structure, runtime, and verification outcomes.

</details>


### [263] [Switching Observers for Linear Systems: Beyond Individual Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10298)
*Masoud S. Sakha,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对具有双输出通道的线性时不变系统，提出了一种基于LMI的切换观测器设计方法。在仅有一个输出通道在任意时刻可用、且切换间隔均匀有界的假设下，通过离线求解LMI设计通道相关的Luenberger观测器增益，证明所设计的切换观测器能使估计误差收敛到零。主要贡献在于不需要单个输出通道可检测即可实现离线增益设计与收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际系统中，传感器可能因故障、通信限制或能量约束等原因，导致在任意时刻只有部分输出通道可用。现有切换观测器设计通常要求每个输出通道单独可检测，这一条件过于严格，限制了方法的适用范围。因此需要一种在输出通道不可检测的情况下仍能保证状态估计收敛的切换观测器设计方法。

Method: 本文采用基于线性矩阵不等式（LMI）的方法：首先利用两个输出通道联合可观的条件，构建LMI可行性问题来离线设计一组通道相关的Luenberger观测器增益；然后在切换间隔均匀有界的假设下分析切换观测器的收敛性。方法的核心在于将观测器增益的设计转化为LMI求解，从而避免了对单个输出通道可检测性的要求。

Result: 证明了在所提LMI条件下，所设计的切换观测器能够使估计误差渐近收敛到零。该结果在不需要任何单个输出通道可检测的情况下成立，放宽了现有文献中的关键约束条件。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种无需单通道可检测性的切换观测器离线设计方法，通过LMI工具统一了增益设计框架，拓展了切换观测器在实际应用中的适用范围。未来工作可能涉及更一般的切换规则或非线性系统的推广。

Abstract: This paper develops an LMI-based switching observer for linear time-invariant systems with two output channels, where only one output is available at any given time. We assume that the system is observable when both outputs are considered together, and that the time intervals between consecutive output switches are uniformly bounded. Under these assumptions, we design a set of channel-dependent Luenberger observer gains offline via linear matrix inequalities and show that the resulting switched observer drives the estimation error to zero. The main contribution is an offline gain design and convergence result for switched observers that does not require individual output channels to be detectable.

</details>


### [264] [Topological Feasibility Guarantees for Differentiable Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10332)
*Guangyu Wu,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对可微预测控制（DPC）这一自监督学习方法，利用拓扑分析研究了其诱导的可达安全集，从有限训练样本中推导出严格的确定性可行性保证，无需在线安全滤波器，并通过CBF代理损失显著提升策略训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPC相比在线优化MPC具有计算优势，但其可行性保证目前仅能通过概率方式或在线安全滤波器获得，缺乏离线策略优化的严格可行性保证，这是安全控制的核心要求，也是当前开放问题。

Method: 利用DPC将可微系统动力学嵌入计算图的模型驱动特性，从拓扑和几何角度分析学习控制策略与系统状态的性质；基于理论分析提出使用控制障碍函数（CBF）代理损失的自监督离线策略学习策略，从有限训练样本推导确定性可行性保证。

Result: 大量闭环仿真验证了理论发现，经验约束违反随训练样本规模增加单调递减至零，证明DPC策略优化能产生传统黑盒方法（如RL或监督学习近似MPC）结构性上无法获得的正式安全证书。

Conclusion: 该工作表明DPC在可行性保证方面具有结构性优势，为基于学习的控制中的可行性保证提供了新视角，证明模型驱动方法能获得黑盒方法无法达到的形式化安全保证。

Abstract: Differentiable predictive control (DPC), a self-supervised learning approach for approximating explicit model predictive control (MPC) policies, offers significant computational advantages over online optimization-based MPC. However, feasibility guarantees, a core requirement for safe control, are currently provided either probabilistically or via online safety filters. The lack of rigorous feasibility guarantees for offline policy optimization remains an open problem. This paper establishes deterministic feasibility guarantees for DPC using a novel topological analysis of the induced reachable safe set, without requiring online safety filters. By exploiting the inherent model-based nature of DPC, in which differentiable system dynamics are embedded directly into the computational graph, we analyze the properties of the learned control policies and the corresponding system states from topological and geometric perspectives. Inspired by our theoretical analysis, we propose a novel self-supervised offline policy learning strategy that utilizes a proxy loss with Control Barrier Functions (CBFs). Crucially, these properties not only significantly improve policy training but also enable the derivation of strict, deterministic feasibility guarantees from a finite number of training samples. Extensive closed-loop simulations validate our theoretical findings, demonstrating that the empirical constraint violations monotonically decrease to zero as the training sample size increases. Ultimately, this work illustrates that DPC policy optimization yields formal safety certificates that are structurally unattainable with conventional black-box methods, e.g., reinforcement learning (RL) or supervised learning-based approximate MPC, thereby providing a new perspective on feasibility guarantees in learning-based control.

</details>


### [265] [From Privileged Control to Deployable Adaptation:Fusing Mechanism-Guided Task Reduction with Learned Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10453)
*Xitong Niu,Peifeng Hui,Zheyong Jiang,Yuan Gao,Chuanlin Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种训练-部署不对称的控制问题：专家控制器在训练时可用已知增益和扰动，而部署时只能用参考信号和状态测量。作者提出一种机制引导的迁移方法，通过精确的采样数据恒等变换消除加性扰动，将学习任务简化为从因果状态历史推断逆输入增益，并提供了公共二次型证书以及残差、覆盖、切换、噪声和饱和等显式条件。实验表明该结构化学生控制器在未见的60秒试验中将跟踪RMSE相比调优观测器降低约69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时存在输入增益变化和大加性扰动的控制问题中，固定观测器或名义控制器无法达到模式感知设计的性能。直接模仿专家动作不安全，因为相同的瞬态学生观测可能对应不同的特权模式从而需要不同的专家动作，因此需要一种安全的机制引导迁移方法。

Method: 提出一种机制引导的迁移路线而非新的神经架构。利用精确的采样数据恒等式从专家控制律中消除加性扰动，将学习简化为从因果状态历史推断任务相关的逆输入增益。潜在目标从专家动作和部署可见轨迹中重建，无需真实植物参数作为学生标签。推导实际增广采样递归的公共二次型证书，并给出残差、覆盖、切换、噪声和饱和的显式限定条件。

Result: 参数区间扫描显示名义观测器的误差随a减小而急剧增长，且所有测试的a<1情况下，最佳非失败调谐之上的下一个增益都会发散。直接动作网络虽离线误差适中但在闭环中失败，而结构化学生控制器接近特权专家性能，在未见的60秒试验中将跟踪RMSE相比调优观测器降低约69%。

Conclusion: 本文贡献在于提供了一种可解释的设计视角，将特权多模式控制知识转化为可部署的自适应控制器，并给出了迁移有意义的条件，为特权信息与部署控制之间的鸿沟提供了一条系统性的解决路径。

Abstract: Simultaneous input-gain variation and large additive disturbance create a control problem in which a fixed observer or nominal controller may be unable to reproduce the performance of a regime-aware design. We study a training--deployment asymmetry: during simulation or commissioning, an expert controller is allowed to use the known gain and disturbance, whereas the deployed controller can use only the reference and measured states. Directly imitating expert actions is generally unsafe because the same instantaneous student observation may correspond to different privileged regimes and hence different expert actions. We propose a mechanism-guided transfer route rather than a new neural architecture. An exact sampled-data identity removes the additive disturbance from the expert law and reduces learning to a task-relevant inverse input gain inferred from causal state history. The latent target is reconstructed from expert actions and deployment-visible trajectories, so the true plant parameter is not required as a student label. A common-quadratic certificate is derived for the actual augmented sampled recursion, followed by explicit residual, coverage, switching, noise, and saturation qualifications. A parameter-regime scan shows that the nominal observer's error grows sharply as $a$ decreases and that the next gain above the best non-failing tuning diverges for every tested $a<1$. Direct action networks also fail in closed loop despite moderate offline error, whereas the structured student remains close to the privileged expert and reduces tracking RMSE by about 69\% relative to the tuned observer in unseen 60-s trials. The contribution is an interpretable design perspective for turning privileged multi-regime control knowledge into a deployable adaptive controller, together with conditions under which the transfer is meaningful.

</details>


### [266] [Nuclear fusion for AI: A pathway to power data centers sustainably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10454)
*Layla Araiinejad,Vineet Jagadeesan Nair*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨核聚变能否为快速增长的人工智能数据中心需求提供可扩展、低碳的电力来源，通过技术经济分析认为聚变（尤其是磁约束系统）有望与数据中心负荷曲线高度匹配，成为可持续供电的战略性路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型、云计算和加密货币挖矿正加速电力消耗增长，数据中心预计将在未来几十年占据美国和全球电力使用量的更大份额，给电网可靠性和脱碳目标带来巨大压力。亟需评估核聚变能否作为满足这一快速增长需求的可扩展低碳电源。

Method: 运用比较分析方法，从容量因子、平准化电力成本、电网并网约束和部署路径等维度，对比核聚变与间歇性可再生能源、核裂变在匹配数据中心负载曲线方面的技术经济表现；同时评估监管发展态势和社会政治可行性。

Result: 初步技术经济分析表明，多个第N代聚变概念（尤其是磁约束系统）可能在超大规模数据中心应用中与加固可再生能源系统和先进裂变形成成本竞争力。聚变电厂与数据中心共址部署可减少输电瓶颈、提升韧性。

Conclusion: 核聚变的有利安全特性和较低废物负担改善了其相对裂变的社会政治可行性，代表可持续供电下一代计算基础设施的战略性重要路径，应在政策和工业部署规划中优先考虑。

Abstract: This perspective examines whether nuclear fusion can provide a scalable, low-carbon power source for rapidly growing AI-driven data center demand. As large language models, cloud computing, and cryptocurrency mining accelerate electricity consumption growth, data centers are projected to account for a substantially larger share of U.S. and global electricity use in the coming decades, creating significant pressure on grid reliability and decarbonization goals. We evaluate the technical and economic alignment between data center load profiles and nuclear power, particularly fusion, through a comparative analysis of capacity factors, levelized cost of electricity, grid interconnection constraints, and deployment pathways. Unlike intermittent renewables, nuclear fission and fusion offer high-capacity-factor, firm baseload generation suited to AI training and inference workloads that require continuous, reliable power. Preliminary techno-economic analysis suggests that several Nth-of-a-kind fusion concepts, particularly magnetic confinement systems, may become cost-competitive with firmed renewable systems and advanced fission for hyperscale data center applications. Co-location of fusion plants with data centers further reduces transmission bottlenecks, improves resilience, and aligns with emerging hyperscaler procurement strategies. We also assess recent regulatory developments and argue that fusion's favorable safety profile and reduced waste burden improve its long-term social and political viability relative to fission. We conclude that fusion represents a strategically important pathway for sustainably powering next-generation computing infrastructure and should be prioritized in both policy and industrial deployment planning.

</details>


### [267] [Removal-Only Actuation in Age-Structured Branching Populations: Fundamental Limits of Equilibrium Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10641)
*Ouerdia Arezki,Ali Zemouche*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究亚临界年龄结构分支种群中的预防性清除（扑杀）控制问题。结果表明，这类清除策动器对Yaglom极限（拟静态平衡）的控制能力存在结构性边界：繁殖单独设定的传播屏障Λ̄在清除策动下保持不变，且可达到的衰减率上确界为Λ̄+ν*，其中ν*≤0是条件化永不绝后的谱系之马尔萨斯参数。该间隙|ν*|有闭式表达，且仅当所有个体后代数不超过1时为零。因此任何此类清除律都无法达到传播屏障，沿容许性边界可达到的衰减率由策动器的形状而非大小决定。作者用2001年坎布里亚口蹄疫疫情数据进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际流行病控制中，扑杀是重要的干预手段，但人们对通过年龄依赖清除策动能否将亚临界分支种群的拟静态衰减率推向理论极限（繁殖单独决定的传播屏障）缺乏深刻理解。理解这种结构性边界对于资源配置和控制策略设计至关重要。

Method: 作者将扑杀建模为年龄依赖的策动器，它提高死亡率但不改变后代分布。通过分析策动器与Foster-Lyapunov漂移的失配关系，论证传播屏障的不变性；进一步利用条件化永不绝后谱系的马尔萨斯参数ν*给出可达衰减率的上确界闭式表达。以2001年坎布里亚口蹄疫疫情为案例进行数值验证。

Result: 关键结果为：(1) 清除策动器无法改变繁殖单独设定的传播屏障Λ̄；(2) 可达衰减率的上确界为Λ̄+ν*，比屏障严格更小（除非所有个体最多一个后代）；(3) 沿容许性边界，可达衰减率由策动器的形状主导而非其强度大小。口蹄疫案例验证了这些结论。

Conclusion: 扑杀（年龄依赖清除）策略对亚临界分支种群平衡态的控制能力存在根本上限：无法达到繁殖单独设定的传播屏障，且存在不可消除的结构性间隙|ν*|。这为流行病控制中扑杀策略的有效性设定了理论边界，提示仅通过增大扑杀强度无法突破该极限。

Abstract: A subcritical age-structured branching population dies out almost surely. Conditioned on survival, it converges to its Yaglom limit, a quasi-stationary equilibrium that we take as the operating point for control. We model preventive removal (culling) as an age-dependent actuator that raises the mortality rate and leaves the offspring law untouched, and we show that its authority over this equilibrium is bounded for structural reasons. Two facts drive the result. First, the input is matched to the killing rate but unmatched with respect to the Foster--Lyapunov drift, so the transmission barrier $\Lbar$ set by reproduction alone is invariant under such actuation. Second, and this does not follow from invariance alone,} the supremum of the reachable decay rates is $\Lbar+ν^\star$, where $ν^\star\le0$ is the Malthusian parameter of the lineage conditioned never to die childless; the gap $|ν^\star|$ is given in closed form and vanishes exactly when no individual has two or more offspring. Consequently no removal law of this class reaches the barrier, and along the admissibility boundary the achievable decay rate is governed by the shape of the actuator rather than by its size. We illustrate these results on a model calibrated to the 2001 Cumbrian foot-and-mouth outbreak.

</details>


### [268] [AIDC Microgrid Vulnerability Assessment Under Computing-Power Coordinated Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10645)
*Ze Yu,Hongwei Zhen,Chao Shen,Mingyang Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文首次探索针对低碳AI数据中心的"算力协调攻击"问题，提出了一种不确定性感知的微电网脆弱性评估框架，结合逆变器参数篡改攻击和AI需求操纵攻击两类攻击面，利用置信加权实现与阻抗筛选方法识别攻击时间窗口和攻击向量，揭示了单一攻击无法达到的不稳定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型服务的快速增长，AI数据中心的扩张加剧了电力系统资源充足性和碳排放问题。虽然可再生能源整合提供了解决路径，但也引入了跨域稳定性挑战——可再生能源波动影响供给侧可靠性，AI数据中心工作负载波动影响需求侧可靠性，二者共同在微电网中产生互联稳定性风险。现有研究尚未系统探索此类跨域攻击威胁。

Method: 1) 提出不确定性感知的AIDC微电网脆弱性评估框架，捕获两类交互攻击面：逆变器控制参数篡改攻击和AI诱导需求操纵攻击；2) 考虑可再生能源侧预测不确定性和AIDC侧需求响应不确定性，引入置信加权实现并构建长期攻击可达域分析；3) 利用基于阻抗的筛选方法，将发电与负荷变化映射为稳定性裕度的侵蚀，识别脆弱攻击时间窗口与攻击向量。

Result: 案例研究表明：算力协调攻击可诱导持续逆变器频率偏移超过标称值的20%，达到单一攻击无法实现的不稳定条件；所提框架能从长期运行轨迹中提取稀疏、高置信度的脆弱时段。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统揭示了低碳AIDC微电网面临的计算-电力协同攻击威胁，证明了比单一攻击更具破坏性的跨域攻击的存在，并提供了一套有效识别脆弱时段与攻击向量的分析框架，为低碳AI数据中心的电力安全防护提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language model (LLM) services is accelerating the expansion of AI data centers (AIDCs), intensifying concerns over power system resource adequacy and rising carbon emissions. The integration of renewable energy provides a pathway toward addressing these pressures, but it also introduces new cross-domain stability challenges to low-carbon AIDCs. For example, variability in renewable generation affects reliability on the supply side, whereas fluctuations in AIDC workloads affect reliability on the demand side, jointly creating interconnected stability risks in AIDC microgrids. To address this problem, this paper is the first to explore computing-power coordinated attacks against low-carbon AIDCs. First, we propose an uncertainty-aware AIDC microgrid vulnerability assessment framework to capture two interacting attack surfaces: inverter control parameter tampering attacks, and AI-induced demand manipulation attacks. Then, accounting for renewable-side forecast uncertainty and AIDC-side demand response uncertainty, we introduce confidence-weighted realizations and construct a long-term attack reachable domain analysis. Furthermore, an impedance-based screening method is utilised to map generation and load variations to erosion of stability margin, thereby identifying vulnerable attack time windows and attack vectors. In addition, case studies show that computing-power coordinated attacks induce sustained inverter frequency excursions exceeding 20% of the nominal value and reach instability conditions unattainable by single attacks. The results also demonstrate that the proposed framework can extract sparse, high-confidence vulnerable periods from long-term operating trajectories.

</details>


### [269] [Optimal Stopping of Self-Refining Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10729)
*Kim Hammar,Tansu Alpcan,Emil C. Lupu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出将基础模型的自精炼（self-refinement）过程形式化为最优停止问题，根据预期改进与成本的权衡来决定精炼迭代次数，并通过随机逼近高效计算最优停止策略。在编码基准上的实验表明，该方法比先前工作提出的停止策略更具成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型可以通过外部反馈驱动的自精炼过程改善输出，但精炼迭代次数如何选择是一个关键问题——迭代太少可能输出质量不足，迭代太多则浪费计算成本。现有工作缺乏对这一权衡的系统性形式化，需要一个理论指导下确定最优停止时机的框架。

Method: 将自精炼过程形式化为最优停止问题，其中精炼迭代次数基于预期改进与成本的比较来决定。推导出最优停止策略，并证明可通过随机逼近方法高效计算这些策略。

Result: 在编码基准上进行的实验表明，所提出的停止策略相比先前工作中提出的停止策略显著更具成本效益。

Conclusion: 将自精炼建模为最优停止问题是一种新颖且有效的方法，能够在保证输出质量的同时有效控制计算成本，为自精炼过程中的迭代次数选择提供了理论依据和实用策略。

Abstract: Foundation models can improve their outputs through a self-refinement process driven by external feedback. In this process, the model is embedded in an iterative loop where it generates outputs, receives feedback from verifiers, and refines its responses through in-context learning. Following a novel approach, we formalize this process as an optimal stopping problem where the number of refinement iterations is decided based on expected improvement relative to cost. We derive optimal stopping policies and show that they can be efficiently computed through stochastic approximation. To evaluate our approach experimentally, we apply it to a coding benchmark for foundation models. The empirical results show that our stopping policies are significantly more cost-efficient than stopping policies proposed in prior work.

</details>


### [270] [KOOPMAN-Luenberger Observer Design for Nonlinear Systems with Application to the Monitoring of a Latent Thermal Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10746)
*Mustapha Habib,Dario Aguiar,Esther Kieseritzky,Tilman Barz,Qian Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于KOOPMAN算子理论的线性状态观测器（KOOPMAN-LSO）设计框架，通过将非线性系统提升到高维可观测空间，利用eDMDc识别带控制的线性预测器，在提升空间中构建离散时间Luenberger观测器，并通过对偶LQR求解观测器增益。该方法在相变材料潜热储能系统中验证，能以极低RMSE（最低0.0819°C）重构不可直接测量的内部温度状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动态系统的状态估计仍是一个根本性挑战，尤其是在测量稀疏且内部状态不可达的情况下。传统非线性观测器设计复杂且计算代价高，需要一种兼具非线性表征能力和线性设计简洁性的系统化方法。

Method: 1）利用物理信息基函数将非线性动力学提升到高维可观测空间；2）通过扩展动态模式分解（eDMDc）识别带有控制的线性预测器；3）在提升空间中构造离散时间Luenberger观测器；4）通过对偶线性二次调节器（LQR）框架求解观测器增益，确保估计误差动态稳定且可调。

Result: 在基于相变材料的潜热储能（LTES）系统中，该方法在多变运行条件下从有限输出测量中准确重构了不可测量状态。出口温度重建RMSE低至0.0819°C，可观测内部PCM温度一般低于1.0°C。

Conclusion: KOOPMAN-LSO框架将KOOPMAN提升的表征能力与线性观测器设计的简洁性和计算效率相结合，为有限传感条件下的非线性状态估计提供了一种系统化方法，并展示了在实际非线性系统中的实用潜力。

Abstract: State estimation for nonlinear dynamical systems remains a fundamental challenge, particularly when measurements are sparse and internal states are inaccessible. This work presents a KOOPMAN-based Linear State Observer (KOOPMAN-LSO) design framework that enables linear observer synthesis for nonlinear systems through KOOPMAN operator theory. The nonlinear dynamics are lifted into a higher-dimensional observable space using physics-informed basis functions, where a linear predictor with control is identified via extended dynamic mode decomposition with control (eDMDc). A discrete-time Luenberger observer is then constructed in the lifted space, and the observer gain is obtained through a dual linear - quadratic regulator (LQR) formulation to ensure stable and tunable estimation error dynamics. The proposed framework combines the representational capability of KOOPMAN lifting with the simplicity and computational efficiency of linear observer design, providing a systematic approach for nonlinear state estimation under limited sensing. Its effectiveness is demonstrated on a latent thermal energy storage (LTES) system based on phase-change materials (PCM), where internal temperature states are not directly measurable. Experimental results under varying operating conditions show accurate reconstruction of unmeasured states from limited output measurements, illustrating the potential of KOOPMAN-LSO design for practical nonlinear systems. The proposed approach achieves high-fidelity reconstruction with an RMSE as low as 0.0819 °C for the LTES outlet temperature and generally below 1.0 °C for observable internal PCM temperatures.

</details>


### [271] [Control of hybrid wind-wave energy systems using reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10754)
*Zechuan Lin,Kemeng Chen,Maosen Fan,Xiaofan Li,Xi Xiao,John V. Ringwood*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于混合风浪能（HWWE）系统的强化学习（RL）控制框架，通过在高保真仿真环境中直接学习实时控制策略，实现波浪能捕获与浮式平台运动抑制的双重目标。在包含IEA 15 MW风机、VolturnUS半潜式平台和三个环形波浪能转换器的系统中，所提出的RL控制器相比传统控制策略实现了显著的Pareto改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将波浪能转换器与浮式海上风力机集成形成HWWE系统，可进一步降低海上可再生能源成本。然而HWWE系统动力学复杂，精确建模只能依赖数值仿真，给控制设计带来巨大挑战。传统控制方法难以同时优化波浪能捕获和平台运动抑制这两个相互竞争的目标。

Method: 提出一种强化学习（RL）控制框架，控制策略通过与高保真仿真的直接交互来实时学习。建立了包含IEA 15 MW风机、VolturnUS半潜式平台和三个环形波浪能转换器的HWWE系统数值模型，并将其作为RL训练环境。从Pareto视角评估波浪能发电和平台运动抑制两个竞争目标的控制性能。

Result: 所提出的RL控制器相比传统控制策略实现了显著的Pareto改进：在相同平台运动水平下，波浪能捕获提高超过75%；在相同能量捕获水平下，平台运动降低近50%。显著扩展了HWWE系统可达到的性能边界。

Conclusion: RL控制框架能够有效应对HWWE系统复杂动力学带来的控制挑战，在波浪能捕获与平台运动抑制两个竞争目标之间实现更优的权衡，为混合风浪能系统的控制设计提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Integrating wave energy converters (WECs) with floating offshore wind turbines (FOWTs), to form hybrid wind-wave energy (HWWE) systems, is a promising approach to achieve further cost reduction for offshore renewable energy. In such systems, the control of the integrated WECs plays an important role, with the potential to generate additional wave energy while simultaneously suppressing floating platform motion. However, HWWE systems are characterized by complex dynamics, making accurate modelling only viable through numerical simulation, and posing significant challenges for control design. This paper proposes a reinforcement learning (RL) control framework for HWWE systems, in which the real-time control policy is learned directly through interactions with high-fidelity simulation. A numerical model is established for a HWWE system consisting of an IEA 15 MW wind turbine, a VolturnUS semi-submersible platform, and three torus-type WECs, which is then employed as the RL training environment. Control performance is evaluated in terms of both wave energy generation and platform motion reduction, two competing objectives, from a Pareto perspective. It is shown that the proposed RL controller achieves substantial Pareto improvements over conventional control strategies, e.g., over 75\% higher wave energy capture at the same platform motion level, or nearly 50\% lower motion at the same energy capture level, thereby significantly extending the attainable performance boundary of HWWE systems.

</details>


### [272] [Variable Step Time Delay Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10770)
*Yonsoo Kim,Hancheol Cho*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出变步长时延控制（VTDC），一种前瞻性的非周期性鲁棒控制框架，将反馈控制与控制更新调度联合设计，而非将控制器与触发机制视为独立组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事件触发控制将控制器和触发机制分开设计，导致触发监控负担大、需要预测或迭代搜索。作者希望借鉴龙格-库塔积分中的自适应步长调节思想，利用控制误差随步长缩放的结构特性，实现控制器与调度器的一体化设计。

Method: 基于滑模面的时延控制（TDC），证明系统时延误差（TDE）以控制间隔的二次方为界。据此构建闭式反馈律，自动增大或减小下一次控制间隔以调节TDE幅值。调度律直接从控制器结构推导，下一更新时刻通过代数计算确定，无需连续触发监控、未来状态预测或迭代搜索。

Result: 证明变步长闭环系统允许有界步长和步长比、排除Zeno行为，并使滑模变量一致最终有界。航天器姿态控制仿真验证了该框架的实践可行性和低计算负担。

Conclusion: VTDC通过将控制率与调度律联合设计，实现了无需传统触发监控机制的事件触发控制，兼具理论保证（Zeno排除、有界性）与工程实用性（低计算成本）。

Abstract: This paper presents Variable-step Time Delay Control (VTDC), a proactive aperiodic robust control framework that jointly addresses feedback control and control-update scheduling, rather than treating the controller and triggering mechanism as separate components. Inspired by adaptive stepsize regulation in Runge-Kutta integration, VTDC exploits the fact that the local control error can be structured to scale with the realized timestep. Using sliding-manifold-based Time Delay Control, the resulting System Time Delay Error (TDE), which reflects local model and uncertainty variations, is shown to be quadratically bounded by the control interval. This relation enables a closed-form feedback law that automatically enlarges or reduces the subsequent interval to regulate the TDE magnitude. Because the scheduling law is constructed directly from the controller structure, the next update time is determined algebraically without continuous trigger monitoring, future-state prediction, or iterative search. The resulting variable-step closed loop is shown to admit bounded timesteps and step ratios, exclude Zeno behavior, and render the sliding variable uniformly ultimately bounded. Spacecraft attitude-control simulations further demonstrate the practical feasibility and low computational burden of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [273] [Cross-modal topology decodes battery faults from sparse voltage snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10825)
*Jinwen Li,Yunhong Che,Simona Onori,Weihan Li,Xiaosong Hu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFault框架，通过将一维电压序列展开为多维相空间拓扑，利用双向交叉注意力机制解码复合电池故障模式。在99辆在役电动车的1640万条数据记录上验证，仅用500秒（<100 mV）的快照即可达到0.96准确率和0.84 F1分数，无需新增传感器即可实现高保真电化学诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电池安全是电动车大规模普及的首要瓶颈，但现场监测面临根本性不对称：复杂的电化学故障必须通过稀疏、低频的电压测量来诊断。现有方法难以在不升级硬件的条件下解决重叠故障模式之间的信号模糊性问题。

Method: DeFault采用跨模态诊断框架，将一维电压序列数学展开为多维相空间拓扑结构，并通过双向交叉注意力机制作为自主的、物理对齐的滤波器，显式解码序列方法无法识别的复合故障模式。

Result: 在包含1640万条数据记录、99辆在役电动车的现场数据集上验证，对四种故障类型仅用500秒快照（跨度<100 mV）即可实现平均准确率0.96和F1分数0.84。

Conclusion: 该工作证明无需新增传感器即可在存量车队上实现高保真、可解释的电化学诊断，为电池安全危机提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Battery safety remains the primary bottleneck for mass electric vehicle (EV) adoption, yet field monitoring is hamstrung by a fundamental asymmetry: complex electrochemical faults must be diagnosed via sparse, low-frequency voltage measurements. Existing methods struggle to resolve the signal ambiguity between overlapping fault modes without hardware upgrades. Here, we demonstrate that these distinct fault fingerprints are not lost, but topologically folded within voltage snapshots. We introduce DeFault, a cross-modal diagnostic framework that mathematically unfolds one-dimensional voltage sequences into multi-dimensional phase-space topologies. DeFault employs a bidirectional cross-attention mechanism that acts as an autonomous, physics-aligned filter, explicitly decoding compounded fault modes that remain fundamentally invisible to sequence-based methods. Validated on a field dataset of 16.4 million data records from 99 in-service EVs, our method achieves an average accuracy of 0.96 and an F1 score of 0.84 for four fault types using only 500-second snapshots (spanning <100 mV). This work proves that high-fidelity, interpretable electrochemical diagnosis is achievable on legacy fleets without new sensors, providing a scalable solution for the battery safety crisis.

</details>


### [274] [Robust Safety Filtering for Input-Constrained Underactuated Linear Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10872)
*Muhamad Rausyan Fikri*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对输入受限、欠驱动线性系统在未知扰动下的鲁棒安全过滤框架。该框架结合零和微分博弈得出的基线H∞输入与扰动观测器，使用扰动估计及其瞬态误差界来定义鲁棒高阶控制屏障函数约束，确保前向不变性。对于标量输入系统，通过精确输入区间判断逐点可行性，并以区间宽度作为可行性裕度。仿真在双轮平衡机器人上展示了位置与车身俯仰约束对同一受限轮毂扭矩输入的竞争关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欠驱动线性系统在面临未知扰动时，既要保证安全性（状态约束），又要满足输入约束，同时还需具备鲁棒性能（H∞）。现有方法难以同时处理扰动观测、安全过滤和输入受限三者的耦合问题，需要统一的鲁棒安全框架。

Method: 1) 从零和微分博弈推导基线H∞控制输入；2) 使用扰动观测器提供扰动估计及瞬态误差界；3) 基于扰动估计调整基线输入，并利用估计值及误差界构建鲁棒高阶控制屏障函数（HOCBF）约束；4) 通过前向不变性保证安全性（前提是可行输入集非空）；5) 对标量输入系统推导精确输入区间以判断逐点可行性，区间宽度作为可行性裕度；6) 引入有限时域H∞性能平衡项来量化实际输入与基线H∞策略的累积偏差。

Result: 在线性化双轮平衡机器人上的仿真表明，该框架能有效处理未知扰动下的安全过滤问题，并揭示了位置约束与车身俯仰约束在共享受限轮毂扭矩输入时的竞争关系，验证了可行性裕度分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架为输入受限欠驱动系统在未知扰动下提供了兼具鲁棒安全性（通过HOCBF实现前向不变性）和鲁棒性能（H∞平衡）的解决方案，并给出了清晰的可行性判定准则，为后续扩展到多输入系统或非线性系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a robust safety-filtering framework for input-constrained underactuated linear systems subject to unknown disturbances. A baseline H-$\infty$ input is derived from a zero-sum differential game, while a disturbance observer supplies an estimate and a transient error bound. The baseline input is adjusted using the disturbance estimate, while the estimate and its error bound are used to define robust high-order control barrier function constraints; forward invariance holds as long as the admissible-input set remains nonempty. For scalar-input systems, pointwise feasibility is determined from an exact input interval, and the interval width defines the feasibility margin. A finite-horizon H-$\infty$ performance balance accounts for the accumulated deviation of the applied input from the baseline H-$\infty$ policy. Simulations on a linearized two-wheeled balancing robot show how position and body-pitch constraints compete for the same bounded wheel-torque input.

</details>


### [275] [Multi-Rate Distributed Unscented Kalman Filtering Under Collective Detectability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10921)
*Mohammad Ali Abooshahab,Morten Hovd*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于无融合中心的非线性网络的多速率分布式无迹卡尔曼滤波（UKF）。各局部连续-离散UKF在公共基网格上运行，在各自采样时刻更新，并在单跳邻域内交换估计。在窗口集体可检测条件下，信息矩阵的扩散使得商协方差一致有界，即使单个节点不可观测。当集体不可见子空间平凡且统计线性化差异满足显式相干界时，信息加权融合均值产生指数有界的均方误差，无需收缩-混合条件（而算术扩散均值需要该条件）。还开发了分布式H∞变体。在随机非线性基准和每节点缺失至少一个模态的三惯性网络上验证，信息扩散比五轮协方差平均共识更精确且通信量为其五分之一，在3:2多速率采样下保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式滤波方法大多假设存在融合中心或依赖强可观测性条件，缺乏适用于非线性网络且无需融合中心、能处理多速率采样场景的分布式UKF方法。

Method: 提出多速率分布式UKF框架：局部连续-离散UKF在公共基网格上运行并各自采样更新；通过信息矩阵扩散（information diffusion）实现邻域估计交换；在集体可检测性下分析协方差有界性；引入统计线性化相干界保证指数均方误差有界；并扩展到分布式H∞变体，通过有限前缀可行性检查和单跳可检测Gramian建立正则化信息余量。

Result: 在随机非线性基准和每个节点至少缺失一个模态的三惯性网络上，信息扩散方法在精度上优于五轮协方差平均共识，且通信量仅为其五分之一；在3:2多速率采样下信息扩散保持稳定（而协方差平均共识和扩散均值均发散），商协方差保持有界。

Conclusion: 信息扩散（information diffusion）相比算术扩散均值和协方差平均共识具有更强的稳定性和更低的通信代价，为无融合中心的多速率非线性网络分布式估计提供了理论保证与实用方法。

Abstract: We develop a multi-rate distributed unscented Kalman filter for nonlinear networks without a fusion center. Local continuous-discrete UKFs run on a common base grid, update at their own sampling instants, and exchange estimates within one-hop neighborhoods. Under a windowed collective-detectability condition, diffusion of information matrices gives uniformly bounded quotient covariances although no individual node need be observable. When the collectively invisible subspace is trivial and the statistical-linearization discrepancies satisfy an explicit coherence bound, the information weighted fused mean yields exponentially bounded mean-square errors without a contraction-mixing condition. This contrasts with the arithmetic diffusiojn mean, which requires one. A distributed $H_{\infty}$ variant uses a fixed attenuation penalty. A finite-prefix feasibility check and the one-hop detectability Gramian yield a uniform regularized-information margin - an explicit local-contraction-mixing condition then yields mean-square error boundedness. The developed methods are tested on a stochastic nonlinear benchmark and a three-inertia network in which every node misses at least one mode. Information diffusion is more accurate than five-round covariance-averaging consensus at one fifth of its communication. It remains stable under 3:2 multi-rate sampling, where the covariance-averaging consensus and the diffusion mean both diverge, and it keeps the quotient covariances bounded.

</details>


### [276] [Techno-Economic Boundary Analysis of Small Modular Reactor Cogeneration for Hyperscale Data Center IT and Cooling Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10999)
*Honglin Li,Buxin She,Jie Zhang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了超大规模数据中心与小模块化反应堆（SMR）共址部署的经济可行性。通过每小时级协同优化反应堆调度、蒸汽抽取、吸收式冷却和电网交换，在ERCOT区域对200 MWe数据中心进行了109次情景分析。结果显示在2023年中等反应堆成本下，核能配置比电网供电贵49-62%；但核电厂成本降至约$5,000/kWe时可行区域开启，碳价$53-64/tCO₂可弥补差距，吸收式冷却可提供38%的年冷却量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心对高利用率的电力需求增长超过了电网供应能力，引发了对将数据中心与小模块化反应堆共址的兴趣。此类电厂可通过两种方式创收：出售低碳电力和将蒸汽转向吸收式制冷机。然而，这两种收入流从未在必须同时出现的条件下被定价，缺乏系统的经济可行性分析。

Method: 作者构建了一个每小时级协同优化模型，同时优化反应堆调度、蒸汽抽取、吸收式冷却和电网交换。研究对象为ERCOT区域200 MWe数据中心，进行了109次运行，涵盖资本成本、市场条件、政策（含45Y生产税收抵免）、融资和冷却效率等多个维度。

Result: 关键发现：（1）2023年中等反应堆资本成本下，核能配置比纯电网供电贵49-62%，即使有45Y税收抵免；（2）可行区域在约$5,000/kWe成本处开启；nth-of-a-kind资本可使成本降低77-89%（2023年），但在2024年低价市场中仍比电网贵0-34%；（3）碳价$53-64/tCO₂可弥合中等成本下的差距；（4）吸收式冷却根据每小时电价调度，供应38%年冷却量，额外成本$9.2M/年；该差距在安装成本$60/kWc（基准效率）或$570/kWc（传统效率园区）时弥合，而商业价格为$450-1,200/kWc。

Conclusion: 研究划定了共址反应堆热电联产相对于电网采购具备竞争力的资本、市场和政策条件，并量化了每个条件变化对结果的影响范围。结论表明在中低反应堆资本成本和适当碳价或税收政策下，该方案有望实现经济可行性，但吸收式冷却在当前商业成本下尚不经济。

Abstract: Hyperscale data centers are adding firm, high-utilization demand faster than grids can serve it, renewing interest in colocating them with small modular reactors. Such a plant could earn revenue in two ways, selling low-carbon power and diverting steam to absorption chillers that serve a cooling load accounting for 20-40% of facility electricity use, but neither revenue stream has been priced across the conditions that must coincide. Here we co-optimize reactor dispatch, steam extraction, absorption cooling and grid exchange hourly for a 200 MW$_\mathrm{e}$ data center in the Electric Reliability Council of Texas (ERCOT) region, across 109 runs spanning capital, market, policy, financing and cooling efficiency. At 2023 mid-range reactor capital, the nuclear configurations cost 49-62% more than grid supply even with the Section 45Y production tax credit. The viable region opens near \$5,000 kW$_\mathrm{e}^{-1}$, and nth-of-a-kind capital makes them 77-89% cheaper in 2023, though between parity and 34% more expensive in the low-price 2024 market. A carbon price of \$53-64 tCO$_2^{-1}$ closes the mid-range gap under hourly export crediting. Absorption cooling is dispatched in response to hourly electricity prices and supplies 38% of annual cooling, at an added cost of \$9.2 million yr$^{-1}$ relative to the reactor-only plant; that gap closes at an installed absorption cost of \$60 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$ at baseline efficiency and \$570 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$ on a legacy-efficiency campus, against surveyed commercial prices of \$450-1,200 kW$_\mathrm{c}^{-1}$. Together these results delineate the capital, market and policy conditions under which colocated reactor cogeneration is competitive with grid procurement, and the range over which each condition moves the outcome.

</details>
